Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Monte Carlo模拟的Siemens Biograph Vision Quadra扫描仪数据，使用NEMA IEC和XCAT体模；评价指标：分类准确率（accuracy）。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 4-feature模型跨体模泛化更好（XGBoost准确率损失仅0.04 vs 6-feature的0.13）；最佳准确率NEMA IEC上0.74、XCAT上0.69，优于传统几何切割方法；但4-feature对体模内散射符合抑制有限。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供直接借鉴。该论文属于医学成像领域，任务定义、数据模态、评价体系均与遥感目标检测不同。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。若强行关联，可思考：（1）事件级分类思路是否可类比遥感图像中的像素级/目标级分类；（2）跨域泛化鲁棒性分析（cross-phantom inference）的方法论可迁移到遥感跨场景泛化评估。但整体相关性极低。

Abstract: The introduction of LAFOV PET scanners brings significant sensitivity gains but also a substantial increase in the background rate from accidental coincidences, phantom-scattered and detector-scattered photons. While machine learning methods have been applied to background reduction in PET imaging, they target specific background components in post-processing rather than event-by-event classification on the raw data. In this work, we formulate coincidence classification as a supervised multi-class problem and evaluate XGBoost, AdaBoost and Neural Network classifiers as pre-reconstruction filters, using Monte Carlo simulations of the Siemens Biograph Vision Quadra scanner with NEMA IEC and anthropomorphic XCAT phantoms. We investigate two feature sets: a 4-feature representation based on the Attenuation Factor, photon time difference, energy sum, and energy difference, and an extended 6-feature set that incorporates topology-based variables. A systematic robustness study via cross-phantom inference reveals that the 4-feature models generalise significantly better across different phantom geometries, with XGBoost suffering an accuracy loss of only 0.04 compared to 0.13 for the 6-feature variant. Our best models achieve accuracies of up to 0.74 and 0.69 for the NEMA IEC and XCAT phantoms, respectively, outperforming traditional geometry-based cuts. However, we show that this compact feature set not only provides limited suppression of in-phantom scattered coincidences, but it also can lead to non-trivial spatial patterns. With scattered coincidences being the dominant background component in clinical conditions, this suggests that while the method serves as an effective and geometry-agnostic replacement for traditional cut-based selection, meaningful further gains in image quality will require either larger input representations or dedicated treatment of the phantom-scattered component.

</details>


### [63] [Image Quality Dependent Degradation for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25736)
*Yannick Kees,Elena Hoemann,Frank Köster,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于归一化流估计图像质量并自适应降低置信度阈值的方法，使自动驾驶目标检测系统在低质量输入下仍能避免关键漏检错误。

Research relevance: 1分。论文与遥感卫星、在轨部署、模型轻量化完全无关。场景为自动驾驶（automated driving），传感器为普通光学图像，不涉及遥感卫星、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感数据。核心关注的是输入图像质量下降时的鲁棒检测策略，而非模型压缩或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：目标检测（object detection）；场景：自动驾驶（automated driving）；传感器类型：普通光学相机；目标类别：行人（pedestrians）。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体检测模型结构，仅提及"state-of-the-art object detection"。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注的是检测鲁棒性而非部署效率。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要仅提及"will conduct experiments"，未给出任何实验结果或对比数据。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向完全无关；2. 论文尚处于计划/提案阶段（"we aim to", "we will"），无实际实验结果；3. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署内容；4. 方法依赖训练数据分布估计图像质量，在遥感星上场景的适用性未知。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能相关的点是：在轨遥感图像常受噪声、光照变化等影响，该论文提出的"根据图像质量自适应调整检测阈值"思路在星上场景中或许有参考价值，但论文本身未涉及轻量化，且自动驾驶场景与遥感差异较大。

Abstract: Perception is one of the primary applications where neural networks outperform conventional algorithms. One example is AI systems for automated driving, which can detect pedestrians based on image data and avoid them accordingly. A substantial challenge with these AI systems is that their output depends heavily on the quality of the input images. For example, if an image is of inferior quality due to heavy contamination, such as noise or darkness, accurate predictions are hardly feasible. Additionally, various types of errors can occur, each with varying relevance to the trustworthiness of the underlying AI system. In particular, it may be more critical not to detect an existing person than to detect a person where there is none. Therefore, we want to show that we can still avoid the most critical errors in situations of inferior image quality. To achieve this, we aim to establish a fail-degraded system by lowering the network's confidence threshold based on the estimated image quality, enabling it to detect objects more cautiously in uncertain situations. Additionally, we present a novel method for estimating the quality of incoming images by comparing them to the training data using normalizing flows. We will also conduct experiments applying our method to state-of-the-art object detection. In summary, we will present a design strategy for AI-based systems in automated driving that can deal with poor-quality input data without resorting to fallback solutions. Such measures enhance trust in AI-based systems and lead to an increased provision of the AI component.

</details>


### [64] [A Unified Benchmark and Modality-Adaptive Network for Day-and-Night Drone-View Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25778)
*Songtianhao Xu,Zhongwei Chen,Zhao-Xu Yang,Weifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建了跨光照条件的无人机-卫星跨模态地理定位基准IRCHN，并提出MASTR-Net网络实现可见光/红外无人机图像与卫星图像之间的匹配定位。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。涉及卫星图像（satellite image）和无人机图像跨模态匹配，属于遥感图像检索/定位任务，但**不是目标检测任务**，也**不涉及任何轻量化方法**（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"方向的相关性仅限于使用了卫星图像这一遥感数据模态。

Reading priority: 2分。与遥感卫星相关（使用了卫星图像），但与目标检测和轻量化两个核心方向均不直接相关。如果课题涉及多模态遥感匹配或跨模态学习可参考，否则优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：drone-view geo-localization（无人机视角地理定位），即跨视角/跨模态图像检索与匹配。场景：farmland, coastline, forest, urban areas。传感器类型：visible drone image（可见光无人机图像）、infrared drone image（红外无人机图像）、satellite image（卫星图像）。目标类别：无目标检测，属于场景级匹配。

Model architecture: MASTR-Net (Modality-Adaptive State-Space Transport Relation Network)，包含三个核心模块：(1) modality-adaptive feature enhancement（模态自适应特征增强）；(2) bidirectional selective state-space relation modeling（双向选择性状态空间关系建模）；(3) soft optimal transport relation alignment（软最优传输关系对齐）。整体用于缩小模态差异和视角引起的结构差异。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。从方法描述看，MASTR-Net包含状态空间模型和最优传输等复杂模块，计算开销可能较大，不具备明显的星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 自建基准IRCHN：26,460张图像，来自8,820个地理位置，4类场景（farmland, coastline, forest, urban），每个位置包含1张可见光无人机图、1张红外无人机图、1张卫星图。此外在IR-VL328和CVGL-RGBT两个红外基准上进行了评估。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称（如Recall@K等），但推测为地理定位常用的top-k匹配准确率。

Experiments: MASTR-Net在IRCHN上优于现有SOTA方法，在IR-VL328和CVGL-RGBT上达到competitive performance。摘要未提供具体数值对比、消融实验或参数量/速度对比。

Limitations: (1) 与目标检测任务无关，无法直接用于遥感目标检测；(2) 未涉及任何模型轻量化或加速方法；(3) 未报告模型参数量、计算量、推理速度等部署关键指标；(4) 状态空间模型+最优传输的复杂度可能较高，实时性存疑；(5) 仅覆盖4类场景，泛化性有待验证。

Ideas for my research: (1) 论文中使用的卫星图像与无人机图像跨模态匹配思路，可启发遥感目标检测中的多模态数据融合（如可见光+SAR+红外目标检测）；(2) 双向选择性状态空间模型（Mamba类架构）是一种较新的序列建模方式，可关注其在轻量目标检测骨干网络中的潜力；(3) 软最优传输对齐方法可用于遥感检测中的域适应或跨域知识蒸馏；(4) 但整体而言，本文对"在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Most existing drone-view geo-localization (DVGL) benchmarks contain drone imagery captured under a single illumination condition and lack geographically aligned visible drone images, infrared drone images, and satellite images from the same locations. To evaluate the generalization capability of DVGL methods under challenging illumination conditions, some methods train models on a visible benchmark and test them on an independent infrared benchmark. This protocol essentially constitutes transfer between datasets, which makes it difficult to systematically evaluate DVGL across daytime and nighttime conditions within a unified benchmark. To address this limitation, we construct IRCHN,a real-world DVGL benchmark designed for localization across different illumination conditions. IRCHN contains 26,460 images collected from 8,820 geographic locations across four representative scene categories, including farmland, coastline, forest, and urban areas. Each location provides one visible drone image, one infrared drone image, and one corresponding satellite image, which enables unified evaluation of DVGL methods across different illumination conditions and sensing modalities. We further propose the Modality-Adaptive State-Space Transport Relation Network (MASTR-Net), a DVGL framework tailored to localization under varying illumination conditions. MASTR-Net integrates modality-adaptive feature enhancement, bidirectional selective state-space relation modeling, and soft optimal transport relation alignment to jointly reduce modality gaps and view-induced structural discrepancies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MASTR-Net outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods on IRCHN for localization under varying illumination conditions and achieves competitive performance on two infrared benchmarks, IR-VL328 and CVGL-RGBT. Code: https://github.com/SongtianhaoXu/MASTR-Net

</details>


### [65] [GeoMFD: Continual Drone-View Geo-Localization with Geometry-Aware Adapter and Margin-Field Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25788)
*Zhongwei Chen,Hai-jun Rong,Tao Zhang,Xianfeng Nie,Xiangbao Zhang,Guoqi Li,Zhao-Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoMFD，一种面向无人机视角地理定位的几何感知持续适应方法，解决多环境部署下的模型遗忘和存储开销问题。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及无人机视角（drone-view）的视觉任务，与遥感图像有一定关联，但核心问题是持续学习/域适应下的地理定位（geo-localization），而非目标检测；且完全不涉及模型轻量化、在轨部署或卫星遥感。属于泛视觉+持续学习方法，对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题借鉴意义有限。

Reading priority: 2分——与课题方向弱相关。如果课题关注"在轨模型持续学习/域适应"可读，否则优先级低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无人机视角地理定位（drone-view geo-localization, DVGL），属于跨视角图像检索/匹配任务。应用场景：无人机在不同环境下的持续部署。传感器类型：可见光图像（drone-view + satellite-view）。目标类别：场景/地标级定位，非目标检测。

Model architecture: GeoMFD包含三个组件：Cold-start Bootstrapping Strategy (CBS) 初始化稳定嵌入空间；Geometry-aware Adapter (Geo-Adapter) 通过受控残差修正实现环境适应；Margin-Field Distillation (MFD) 通过正样本对与难负样本之间的相似性边际蒸馏来缓解跨视图几何遗忘。整体为持续学习框架，非轻量化网络设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。MFD中的蒸馏是用于缓解遗忘（anti-forgetting），而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文关注的是持续适应能力而非计算效率或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及在多种环境下进行实验，与environment-specific DVGL方法对比。

Experiments: 实验表明GeoMFD有效缓解了遗忘（forgetting），使用单个持续更新的模型达到了与多环境专用DVGL方法相当的竞争性能。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何轻量化技术；3. 不涉及目标检测任务；4. 未提供计算开销、推理速度等部署关键指标；5. 持续学习设定虽有趣，但未考虑星上资源受限场景。

Ideas for my research: 1. 持续学习/域适应思想可借鉴：遥感卫星在轨运行中会面临不同地区、不同光照、不同季节的数据分布变化，GeoMFD中的持续适应框架（CBS + adapter + 蒸馏）可迁移到在轨目标检测模型的持续更新场景；2. Margin-Field Distillation中的边际保持思想可用于在轨模型更新时防止旧知识灾难性遗忘；3. 但需注意：本文方法未考虑计算资源约束，直接迁移到星上需额外进行轻量化改造。

Abstract: Existing drone-view geo-localization (DVGL) methods are mainly developed under a static training paradigm, where models are optimized for fixed environments with all training data available in advance. However, this paradigm is difficult to extend to real-world deployment, where drones may encounter diverse environments and require multiple environment-specific models, resulting in additional storage and model-selection costs. Directly adapting a single model to new environments also risks distorting previously learned cross-view embedding geometry and causing forgetting. To address these challenges, we formalize the continual drone-view geo-localization (C-DVGL) setting and propose GeoMFD, a geometry-aware continual adaptation method for DVGL. GeoMFD combines a cold-start bootstrapping strategy (CBS), a geometry-aware adapter (Geo-Adapter), and margin-field distillation (MFD) to balance adaptation and cross-view geometry preservation. CBS initializes a stable embedding space, Geo-Adapter enables environment adaptation through controlled residual corrections, and MFD preserves similarity margins between positive pairs and hard negatives to alleviate cross-view geometry forgetting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GeoMFD effectively mitigates forgetting and achieves competitive performance with environment-specific DVGL methods using a single continuously updated model.

</details>


### [66] [Towards Faithful Sentimental Image Captioning via Evidence-Aware Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25789)
*Tiecheng Cai,Zexian Yang,Chao Chen,Shanshan Lin,Xiangwen Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SEA-Cap，一种基于情感证据感知的多智能体系统，用于忠实且证据支持的情感图像描述生成，通过情感证据挖掘和协作式幻觉检查机制提升情感准确性与事实一致性。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是情感图像描述生成（Sentimental Image Captioning），属于多模态/视觉语言任务，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：情感图像描述生成（Sentimental Image Captioning）；应用场景：通用图像的情感化描述；传感器类型：通用RGB图像；目标类别：无特定目标检测类别，关注的是图像中的情感线索（affective cues）。

Model architecture: SEA-Cap多智能体系统，包含三个核心模块：Sentiment Evidence Miner（情感证据挖掘器）、Generator（生成器）、Hallucination Checker（幻觉检查器）、Arbitrator（仲裁器），通过共享黑板（shared blackboard）进行迭代式协作优化。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。多智能体协作系统通常计算开销较大，与星上/边缘部署的轻量化需求相悖。

Datasets and metrics: 两个基准数据集（摘要未说明具体名称）；评价指标涉及情感准确性和事实一致性（具体指标名称摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 在两个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，有效缓解幻觉问题。具体对比方法和消融实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向完全无关；2. 不涉及任何轻量化或模型压缩技术；3. 多智能体协作系统计算复杂度高，不适合资源受限的星上部署场景；4. 未提供参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1）多智能体协作框架中的"幻觉检查器"（Hallucination Checker）概念，可类比用于遥感目标检测中的误检/虚警抑制模块；2）"情感证据挖掘"中从全局到局部证据的细粒度控制思路，可类比遥感小目标检测中从全局特征到局部细粒度特征的注意力机制设计。但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴意义很小。

Abstract: Sentimental Image Captioning (SIC) requires balancing emotional expression with visual fidelity. Existing methods often struggle with this trade-off, leading to hallucinations due to insufficient local grounding and the lack of sentimental verification mechanisms. To address these limitations, we propose SEA-Cap, a Sentiment-Evidence-Aware Multi-Agent System for faithful and evidence-grounded sentimental image captioning. SEA-Cap incorporates a Sentiment Evidence Miner that extracts structured, local affective cues to shift sentiment control from global attributes to verifiable object-level evidence. Leveraging this evidence, our framework orchestrates a collaborative workflow where a Generator, Hallucination Checker, and Arbitrator iteratively refine captions via a shared blackboard. By explicitly auditing generated content against mined visual evidence, SEA-Cap ensures both sentiment accuracy and factual consistency. Extensive experiments on two benchmark datasets demonstrate that SEA-Cap effectively mitigates hallucinations and achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [67] [FLASH: Efficient Impact Fall Detection with Unified Hypergraph State-Space Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25791)
*Tresor Y. Koffi,Youssef Mourchid,Yohan Dupuis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FLASH框架，融合超图表示与Mamba状态空间模型，用于人体跌倒冲击时刻检测，实现实时推理与跨数据集泛化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是人体跌倒检测（fall detection），属于人体行为识别/健康监测领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型在遥感中的应用。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：跌倒冲击时刻检测（fall impact detection）；场景：人体健康监测；传感器：骨架数据（skeleton-based）；目标类别：跌倒事件。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: FLASH框架：单矩阵超图表示（single-matrix hypergraph）+ Mamba选择性状态空间模型（selective state-space models）+ 自适应反馈机制（adaptive feedback mechanisms）。超图建模多关节协调，Mamba捕捉时序动态。

Lightweight method: 利用Mamba的线性时间复杂度（linear-time complexity）替代Transformer的二次复杂度，降低计算成本，实现实时推理。但未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提及"real-time inference capability"和"significantly reducing computational cost"，但未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：UP-Fall、UMAFall（人体跌倒检测数据集，非遥感数据集）；评价指标：准确率（accuracy）、零样本跨数据集泛化能力（zero-shot cross-dataset generalization）。

Experiments: 在UP-Fall和UMAFall上达到SOTA准确率，具备实时推理能力，计算成本低于双表示方法和Transformer方法，具有零样本跨数据集泛化能力。

Limitations: 1. 完全面向人体跌倒检测，与遥感/卫星/目标检测无关；2. 未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟等量化指标；3. 仅在两个跌倒检测数据集上验证，未在遥感数据集上测试；4. 轻量化仅体现在Mamba的线性复杂度上，未涉及剪枝/量化等经典压缩手段。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点有限，但Mamba（状态空间模型）作为Transformer的轻量替代方案，在遥感时序建模（如视频卫星目标跟踪、时序变化检测）中可能有潜在应用价值。超图建模多关节协调的思路，类比到遥感中可建模多尺度特征或多传感器协同关系。但整体相关性弱，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Falls represent a critical public health challenge, and accurate detection of the impact moment when an individual hits the ground is crucial for timely intervention. Existing skeleton-based methods rely on graph neural networks modeling only pairwise joint connections, failing to capture multi-joint coordination characteristic of fall impacts, while transformer-based temporal models suffer from quadratic complexity limiting real-time deployment. We propose FLASH, a novel framework integrating single-matrix hypergraph representations with Mamba's selective state-space models through adaptive feedback mechanisms for efficient impact detection. Our approach constructs biomechanically-grounded hyperedges to model functional joint coordination while leveraging Mamba's linear-time complexity to capture temporal dynamics. Experiments on UP-Fall and UMAFall datasets demonstrate that FLASH achieves state-of-the-art accuracy with real-time inference capability and strong zero-shot cross-dataset generalization, while significantly reducing computational cost compared to dual-representation and transformer-based methods. The model provides interpretable feedback through learned attention patterns aligned with biomechanical principles. Code is available at https://github.com/Tresor-Koffi/FLASH-Impact-Fall-Detection.

</details>


### [68] [Fine-Grained Food Image Understanding via Target-Aware Data Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25794)
*Jui-Feng Chi,Wei-Lun Chu,Bruce Coburn,Jinge Ma,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向细粒度食物视觉语义理解的数据中心多模态对齐方法，通过目标感知数据选择、VLM字幕精炼和多专家决策融合提升CLIP模型在食物检索上的性能。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦细粒度食物图像理解（fine-grained food visual-semantic understanding），属于多模态检索和视觉语言模型领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：细粒度食物描述与识别（fine-grained food description and recognition）、跨模态检索。场景：食物图像理解。传感器类型：普通RGB图像。目标类别：食材、烹饪方式、熟度、颜色、质地、摆盘等食物细粒度属性。

Model architecture: CLIP-style vision-language models（CLIP风格视觉语言模型）；多专家检索模型（multiple retrieval experts）；VLM辅助的层次化多专家决策融合（hierarchical VLM-assisted multi-expert decision-level fusion）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。论文提到"substantially more efficient"仅指相比纯VLM检索的计算效率更高，而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文未涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：检索性能（retrieval performance），具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: VLM字幕精炼单独带来约19%的平均性能提升；完整方法相比纯VLM检索取得两倍以上的检索分数（more than twice the retrieval score）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 摘要未说明具体数据集、指标、模型参数量等关键信息。3. 方法依赖VLM辅助，推理时可能引入额外开销。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1. 数据中心的"目标感知数据选择"（target-aware data selection）思想可用于遥感场景下的域适应或数据清洗；2. 多专家决策融合策略（multi-expert fusion）在遥感多模态检测中可能有启发；3. VLM辅助的精炼策略可类比遥感图像-文本对齐任务。但整体与在轨轻量化目标检测的核心需求（实时推理、小模型、低功耗）差距较大。

Abstract: Fine-grained food visual--semantic understanding requires models to capture subtle distinctions across ingredients, cooking methods, doneness, color, texture, and plate composition. Although CLIP-style vision-language models provide a natural framework for this task, their effectiveness is limited when training relies on heterogeneous web-collected image--text pairs. Such data often exhibit a web-to-target domain gap and cross-modal misalignment, where images differ from the target distribution and captions are noisy, multilingual, or weakly grounded in visual content. We propose a data-centric multimodal alignment method for fine-grained food description and recognition. Our method first performs target-aware data selection to identify visually relevant training subsets, then applies VLM-based caption refinement to generate visually grounded, target-style descriptions. Using these curated image--caption pairs, we train complementary CLIP-style retrieval experts and further combine their decisions through a hierarchical VLM-assisted multi-expert decision-level fusion strategy that invokes the VLM only when experts disagree. Experiments show that our data refinement strategy significantly improves retrieval performance over naive web supervision, with VLM-based caption refinement alone yielding an average performance gain of approximately 19%. Our full method also achieves more than twice the retrieval score of pure VLM-based retrieval while remaining substantially more efficient.

</details>


### [69] [Explicit Layer Modeling for Video Object Insertion and Layer Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25802)
*Kyujin Han,Seungjoo Shin,Sunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出大规模三元组视频数据集TriLayer和双分支扩散框架DBL-Diffusion，用于显式建模分层视频表示，解决视频对象插入和视频层分解问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于通用视频编辑中的分层表示、对象插入和层分解，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱、目标检测或模型轻量化等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频对象插入（video object insertion）、视频层分解（video layer decomposition）；应用场景：通用视频编辑；传感器类型：RGB视频；目标类别：通用前景对象及视觉效果。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: DBL-Diffusion：双分支扩散框架（dual-branch diffusion framework），通过共享去噪和跨分支交互联合建模RGB合成图和RGBA前景层。包含DBL-Insert（分层对象插入）和DBL-Decompose（视频层分解）两个实例化任务。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。扩散模型通常计算开销大，不适用于星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: TriLayer：大规模三元组视频数据集，包含对齐的合成视频（composite）、背景视频（background）和前景视频（foreground），前景层包含对象外观及关联视觉效果。评价指标：插入保真度（insertion fidelity）和分解质量（decomposition quality），具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 实验表明显式层建模显著提升了插入保真度和分解质量。具体对比方法和消融实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。扩散模型推理速度慢、计算量大，不适合资源受限的星上场景。数据集TriLayer为通用视频数据，不包含遥感图像。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于视频编辑/生成方向，与遥感目标检测和轻量化无交集。不建议在本课题中参考。

Abstract: Most video editing systems still lack explicit layered video representations, limiting their ability to perform realistic compositing, object reuse, and consistent manipulation. This limitation is especially pronounced in video object insertion and video layer decomposition, where existing methods rely on implicit inference or per-scene optimization due to the absence of explicit foreground-layer supervision. We introduce TriLayer, a large-scale triplet video dataset containing aligned composite, background, and foreground videos, where the foreground layers include both object appearance and associated visual effects. This explicit supervision enables models to learn layered video representations directly rather than inferring them implicitly. Building on this dataset, we propose DBL-Diffusion, a dual-branch diffusion framework that jointly models RGB composites and RGBA foreground layers through shared denoising and cross-branch interaction. We instantiate the framework in two tasks: DBL-Insert for layered object insertion, which generates explicit RGBA layers for realistic compositing and flexible post-editing, and DBL-Decompose for video layer decomposition, which recovers foreground and background layers using triplet supervision. Experiments demonstrate that explicit layer modeling substantially improves both insertion fidelity and decomposition quality.

</details>


### [70] [Freq-RemoteVAR: Next-Frequency Autoregressive Modeling for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25815)
*Luqi Gong,Rui Xu,Yue Chen,Chao Li,Jingqi Hong,Xuefeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种频域自回归框架Freq-RemoteVAR，将遥感变化检测重构为频域中的结构化生成问题，通过逐频预测范式实现从粗到细的变化掩码生成。

Research relevance: 3分：论文涉及遥感（remote sensing change detection），但与目标检测无关（是变化检测任务），且完全不涉及轻量化/在轨部署相关内容。属于遥感场景下的检测类任务，但任务类型（变化检测 vs 目标检测）和关注点（频域建模 vs 轻量化部署）均与课题方向有较大偏差。

Reading priority: 2分：与课题方向弱相关。虽然涉及遥感场景，但任务是变化检测而非目标检测，且完全不涉及轻量化/在轨部署。频域建模思路有一定借鉴价值，但整体框架复杂、推理慢，不适合直接迁移到在轨轻量化目标检测课题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：遥感变化检测（change detection），非目标检测。场景：双时相遥感图像（bi-temporal images）。传感器类型：摘要未说明（光学遥感图像可能性大）。目标类别：变化区域（land-cover changes），非舰船/飞机/车辆等具体目标。

Model architecture: Freq-RemoteVAR框架，核心是Frequency VAR Transformer，包含：(1) 频域感知掩码分词化（frequency-aware mask tokenization），通过Fourier变换和量化将变化监督分解为多频token目标；(2) 因果自回归建模（causal autoregressive modeling over frequency tokens）；(3) Scale-Aligned RoPE Cross Attention (SRCA)模块，对齐频域掩码查询与空域双时相特征；(4) Change-quality Control模块，通过动态归一化、注意力偏置和空间偏移调整抑制伪变化。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。自回归生成范式（逐频token预测）在推理速度上可能较慢，不利于星上实时部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CDD, GZ-CD, LEVIR-CD（均为遥感变化检测公开数据集）。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称（通常变化检测使用IoU, F1, OA等，但摘要未列出）。

Experiments: 在CDD、GZ-CD、LEVIR-CD三个数据集上，Freq-RemoteVAR一致优于现有方法，尤其在复杂外观变化和噪声干扰场景下表现突出。摘要未提供具体数值对比。

Limitations: (1) 任务为变化检测而非目标检测，与课题方向任务类型不匹配；(2) 自回归生成范式推理速度慢，不适合在轨实时推理；(3) 无任何轻量化设计或部署相关指标；(4) 频域分解+自回归建模的计算开销可能很大；(5) 未说明模型参数量、FLOPs等效率指标。

Ideas for my research: (1) 频域分解思想可借鉴：将目标检测中的特征或预测目标分解到不同频率分量，可能有助于小目标检测（小目标对应高频信息）；(2) 从粗到细的渐进式生成策略可迁移到旋转框检测或密集检测中；(3) SRCA模块中频域-空域对齐的跨注意力机制，可能对多尺度遥感目标检测有启发；(4) 但整体框架复杂度过高，直接用于在轨轻量化场景的可行性低，需大幅简化。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection aims to identify land-cover changes from bi-temporal images. Most existing methods follow a one-shot dense prediction paradigm, directly regressing a change mask from fused features. However, such approaches overlook the intrinsic frequency characteristics of change patterns. We propose Freq-RemoteVAR, a frequency autoregressive framework that reformulates change detection as a structured generation problem in the frequency domain. Instead of predicting the change mask in a single step, we introduce a next-frequency prediction paradigm, where change information is progressively generated from coarse to fine. We design a frequency-aware mask tokenization strategy that decomposes change supervision into multi-frequency token targets via Fourier transformation and quantization. We develop a Frequency VAR Transformer, which performs causal autoregressive modeling over frequency tokens. The model starts from learned mask queries and progressively predicts frequency-level tokens conditioned on previously generated tokens and bi-temporal image features, effectively capturing long-range dependencies across frequency scales. We introduce Scale-Aligned RoPE Cross Attention (SRCA) module, which aligns frequency-domain mask queries with spatial-domain bi-temporal features under a unified coordinate system, enhancing spatial-frequency consistency during generation. We propose a Change-quality Control module that adaptively modulates the generation process through dynamic normalization, attention biasing, and spatial offset adjustment, thereby suppressing pseudo-change responses and improving robustness. Extensive experiments on CDD, GZ-CD, and LEVIR-CD demonstrate that Freq-RemoteVAR consistently outperforms existing methods, particularly in challenging scenarios with complex appearance variations and noisy disturbances.

</details>


### [71] [SepPrune:A Separator-based Pruning Framework for Efficient Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25818)
*Yuchen Wang,Qihui Zhu,Yang Liu,Xiaoyan Sun,Siying Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模态分隔符（separator token）的无训练视觉Token剪枝方法SepPrune，用于多模态大语言模型的高效推理，可去除80.2%的视觉Token并保留96.3%的精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的视觉Token剪枝，不涉及遥感图像、卫星、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然"剪枝"一词与轻量化相关，但这里的剪枝是视觉Token级别的剪枝（减少LLM输入序列长度），而非模型参数/通道/结构的剪枝，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化的核心需求（减少模型参数量、FLOPs、延迟以适应星上资源约束）完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对MLLM的Token剪枝有交叉兴趣，否则对课题帮助甚微。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的视觉Token剪枝，用于加速视觉-语言理解。应用场景：通用视觉-语言任务（如VQA、图像描述等）。传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器。目标类别：不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B等MLLM架构，利用LLM内置的投影参数，使用模态分隔符（separator token）作为统一查询来排序和选择信息性视觉Token。无需架构修改，即插即用。

Lightweight method: 视觉Token剪枝（Vision Token Pruning），属于推理加速方法，而非模型参数压缩。具体方法：利用模态分隔符token的注意力分数作为排序依据，选择信息量最高的视觉Token，丢弃其余Token。方法特点：无训练（training-free）、即插即用（plug-and-play）、无需跨模态注意力计算。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等星上部署关键指标。仅报告了Token剪枝比例（80.2%）和精度保留率（96.3%）。该方法针对的是LLM推理的prefill阶段加速，而非遥感在轨目标检测模型的轻量化部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提到在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上实验，保留96.3%的原始精度。

Experiments: 摘要未说明对比方法和消融实验细节。仅声称达到SOTA性能，去除80.2%视觉Token时保留96.3%精度。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，不涉及遥感图像特性（如小目标、旋转框、多尺度等）。2. 视觉Token剪枝减少的是LLM输入序列长度，而非模型参数量或计算量（FLOPs）的实质性降低，对星上部署的硬件资源约束帮助有限。3. 仅验证了7B规模模型，未讨论在更小模型上的效果。4. 未讨论对目标检测任务的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1）"利用注意力分数作为Token重要性排序依据"的思路，可类比到遥感检测模型中特征图/通道的重要性评估，用于通道剪枝或特征剪枝；2）"无训练、即插即用"的设计理念值得借鉴，可探索遥感目标检测模型的免训练轻量化方法。但整体而言，该工作与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化的核心问题（小目标检测精度保持、旋转框检测、星上资源约束下的实时推理等）差距较大。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs), such as Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3, generate large numbers of vision tokens for high-resolution inputs, leading to substantial computational cost. Existing vision token pruning methods either depend on cross-modal attention and cannot prune before the prefill stage, or rely on diversity estimation with high computational overhead. We observe that attention scores from both vision and text tokens peak at modality separator tokens, suggesting that these separators bridge the two modalities. Based on this observation, we propose SepPrune, an efficient, training-free, plug-and-play pruning method that uses the separator token as a unified query to rank and select informative vision tokens. SepPrune reuses the LLM's built-in projection parameters and requires no architectural changes. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-7B show that SepPrune achieves state-of-the-art performance, retaining 96.3% of the original accuracy while removing 80.2% of vision tokens.

</details>


### [72] [Food Image Segmentation with LLM-Derived Ingredient Labels and Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25820)
*Jui-Feng Chi,Wei-Ta Chu,Sheng-Long Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两个即插即用的多模态模块（LIM-F和LIM-Q），通过LLM推断的食材标签注入视觉分割模型，提升食品图像分割精度。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文任务为食品图像分割（food image segmentation），场景为营养追踪与健康监测，传感器为普通RGB图像，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或空间在轨智能。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：食品图像语义分割（food image semantic segmentation）；场景：营养追踪、个性化健康监测；传感器类型：普通RGB图像；目标类别：食材/食品类别（FoodSeg103数据集中的103类食材）。不涉及遥感目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等。

Model architecture: 两个即插即用模块：LIM-F（Language Injection Module for Features）与任意多层输出图像编码器（如Swin Transformer）配合；LIM-Q（Language Injection Module for Queries）针对Mask2Former风格的Transformer解码器。整体架构为Swin-L + Mask2Former，以及CNN-based架构。利用LLM从食品图像推断食材标签作为语义先验。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速技术。相反，论文通过引入额外模块（LIM-F/LIM-Q）提升精度，训练时GPU显存增加最多3.8 GB，属于精度导向而非轻量化导向的工作。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。仅提及训练时GPU显存增加最多3.8 GB（Swin-L+Mask2Former配置下），但未提供推理时的显存或速度数据。不涉及星上/边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：FoodSeg103（食品图像分割基准）；评价指标：mean Intersection over Union (mIoU)。

Experiments: Swin-L+Mask2Former + LIM-Q 达到 mIoU 55.0（SOTA）；Swin-L+Mask2Former + LIM-F 达到 mIoU 54.4；CNN-based架构 + LIM-F 从 47.7 提升至 49.8 mIoU。消融实验和对比方法细节摘要未充分说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 依赖LLM推断食材标签，引入额外推理开销和延迟，不适合在轨实时场景；3. 模块增加显存消耗，未考虑轻量化部署；4. 仅在食品分割数据集上验证，泛化到遥感场景未经证实。

Ideas for my research: 1. LIM-F/LIM-Q的"语言注入"思路可类比为遥感场景中利用类别语义先验（如舰船/飞机/车辆类别名）增强检测头特征表达，但需注意引入LLM在星上不可行，可考虑轻量级语义嵌入替代；2. 即插即用模块设计思路值得借鉴，但需设计为计算高效的轻量模块；3. 该论文整体与在轨遥感轻量化目标检测方向弱相关，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Food image segmentation plays a vital role in health-related applications such as nutrition tracking and personalized health monitoring. However, existing models often underperform on visually similar ingredients and rare food categories. To address this issue, we propose two plug-and-play multimodal modules that enhance the segmentation performance by leveraging ingredient labels inferred from food images using large language models (LLMs). The first module, called LIM-F (Language Injection Module for Features), is designed to pair with any image encoder that produces multi-layer outputs (e.g., Swin Transformer), while the second module, LIM-Q (Language Injection Module for Queries), targets Mask2Former-style Transformer-based decoders. Both modules enable training without the need for pre-aligning images with text by directly injecting semantic ingredient information into the visual analysis pipeline. On the FoodSeg103 benchmark, the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance. Specifically, integrating LIM-Q into the Mask2Former decoder with a Swin-L image encoder yields a mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 55.0. LIM-F also demonstrates strong generalization and competitive performance, reaching an mIoU of 54.4 under the same model (Swin-L+Mask2Former). Furthermore, its applicability extends beyond Transformer-based decoders, as evidenced by an improvement from 47.7 to 49.8 mIoU when integrated into a CNN-based architecture. Notably, the improved segmentation accuracy is achieved with only a moderate (at most 3.8 GB) increase in the GPU memory consumption during training. Thus, the proposed approach offers a practical and scalable solution for fine-grained food understanding.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Static Costs: Learning-Dynamics Aware Loss Functions for Long-Tailed Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25830)
*Varad Shinde,Nikhil Kumar Shrey,Magesh Rajasekaran,Md Saiful Islam Sajol,Harshil Bhargava,Subhajit Sidanta,Supratik Mukhopadhyay,Yimin Zhu*

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通用计算机视觉中的长尾分布问题，不涉及遥感/卫星场景，不涉及目标检测任务，不涉及轻量化/部署方法。虽然损失函数设计在理论上可迁移，但摘要中完全没有遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化的任何元素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：长尾分布下的图像分类（非目标检测）；应用场景：稀有物种识别、制造缺陷检测、医学图像理解；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：摘要未说明。完全不涉及遥感场景。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文仅提到计算开销可忽略，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多个长尾分布benchmark数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：准确率(accuracy)和泛化能力(generalizability)；不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在多个benchmark数据集上超越现有重加权损失函数(state-of-the-art reweighting loss functions)，实现了准确率与泛化能力的最优权衡。消融实验验证了三个组件（语义尺度、Shannon熵、跨epoch正则化）的有效性。

Limitations: 1. 仅在图像分类任务上验证，未涉及目标检测；2. 未在遥感数据集上实验；3. 未讨论与轻量化方法的兼容性；4. 未提供计算开销的具体量化指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限。如果课题涉及遥感长尾分布目标检测（如罕见舰船/飞机类别），可考虑将LDAL的动态学习感知思想引入检测损失函数设计，但需要大量适配工作。对轻量化方向无直接借鉴意义。

Abstract: Deep learning models in computer vision face significant challenges when trained on long-tailed datasets, where a few majority classes dominate while many minority classes are severely underrepresented. Such imbalances frequently arise in real-world scenarios such as rare species recognition, manufacturing fault detection, and medical image understanding, leading to biased models that underperform on tail classes. Existing reweighting methods typically rely on static class frequencies to penalize the model, ignoring the dynamic nature of how effectively a network actually learns a class over time. We address this by introducing a novel Learning-Dynamics Aware Loss (LDAL) function that shifts the focus from static sample counts to dynamic learning progress. LDAL framework adjusts class weights continuously by leveraging: (i) the strength of learned feature representations (semantic scale), (ii) the intrinsic learning difficulty of each class, measured via the Shannon entropy of its predictions, and (iii) an inter-epoch regularizer term that tracks prediction shifts between consecutive epochs to stabilize training and avoid local minima. LDAL is purely a objective function which incurs negligible computational overhead while adapting to the feature learning of the model. Experimental results on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly surpasses state-of-the-art reweighting loss functions, providing an optimal trade-off between accuracy and generalizability. The source code is available at https://github.com/sdm2026/ldal

</details>

Model architecture: 生成端：FLUX.1-dev + PuLID（身份保持人脸合成）；检测端：SigLIP+DINOv2（AI生成图像检测）、GenD-DINOv3（人脸篡改检测）；对抗攻击：多模型PGD（含DiffJPEG-in-loop、MI/DI/EoT、自适应加权、两阶段热启动）；净化检测：CLIP ViT-L/14 backbone + EFFORT净化器。

Experiments: 生成任务：90%逃逸率对抗组织者检测器，57.6%逃逸率对抗参与者检测器，最终生成分数0.4170；检测任务：基线深度伪造99.4%准确率，但真实图像假阳性率高，最终检测分数0.6986；净化检测：median-3净化下|Δlogit|在四种对抗源上AUROC 0.81-0.98，发现JPEG质量因子Q70处信号崩溃。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感相关方法、数据集或部署策略。检测端模型（SigLIP、DINOv2、DINOv3、CLIP ViT-L/14）均为大规模视觉Transformer，参数量大，不适合星上部署。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。若强行关联：对抗攻击中的PGD方法或可迁移至遥感目标检测的对抗鲁棒性研究；DiffJPEG-in-loop的JPEG压缩鲁棒性思路可能对遥感图像压缩后的检测性能有启发，但关联度极低。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of team "Go To Germany" in the ImageCLEF 2026 Deepfake Detection and Generation Task. For the image generation task, we employ FLUX.1-dev with PuLID for identity-preserving face synthesis, combined with a multi-model PGD adversarial attack targeting 12 detectors simultaneously (DiffJPEG-in-loop, MI/DI/EoT, adaptive weighting, two-stage warm-start). Our approach achieved 90% evasion against organizer detectors and 57.6% against participant detectors, with a final generation score of 0.4170. For the image detection task, we combine two complementary detectors - SigLIP+DINOv2 for AI-generated images and GenD-DINOv3 for face manipulations - in a max-probability ensemble, achieving 99.4% accuracy on baseline deepfakes but suffering from high false-positive rates on real images, resulting in a final detection score of 0.6986. Beyond the official submission, we conducted a self-initiated investigation of purification-based adversarial detection, comparing three families of detection signals across six detectors that share a CLIP ViT-L/14 backbone. We find that raw $|Δ\text{logit}|$ under median-3 purification, applied through the EFFORT detector, separates adversarial inputs from clean inputs with AUROC 0.81-0.98 across four adversarial source types - a finding that refutes the simple backbone-preservation hypothesis and exposes a sharp JPEG-quality cliff at Q70 where the signal collapses.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a method for training a text-conditioned segmentation model for CT scans, which combines voxel-level supervision with coarse but scalable slice-level supervision from reports. We extract, from a large database of scan-report pairs, descriptions of findings with indices of slices where those findings occur. We then finetune a general-purpose 2D image segmentation model, SAM3, with standard segmentation losses from strongly labeled data and with a slice-level classification loss from the extracted weak supervision. Our results on the ReXGroundingCT dataset illustrate that this strategy improves the segmentation dice score: from an 8% relative gain when there are 1000 fully labeled volumes to 22% when there are 250 fully labeled volumes.

</details>


### [76] [TIGA: Trajectory-Injected Generative Attack against Black-box AIGC Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25894)
*Xia Du,Zhuosen Bao,Zheng Lin,Jizhe Zhou,Jiawei Lian,Chi-man Pun,Jun Luo,Wei Ni,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需源图像、无需重新训练扩散模型的轨迹注入式生成攻击方法TIGA，在单次DDIM采样轨迹中生成能欺骗AIGC取证检测器的对抗图像。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于AIGC图像取证对抗攻击（人脸合成图像的检测规避），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及black-box攻击、梯度聚合、动量稳定等优化技巧，但这些方法面向的是生成式对抗攻击任务，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。

Model architecture: 基于预训练扩散模型（DDIM），在采样轨迹中注入对抗方向。核心模块包括：多白盒代理检测器梯度聚合、各向异性方向搜索（symmetric finite-difference queries）、衰减动量稳定、频域重塑抑制高频伪影。与遥感目标检测模型架构无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该方法需要在扩散模型采样过程中进行多步梯度计算和黑盒查询，计算开销大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：黑盒攻击成功率（black-box attack performance）、迁移性（transferability）、鲁棒性（robustness under post-processing）、感知质量（perceptual quality）。

Experiments: 在代理检测器和未见过的专用取证检测器上实验，TIGA实现了强黑盒攻击性能、高迁移性和鲁棒性，且无需源图像或重新训练扩散模型。摘要未给出具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 方法计算开销大（需多步梯度聚合和黑盒查询），不适合资源受限场景；3. 仅针对人脸AIGC取证检测，领域迁移性未知；4. 摘要未说明推理速度、模型大小等关键部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1. 黑盒场景下的梯度估计方法（symmetric finite-difference queries）可用于遥感模型在未知硬件平台上的适配；2. 频域重塑抑制高频伪影的思路，可能对遥感图像中的噪声抑制有启发；3. 多代理模型梯度聚合提升迁移性的策略，可用于遥感轻量化模型的知识蒸馏中多教师集成。整体而言，该论文与课题方向弱相关，不建议深入精读。

Abstract: Recent diffusion models have achieved remarkable realism in facial image synthesis, posing growing challenges to artificial intelligence-generated content (AIGC) forensic detectors.Existing evasion methods typically perturb pre-generated images or require detector-aware training, which may introduce visible or statistical artifacts and limit applicability when the diffusion model must remain frozen and the target detector is accessible only through black-box queries. We propose Trajectory-Injected Generative Attack (TIGA), a source-image-free and training free framework that generates detector-evasive images within a single diffusion sampling trajectory. TIGA steers the latent Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM) trajectory so that adversarial properties emerge during generation rather than being added afterward. TIGA first aggregates gradients from multiple white-box surrogate detectors to form a transferable, sign-aware prior, and then performs anisotropic directional search with symmetric finite-difference queries to estimate the black-box target response. The estimated directions are stabilized by decayed momentum and injected according to the DDIM noise schedule, with frequency-domain reshaping to suppress high frequency artifacts. Experiments on surrogate and unseen specialized forensic detectors show that TIGA achieves strong blackbox attack performance, transferability, and high robustness under common post-processing operations without source images or diffusion-model retraining, while preserving high perceptual quality.

</details>

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自定义游戏场景数据集，通过探索代理(exploration agent)收集视觉观测，自动标注管线提供帧级裁剪标签(frame-level clipping labels)。评价指标：准确率(accuracy)、精确率-召回率(precision-recall)、对提示变体的鲁棒性分析。

Experiments: Gemini-3.1-Flash在整体准确率和提示鲁棒性上最优；开源模型(VLM)的精确率-召回率随提示设计大幅波动；所有VLM在视觉模糊帧（近接触几何、部分遮挡）上产生大量假阳性；结论：当前VLM更适合作为多阶段QA管线中的高召回候选过滤器，而非独立bug检测器。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术贡献；2. 仅评估零样本VLM能力，未涉及模型训练、微调或压缩；3. 实验场景高度特化（游戏几何裁剪），泛化性有限；4. 未讨论任何计算效率或部署约束问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向完全不相关，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或轻量化方法。不建议投入时间深入阅读。

Abstract: In this work, we study the use of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for anomaly detection in an agent-driven game Quality Assurance (QA) pipeline focusing on geometry clipping. In this evaluation, a custom exploration agent navigates a game level to collect visual observations, while the automatic annotation pipeline provides frame-level clipping labels. This setup allows us to evaluate recent VLMs on a controlled anomaly detection task without manual annotation. We benchmark six recent VLMs (Gemini, GPT, Qwen, Gemma, Llama, and Ministral) under a zero-shot prompting setting and analyse their sensitivity to four prompt variants.
  Our results show that while the VLMs can capture visual cues associated with geometry clipping, they all produce substantial false positives on visually ambiguous frames such as near-contact geometry and partial occlusions. Gemini-3.1-Flash achieves the best overall accuracy and is the most robust to prompt variation, while open-source models exhibit large precision--recall swings depending on the prompt design. These findings suggest that current VLMs are best suited as high-recall candidate filters within multi-stage QA pipelines rather than as standalone bug detectors.

</details>

Abstract: Face de-identification (De-ID) aims to remove or conceal personally identifiable facial features in images or videos to prevent identity recognition while preserving utility for downstream tasks. With the rising emphasis on data privacy and responsible AI, face De-ID has emerged as an active research area spanning computer vision and privacy-preserving communities. Early approaches, and many contemporary ones, operate in the digital domain by modifying pixel-level or appearance-level features through post-capture processing. Recent advances extend face De-ID beyond post-processing by integrating privacy mechanisms directly into sensors during image acquisition, bridging sensing systems and downstream vision algorithms. In parallel, physical-domain methods explore wearable accessories and materials that conceal identity information in real-world environments prior to capture. In this survey, we present the first unified overview that spans the full data acquisition pipeline, encompassing the physical, sensor, and digital domains. Through this domain-centric lens, we systematically analyze current methodologies, technical progress, and the distinct challenges inherent to each stage. We then review and organize existing evaluation protocols, examining current practices and highlighting the critical need for standardized, comprehensive benchmarks. Finally, we identify key open problems and outline emerging research directions to guide future work in this rapidly evolving field. To support ongoing research, we maintain a project page that organizes relevant literature with collected datasets and open source code: https://github.com/CV-AC/Awesome-FaceDe-ID.

</details>


### [79] [MODUS: Decoder-Only Any-to-Any Modeling of Diverse Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25948)
*Mingqiao Ye,Zhaochong An,Zhitong Gao,Xian Liu,François Fleuret,Chuan Li,Amir Zadeh,Serge Belongie,Afshin Dehghan,Jesse Allardice,David Mizrahi,Oğuzhan Fatih Kar,Roman Bachmann,Amir Zamir*

TL;DR: 提出一种纯解码器架构的任意到任意多模态模型Modus，支持任意模态组合作为输入和输出，无需特定模态头或损失函数。

Model architecture: 纯解码器架构（decoder-only），所有模态对称处理，无需特定模态头（modality-specific heads）、损失函数或任务流水线。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标，仅提及"various benchmarks"。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；4. 解码器架构在资源受限平台上的推理效率未讨论。

Abstract: Any-to-any models predict any modality from any combination of others within a single network, a formulation used in multimodal vision and vision-language models, and increasingly in scientific domains such as ecology and astronomy. Existing any-to-any models are typically trained from scratch using encoder-decoder or diffusion architectures, impacting their performance and preventing them from using strong pre-trained decoder-only models as a prior. In this work, we investigate decoder-only any-to-any multimodal modeling, which treats all modalities symmetrically and supports arbitrary modalities as inputs and outputs without modality-specific heads, losses, or task pipelines. Because every modality is both an input and an output of the same model, the resulting model, named Modus, can support a range of applications, such as chained generation through intermediate modalities or cross-modal self-verification by scoring the model's own outputs with another generated modality. Modus demonstrates strong out-of-the-box performance and is competitive with specialist and multitask baselines using a single model across various benchmarks. All materials are open-sourced at https://modus-multimodal.epfl.ch/.

</details>


### [80] [Knowledge-Guided Multimodal Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Video-Level Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25961)
*Podakanti Satyajith Chary,Barath Parthiban,Pranesh Velmurugan,Adeeba Khan,Nagarajan Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRISM-AH框架，通过多模态冲突建模和轻量级流式模型识别视频中的矛盾/犹豫情绪，在525个视频上达到macro F1 0.6133。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向基本无关（1分）。该论文研究的是健康行为改变中的情绪识别（ambivalence/hesitancy），属于多模态情感计算/行为分析领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署于边缘/星上设备。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多模态矛盾/犹豫情绪识别（视频级）；场景：健康行为改变咨询；传感器：视频（面部、语音、语言、身体姿态等多模态）；目标类别：矛盾/犹豫情绪状态。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: PRISM-AH框架：冻结的视觉、音频、文本编码器 → 对齐到短时间窗口 → 轻量级流式模型（streaming model），包含跨模态不协调评分、下一窗口预测（暴露犹豫惊喜信号）、行为原型发现、参与者元数据条件化。最后使用知识引导的大语言模型基于专家线索分类法进行推理，仅在验证性能提升时进行晚期融合。

Lightweight method: 文中提到"lightweight streaming model"，但未说明具体参数量、FLOPs、剪枝/量化/蒸馏等轻量化技术细节。轻量级在此处指模型结构相对简单，并非面向边缘部署的系统性轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。该工作面向通用视频分析，不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：标注的公开测试集，525个视频；评价指标：macro F1（主指标），决策阈值经校准优化；辅助目标：密集窗口级标注监督。零样本基线macro F1为0.2827。

Experiments: PRISM-AH在525个视频的标注公开测试集上达到macro F1 0.6133，对比零样本基线0.2827有显著提升。推理增益从验证集迁移到更大的测试集。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 轻量级仅体现在模型设计理念上，缺乏量化指标；3. 数据集规模较小（525个视频），泛化性存疑；4. 依赖多模态输入（视频+音频+文本），与遥感单模态（光学/SAR图像）场景差异大。

Ideas for my research: 1. 跨模态/跨特征不协调评分（cross-modal dissonance）的思想，可类比遥感多模态（光学+SAR）融合中的特征冲突建模；2. 下一窗口预测作为"惊喜信号"的思路，可用于遥感时序变化检测中的异常发现；3. 知识引导的大语言模型推理+晚期融合策略，可借鉴用于遥感场景中先验知识（如地理先验、目标先验）与检测结果的融合。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are conflicting affective states that precede the delay or abandonment of health behaviour change. Recognition of A/H at the video level is difficult, since the signal arises from disagreement across and within facial, vocal, linguistic, and bodily modalities, and manifests differently across individuals. The proposed PRISM-AH (Predictive Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Multimodal Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition), is a framework that treats A/H as a multimodal conflict that unfolds over time. Frozen vision, audio, and text encoders are aligned into short time windows and passed to a lightweight streaming model that scores cross-modal dissonance, predicts each next window to expose a hesitation surprise signal, discovers behaviour prototypes, and is conditioned on participant metadata. Dense window-level annotations supervise the model as an auxiliary objective, and the decision threshold is calibrated for macro F1. A knowledge-guided large language model then reasons over structured evidence using the expert cue taxonomy of the dataset, and its verdict is fused late only when validation performance improves. On the labelled public test partition of 525 videos, PRISM-AH attains a macro F1 of 0.6133, compared to the reported zero-shot baseline of 0.2827. The reasoning gain is validated to transfer from validation to the larger test partition.

</details>


### [81] [LaP-Forensics: Latent-Pixel Consistency Guided Multimodal Reasoning for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25962)
*Can Wang,Yuhao Wang,Yushe Cao,Canran Xiao,Fei Shen*

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于AI生成图像（deepfake）检测与伪影定位，属于数字图像取证领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于数字图像取证领域，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>

Model architecture: 多模态框架LaP-Forensics：冻结的Stable Diffusion DDIM逆变换-重建模型提供固定重建参考，残差图衡量局部与参考的兼容性；独立projector编码RGB和残差图；Where-What-Why模型输出文本分析和伪影mask；额外image-level head融合RGB和DDIM残差类特征。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。使用Stable Diffusion作为基础模型，计算开销极大，不适合星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：UniversalFakeDetect（跨生成器检测）、SynthScars（伪影定位）；指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称（mask overlap等提及但未给出标准指标名）。

Experiments: 跨生成器检测实验（UniversalFakeDetect）、伪影定位实验（SynthScars）；消融实验包括：cue-construction、inversion-horizon、component、reward-term、counterfactual分析。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 使用Stable Diffusion，计算开销大，不满足轻量化需求；3. 自由文本忠实度和后处理鲁棒性仍是开放问题；4. 未提供任何参数量、速度等部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能参考的是：残差图/重建一致性作为辅助特征的思想，但遥感在轨场景中计算资源受限，无法部署Stable Diffusion这类大模型。不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Recent generative models can produce images with few obvious visual artifacts, weakening detectors and explanations that rely only on surface appearance. We present LaP-Forensics, a multimodal framework that augments RGB semantics with reconstruction-based forensic evidence. A frozen Stable Diffusion DDIM inversion-reconstruction model provides a fixed reconstruction reference, and its residual map measures local compatibility with that reference. Independent projectors encode the RGB image and residual map before a structured Where-What-Why model predicts a textual analysis and an artifact mask.Supervised fine-tuning is followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), whose reward combines mask overlap with output-structure and evidence-reference terms. These text-side terms encourage the model to refer to the consistency map but do not constitute a verifier of free-form textual truth. A separate image-level head fuses RGB and DDIM-residual class features. Experiments show cross-generator detection on UniversalFakeDetect and competitive artifact localization on the official SynthScars benchmark. Controlled cue-construction, inversion-horizon, component, reward-term, and counterfactual analyses support the utility of the residual stream under the evaluated settings, while free-form textual faithfulness and reliability under post-processing remain open limitations.

</details>


### [82] [Quasi-SVD: Learning a Lie-constrained matrix factorisation for real-time imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25967)
*Christopher Hahne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Quasi-SVD，一种可微、全并行化的GPU矩阵分解框架，通过非对称正交约束设计实现3-20倍加速，面向医学影像实时处理。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦医学影像（超声定位显微镜、Mueller矩阵偏振测量）的实时矩阵分解，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：矩阵分解加速；应用场景：医学影像实时处理（超声定位显微镜、神经外科组织表征）；传感器类型：医学成像设备；目标类别：不适用。与遥感场景无关。

Model architecture: Quasi-SVD框架：非对称正交约束设计，一个因子通过Lie参数化保证严格正交，其余分量通过软约束恢复，无需迭代奇异向量优化，支持全并行GPU分解。

Lightweight method: 方法本身属于算法加速（并行化SVD），不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统模型轻量化技术。加速比3-20x（相对cuSOLVER和随机SVD），吞吐率>25 FPS。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。方法面向GPU实现，未提及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT或星上/边缘部署。吞吐率>25 FPS在医学场景中提及，但未在遥感或星上场景验证。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：两个医学成像任务（超声定位显微镜时空背景减除、Mueller矩阵偏振测量）；评价指标：SSIM（0.89-0.94）、吞吐率（FPS）、加速比（3-20x）。无遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在两个医学成像任务上评估，跨多种成像仪器验证领域迁移能力，吞吐率>25 FPS。未与遥感目标检测方法对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 仅在医学影像上验证，未在遥感图像上测试；3. 未提供参数量、FLOPs等轻量化关键指标；4. 未讨论星上部署或边缘推理场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可参考的点：其"非对称正交约束+软约束"的并行化设计思路，若未来遥感在轨任务涉及矩阵分解类操作（如高光谱解混、SAR成像处理），可考虑类似并行加速策略。但对遥感目标检测模型轻量化的核心问题（检测头压缩、骨干网络剪枝、量化部署等）无直接帮助。

Abstract: Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) underlies matrix factorisation tasks across computational imaging, with medical applications increasingly demanding real-time processing. Yet SVD algorithms are inherently sequential, constraining real-time GPU throughput and limit online deployment in clinical pipelines. This study introduces Quasi-SVD, a differentiable, fully parallelized matrix factorization framework for GPUs. Rather than enforcing orthogonality on both factors, it guarantees exact orthogonality for a single Lie-parameterized factor while recovering the remaining components through soft constraints, enabling efficient parallel decomposition without iterative singular-vector optimization. This asymmetric design, provably sufficient for valid factorisation, achieves reconstruction fidelity of SSIM = 0.89-0.94 and accelerates computation by 3-20x relative to cuSOLVER and randomised SVD, enabling throughput above 25 FPS. Performance is evaluated on two medical imaging tasks spanning complementary computational regimes: (1) spatio-temporal background subtraction for ultrasound localisation microscopy, requiring high-dimensional matrix separation, and (2) Mueller matrix polarimetry for neurosurgical tissue characterisation, requiring massive batch processing of small matrices. Across both regimes and multiple imaging instruments, the proposed framework demonstrates robust domain transfer and throughput exceeding 25 FPS at clinical matrix scales, a rate sufficient for live image-guided workflows that classical solvers cannot currently support in these settings. By prioritising downstream reconstruction fidelity over exact spectral recovery, Quasi-SVD makes structured matrix factorisation practical for real-time imaging.

</details>


### [83] [Schrödinger's Cat: Probabilistic Representation and Prediction of Potential Scene Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25984)
*Timy Phan,Jannik Wiese,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GARFIELD，一种场景运动学概率模型，通过结构化时空潜变量表示对未来运动分布进行建模，实现高效轨迹采样和密度估计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通用场景运动预测（video prediction / motion planning），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感数据，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：场景运动预测（scene motion prediction / video prediction）；应用场景：通用视觉场景运动规划；传感器类型：普通RGB图像；目标类别：未说明，面向场景中所有运动元素。

Model architecture: GARFIELD：基于概率模型的场景运动学潜变量表示，包含结构化时空潜变量编码器、确定性密度解码器，支持联合轨迹采样和运动分布密度估计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及推理速度：轨迹采样比大视频生成模型快97倍，密度估计比蒙特卡洛采样快两个数量级，但未给出具体硬件平台和延迟数值。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及运动规划性能（motion planning performance）与大型视频生成模型相当。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体对比方法和消融实验细节。仅提及运动规划性能与大型视频生成模型有竞争力，采样速度更快。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 未涉及任何遥感场景下的目标检测任务；3. 未提供模型参数量、FLOPs等轻量化关键指标；4. 未讨论边缘/星上部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的启发点：其概率建模思路（对未来运动分布的结构化潜变量表示）若迁移到遥感视频流中的运动目标预测场景，可能有一定参考价值，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向关联度极低。

Abstract: Predicting how a scene may evolve from partial observations requires reasoning about multiple possible futures rather than committing to a single trajectory. Existing approaches either generate appearance-dominated video predictions or sample a small number of trajectories without explicitly modeling the distribution of possible motion. We introduce Goal-Aware Representations of Future kInEmatic Latent Distributions (GARFIELD), a probabilistic model of scene kinematics that learns a structured spatio-temporal latent representation of the distribution over possible futures given an image and optional spatio-temporally sparse constraints. The same latent representation enables both joint sampling of all trajectories and direct access to the underlying motion distribution through an efficient deterministic density decoder. As a result, uncertainty about future motion can be localized to specific scene elements and timesteps and progressively refined through additional constraints. Experiments demonstrate strong motion planning performance competitive with large video generation models while sampling trajectories $97\times$ faster. Our method further estimates motion densities two orders of magnitude faster than Monte-Carlo sampling from motion generation models, enabling interactive exploration and uncertainty-aware planning.

</details>


### [84] [On the Use of Synthetic Data for Threshold Calibration in Face Recognition: Performance and Security Implications for Border Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25990)
*Arto Apila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究利用合成人脸数据对欧洲边境管控EES系统中人脸识别阈值进行校准，发现合成数据在受控场景下有效，但在非约束条件下因分数分布尾部不匹配导致性能下降，无法替代真实数据。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于欧洲边境管控EES系统中的人脸识别阈值校准问题，涉及合成数据与真实数据的分布对齐分析，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人脸识别阈值校准（face recognition threshold calibration）；应用场景：欧洲边境管控EES系统（Entry/Exit System），document-to-live verification；传感器类型：人脸摄像头（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：人脸。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。涉及人脸识别系统（face recognition systems），但未给出网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用了合成人脸数据集（synthetic face data）和真实人脸数据集（real datasets）；评价指标：false match rate (FMR)，低FMR操作点（low-FMR operating points），genuine and impostor score distributions，识别性能（recognition performance），对morph-based attacks的脆弱性。

Experiments: 实验分析了合成数据与真实数据在受控和非约束条件下的校准行为差异，发现合成数据在受控场景可近似校准行为，但在非约束条件下因分数分布尾部不匹配导致性能显著下降，且校准结果高度依赖数据集。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，因此局限性不具参考价值。论文自身局限性：合成数据在非约束条件下无法可靠泛化，校准结果数据集依赖性高，无法替代真实数据进行高安全部署的阈值选择。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向完全无关，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、轻量化方法或星上部署中的任何核心要素。

Abstract: The recently deployed Entry/Exit System (EES) introduces large-scale biometric verification into European border control, requiring face recognition systems to operate at extremely low false match rates (FMR). While regulatory frameworks define performance targets at the EES Central System level, they do not specify how verification thresholds should be calibrated in practice at the Member State level. In operational settings, obtaining representative real-world data for calibration is often constrained by legal, logistical, and privacy limitations.
  In this work, we investigate the use of synthetic face data for threshold calibration in document-to-live verification scenarios relevant to border control systems. We analyze the alignment of genuine and impostor score distributions between synthetic and real datasets and evaluate the transferability of calibrated thresholds across domains, with a focus on low-FMR operating points.
  Our results show that synthetic data can approximate calibration behavior in controlled settings, but fails to reliably generalize to unconstrained conditions due to mismatches in score distribution tails. These discrepancies lead to significant degradation in recognition performance and increased vulnerability to morph-based attacks. We further demonstrate that calibration outcomes are highly dataset-dependent, even across synthetic datasets.
  Overall, our findings highlight that while synthetic data is useful for system development and preliminary calibration, our results indicate that reliable threshold selection in high-security deployments typically requires validation and adjustment using representative real-world data.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Zooming: Learning Multi-Tool Visual Reasoning for Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25993)
*Fengxiang Wang,Jiangnan Huang,Mingshuo Chen,Yueying Li,Yang Shi,Junwei Luo,Haoyu Wang,Yansheng Li,Jing Zhang,Haiyan Zhao,Wenjing Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoMTVR数据集和GeoLens多工具视觉推理MLLM，用于超高分辨率遥感图像的多工具视觉推理，通过强化学习优化工具调用策略，解决单工具放大方法的局限性。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及遥感/卫星图像（UHR RS imagery），但核心任务是视觉问答（VQA）和多工具视觉推理，而非目标检测。不涉及任何轻量化、模型压缩、在轨部署或实时推理内容。属于遥感场景下的多模态大语言模型应用，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 1分（不推荐精读）。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的核心方向（检测+轻量化+在轨部署）弱相关，不涉及目标检测任务，也不涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术。仅作为遥感MLLM应用方向的背景参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多工具视觉推理（multi-tool visual reasoning）、视觉问答（VQA）；应用场景：城市级超高分辨率遥感图像理解；传感器类型：光学卫星遥感图像（UHR RS imagery）；目标类别：摘要未明确说明具体目标类别，涉及object-level grounding；数据集：GeoMTVR（13K UHR VQA samples）。

Model architecture: GeoLens：基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的多工具视觉推理框架，包含工具调用（zoom-in等）、区域检查、目标级定位、跨工具证据整合等模块。采用SFT + 工具注意力强化学习（tool-attention-focused RL）的训练范式。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。MLLM架构通常参数量巨大（数十亿参数级别），不适合星上部署。未讨论任何边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件加速方案。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GeoMTVR（13K UHR VQA samples，包含交错的推理轨迹、多种视觉工具调用及返回的视觉观测结果）；XLRS-Bench（用于pilot study）；评价指标：accuracy（准确率）、evidence grounding（证据定位能力）、tool-use trajectories efficiency（工具使用轨迹效率）。

Experiments: GeoLens在GeoMTVR上经过SFT+RL训练后，一致优于直接推理（direct reasoning）和单工具放大（single-tool zoom-in）基线方法，在准确率、证据定位和工具使用效率上均取得更好结果。

Limitations: 1. 与目标检测任务无关，不涉及检测精度、召回率等指标；2. MLLM架构计算开销极大，完全不考虑轻量化和在轨部署；3. 仅针对光学遥感图像，未涉及SAR、高光谱、多光谱等其他遥感模态；4. 未讨论实时推理能力；5. 未提供模型参数量、推理速度等工程化指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 工具调用（tool-use）的思想可借鉴到在轨检测场景：例如设计一个轻量级"检测调度器"，根据图像复杂度动态决定是否调用高精度检测模型或快速检测模型，实现自适应推理；2. 强化学习优化工具选择策略的思路可用于在轨资源受限场景下的模型选择或分辨率自适应；3. 多工具证据整合的思想可启发多传感器（光学+SAR）融合检测的决策策略设计；4. 但整体而言，该论文与轻量化目标检测的核心方向差距较大，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote-sensing (RS) imagery provides fine-grained Earth-observation evidence over city-scale scenes, but poses a fundamental challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs): task-relevant evidence is often sparse, local, and spatially dispersed across extremely large visual contexts. A natural solution is to equip MLLMs with zoom-in tools for active local inspection. However, through a pilot study on XLRS-Bench, we find that zoom-in is only partially effective: it resolves easy and medium-level tasks with locally recoverable evidence, but saturates on hard cases requiring global search, multi-region comparison, path planning, or dispersed-evidence reasoning. Motivated by this finding, we move beyond single-tool zoom-in and introduce GeoMTVR, a large-scale Geospatial Multi-Tool Visual Reasoning dataset built from wide-area satellite imagery. GeoMTVR contains 13K UHR VQA samples with interleaved reasoning trajectories, diverse visual tool calls, and returned visual observations, enabling models to learn question decomposition, tool selection, regional inspection, object-level grounding, auxiliary visual reasoning, and cross-tool evidence integration. Beyond supervised fine-tuning, we propose a tool-attention-focused reinforcement learning algorithm that concentrates optimization on critical tool-use decisions, including when to invoke tools, which tool to select, where to apply it, and how to interpret tool outputs. By combining SFT on GeoMTVR with our RL algorithm, we develop GeoLens, a multi-tool visual reasoning MLLM for UHR RS. Experiments show that GeoLens consistently outperforms direct reasoning and single-tool zoom-in baselines, achieving stronger accuracy, better evidence grounding, and more efficient tool-use trajectories.

</details>


### [86] [Parallel Decoding Distillation for Fast Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26004)
*Neta Shaul,Chao Liu,Arash Vahdat,Julius Berner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并行解码蒸馏(PDD)方法，通过一次网络评估预测多个去噪步，加速视频扩散/流模型的推理，减少采样步数至4-8 NFE。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于视频/图像生成模型的推理加速（扩散模型蒸馏），与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关系。虽然"蒸馏"方法在轻量化领域有通用性，但论文场景是生成式AI（Text-to-Video/Audio/Image），而非遥感目标检测的判别式任务，属于泛机器学习方法，弱相关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。仅作为蒸馏方法的通用参考，优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频/图像生成加速（Text-to-Video, Text-to-Audio, Text-to-Image）；应用场景：生成式AI推理加速；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于预训练的扩散/流匹配模型（LTX-2.3, Wan 14B, Qwen-Image），通过并行解码蒸馏架构，一次网络评估预测多个去噪步。

Lightweight method: 知识蒸馏（Knowledge Distillation）的一种变体——轨迹蒸馏（trajectory-based distillation），并行解码蒸馏（Parallel Decoding Distillation, PDD），将多步扩散采样蒸馏为少步（4-8 NFE）生成。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS等传统轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及采样步数减少至4-8 NFE，但模型本身为14B参数级别（Wan 14B），不适合星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 评估在LTX-2.3 Text-to-Video/Audio、Wan 14B Text-to-Video、Qwen-Image Text-to-Image上进行。评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称（如FVD、IS、CLIP score等未提及）。

Experiments: 在多个生成模型上达到SOTA性能（4-8 NFE），并显著提升生成视频的多样性。未提供与传统蒸馏方法（VSD、对抗损失）的定量对比细节。

Limitations: 1. 论文面向生成任务（视频/图像），与遥感目标检测的判别任务本质不同；2. 模型参数量巨大（14B级别），不适用于星上资源受限场景；3. 摘要未报告实际推理速度、模型大小等关键部署指标；4. 未说明在遥感数据上的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 1. 轨迹蒸馏的思想可借鉴：在遥感目标检测模型中，可尝试将多阶段检测头或特征金字塔的推理过程蒸馏为单步或少步预测；2. 并行解码策略：在遥感旋转框检测中，可探索一次前向预测多个尺度的检测结果，减少推理次数；3. 但需注意：该方法是生成式蒸馏，与检测任务的判别式蒸馏差异较大，直接迁移需谨慎。

Abstract: Generation in video diffusion or flow models is computationally expensive due to the slow and iterative sampling process. Current state-of-the-art (SOTA) acceleration methods heavily rely on variational score distillation (VSD) and adversarial losses to distill diffusion models into few-step generators. Albeit achieving high-quality video generation, these training losses are notoriously hard to optimize and suffer from mode collapse, leading to loss of video diversity and lack of motion. In this paper, we introduce Parallel Decoding Distillation (PDD), a simplified and scalable trajectory-based distillation method for fast inference of diffusion and flow matching models. Our architecture and training procedure are compatible with any pre-trained model and support sampling with a varying number of function evaluations (NFE). PDD accelerates generation by predicting multiple denoising steps per network evaluation. Conceptually, it learns a representation of the mean velocity without regressing its derivative using JVPs or finite-difference approximations. Our method achieves SOTA performance with 4-8 NFE on LTX-2.3 Text-to-Video/Audio, Wan 14B Text-to-Video, and Qwen-Image Text-to-Image. Moreover, PDD presents a significant improvement in generated video diversity.

</details>


### [87] [Wonder: Video World Model Done Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26037)
*Jiacong Xu,Hanwen Jiang,Zhixin Shu,Kalyan Sunkavalli,Vishal M. Patel,Yiqun Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个通用视频世界模型Wonder，支持实时、相机可控的世界探索，通过密集坐标场、稀疏注意力记忆机制和蒸馏流水线实现长时程、高帧率的视频生成。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是通用视频生成/世界模型方向，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频生成/世界模型；应用场景：通用视频探索与交互式相机控制；传感器类型：RGB图像/视频；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于扩散/蒸馏的视频生成模型，包含相机控制模块（密集坐标场）、稀疏注意力记忆机制、教师-学生蒸馏架构。摘要未说明具体backbone。

Lightweight method: 涉及蒸馏（self-forcing-style distillation），但目的是提升学生模型对控制信号的响应能力和长时记忆保持，而非模型压缩或加速用于边缘/星上部署。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。虽然提到16 FPS实时生成，但这是在高性能GPU上的推理速度，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。该论文面向通用视频生成，不涉及检测任务，不涉及遥感场景，不涉及模型压缩部署。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，仅两点可间接参考：(1) 稀疏注意力机制可用于降低遥感大图的注意力计算开销，但需改造为检测任务；(2) 蒸馏技术虽用于生成任务，但其控制信号保持策略可能对检测模型蒸馏有启发。总体而言借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: We present Wonder, a general-purpose video world model for real-time, camera-controllable world exploration. Given an image or a conditional video, Wonder constructs a playable world where users can navigate interactively by moving the camera, discovering unseen regions, and revisiting previously observed areas in real time and over a long-term horizon. Achieving this capability requires a system-level co-design of control method, memory mechanism, and training strategy. We introduce a novel camera conditioning with a dense coordinate field whose renderings provide spatially aligned motion and orientation cues, allowing the model to interpret camera motion directly as visual evidence. To support fast and precise memory retrieval over a growing generation context, we propose an efficient sparse attention-based memory mechanism, enabling the model to selectively attend to a small set of relevant context tokens at inference time, regardless of actual context length. We further develop several techniques to rectify the self-forcing-style distillation pipeline, improving the student model's ability to respect control signals, as well as maintaining diverse generation modes and long-term memory from the teacher. Together, these components enable Wonder to synthesize diverse, minute-scale videos at 16 FPS while preserving coherent geometry, appearance, and dynamics across long rollouts. Beyond image-to-video generation, Wonder naturally supports video-conditioned generation, allowing existing dynamic scenes to be re-shot in real time.

</details>


### [88] [VetClaw: An Edge-Cloud Multimodal Agentic System for Veterinary Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26042)

Abstract: We present VetClaw, an edge-cloud multimodal agentic system for early veterinary disease screening. VetClaw uses a camera module as an edge sensing device and sends captured images, together with optional symptom descriptions, to a server-hosted vision-language model for zero-shot disease classification. The system separates agent interaction from workflow orchestration: OpenClaw provides scheduling, tool access, user interaction, and notification services on the edge device, while LangGraph manages the stateful screening workflow, including input validation, image transmission, model invocation, safety checks, conditional routing, failure handling, and structured logging. This design moves beyond static image classification by enabling the system to collect visual evidence, invoke external models, apply deterministic safety rules, and generate diagnostic-support alerts. Results show that image-only VLM prediction remains limited, whereas symptom-guided and multimodal inputs improve zero-shot classification performance. Thus, VetClaw transforms a static prediction model into a coordinated, safety-aware system that can use tools, manage workflows, handle failures, and escalate uncertain cases.

</details>

### [89] [Egocentric Station Holding of Robotic Fish in Unknown Turbulent Background Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24860)
*Xiaozhu Lin,Xu Huang,Hongru Dai,Xiaopei Liu,Junzhi Yu,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SWiFT框架，通过强化学习实现仿生机器鱼在未知湍流中的定点保持控制，核心是计算流体力学仿真与sim-to-real迁移。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究水下仿生机器鱼（robotic fish）在流动水中的定点保持（station-holding）控制问题，属于水下机器人/流体力学/强化学习控制领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：水下仿生机器鱼定点保持控制（station-holding）；场景：自然水体环境中的未知湍流；传感器类型：egocentric feedback（自我中心反馈，无显式流场感知）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: SWiFT框架包含：自由游动水槽实验装置、基于CFD的高效物理一致性仿真器、sim-to-real迁移管线、强化学习策略。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提到"real-world deployment"，但指的是水下机器人的实际部署，而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 评价指标：root-mean-square error (RMSE) of distance（距离均方根误差）。摘要未说明具体数据集。

Experiments: 该方法在所有指标上优于现有方法，尤其在距离RMSE上提升显著。验证了仅凭egocentric feedback（无显式流场感知）即可在未知湍流中实现定点保持，类似生物趋流性（rheotaxis）。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向的任何信息。摘要本身未说明局限性。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究水下仿生机器鱼控制，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务场景、传感器模态、技术路线（强化学习控制 vs. 深度学习目标检测与模型压缩）上均无交集。

Abstract: Approaching a target position and holding station in flowing water is a fundamental and critical capability for robotic fish operating in natural aquatic environments. Despite decades of advances in enhancing swimming efficiency and maneuverability, this capability remains underdeveloped, largely owing to the insufficiently characterized, highly nonlinear fluid-structure interactions inherent to freely swimming robotic fish in flows. To bridge this gap, we propose the SWiFT framework, a Swimming With Flow Toolbox that enables the efficient exploration of an egocentric station-holding policy for a body and/or caudal fin (BCF) robotic fish in unknown and turbulent background flows via reinforcement learning (RL). Our SWiFT integrates a free-swimming flow-tank experimental setup with a highly efficient, physically consistent computational fluid dynamics (CFD)-based simulator and a systematic sim-to-real transfer pipeline. The resulting policy achieves substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods across all metrics, most notably root-mean-square error (RMSE) of distance. Furthermore, we validated that egocentric feedback alone, without any explicit flow sensing, enables station-holding in unknown turbulent flows, closely mirroring the biological phenomenon of rheotaxis. Accordingly, the success of this egocentric station-holding policy not only advances robotic fish control toward real-world deployment, but also highlights SWiFT's promise as a foundation for tackling complex swimming tasks for underwater robots.

</details>


### [90] [Steeringless Drifting: Differential-Torque Control of a Four-Wheel Independently Driven Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24863)
*Sheng Zhao,Zexin Wu,Dongyang Zhou,Bolin Zhao,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种针对无转向四轮独立驱动车辆的差动扭矩漂移控制方法，通过差动车轮扭矩产生直接横摆力矩实现稳态漂移。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是自动驾驶车辆的漂移控制方法，属于车辆动力学与控制领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明深度学习模型结构。涉及双轨车辆动力学模型（double-track vehicle model），属于物理模型而非神经网络。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。实验在1:10比例车辆上进行，与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明遥感数据集。使用仿真和1:10比例车辆实验验证，评价指标为侧滑角（sideslip angle约20°）和轨迹跟踪能力（figure-eight drift tracking）。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于车辆动力学控制领域，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Control methods for emerging vehicle chassis architectures are important for autonomous driving near handling limits. Unlike conventional drift control, which relies on mechanical steering and rear-tire saturation, a steering-free four-wheel independently driven (4WID) vehicle can generate direct yaw moment through differential wheel torques. This paper proposes a differential-torque drift control method for such a vehicle. A double-track vehicle model incorporating four-wheel differential actuation is established, based on which a drift-equilibrium calculation method and a closed-loop drift controller are developed. The proposed approach is validated through simulations and experiments on a 1:10-scale vehicle. The results show that the vehicle can achieve steady circular drifting with a sideslip angle of approximately 20$^\circ$ and perform figure-eight drift tracking. This study demonstrates the feasibility of drift control using only differential wheel torques and provides a new perspective on near-limit control for steering-free vehicle architectures.

</details>


### [91] [Amortising Trajectory Optimisation for Residual MPC via Implicit Contact Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24959)
*Daniel Layeghi,Thomas Corbères,Calum Arnott,Aditya Kamireddypalli,Hashim Al-Obaidi,Steve Tonneau,Michael Mistry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于隐函数定理(IFT)的AD辅助隐式微分方法，用于可微仿真中接触丰富轨迹优化的高效求导，并引入优化器蒸馏用于残差MPC。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人学中的可微仿真、接触动力学轨迹优化和模型预测控制(MPC)，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星在轨部署或任何视觉感知任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：可微仿真中的接触丰富轨迹优化；应用场景：机器人操作（Finger, Franka, Unitree机器人）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于隐函数定理(IFT)的隐式微分方法，用于Mujoco MJX仿真器中的正则化光滑接触模型求导。

Lightweight method: 提出了"optimizer distillation for residual MPC"（优化器蒸馏），将全时域iLQR摊销为策略网络，用于引导短时域残差iLQR。这是一种知识蒸馏思想，但应用于控制策略而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。文中提及的IFT方法相比unrolled AD在内存上有显著优势（256个接触点时节省20倍内存，16个接触点96自由度时节省6倍内存），但这是针对仿真求导场景，非模型部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 实验平台：Finger, Franka, Unitree机器人；评价指标：六步成功率(six-step success)，IFT相比unrolled AD的内存增长对比。

Experiments: IFT方法保持编译临时内存几乎恒定（1到10次迭代变化<4%），而unrolled AD增长10.6倍；IFT在256个接触点时内存使用减少20倍，16个接触点96自由度时减少6倍。优化器蒸馏在Finger/Franka/Unitree上将六步成功率提升28-98个百分点。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明IFT方法在视觉任务上的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。可借鉴的点非常有限：(1) IFT方法避免展开计算图的思想，与轻量化中的"避免冗余计算"思路有类比价值；(2) 优化器蒸馏(optimizer distillation)将复杂优化过程摊销为轻量策略网络的思想，与知识蒸馏将大模型知识迁移到小模型有相似性，但应用场景完全不同。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation can accelerate contact-rich trajectory optimisation by exposing local sensitivities of task outcomes to controls. Existing approaches either use finite differences, which are expensive and step-size sensitive; differentiate iterative contact solvers by unrolling automatic differentiation (AD), which stores a growing computation trace; or require intricate, solver-specific KKT sensitivity derivations.
