Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到Direct推理时间为2.9-5.5 ms，但未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署关键指标。该延迟数据是在何种硬件平台上测得，摘要未说明。且该工作面向机器人控制，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LeWM（四个官方任务）；评价指标：成功率（success rate）、宏成功率（macro success）、采样减少倍数、预测-专家动作族kNN与Direct成功率的相关系数r=0.954。

Experiments: 在LeWM四个任务上，单epoch、零搜索模型分别达到85.78%、100.00%、97.67%、97.89%成功率；可选局部CEM结合Direct计划达到96.86%宏成功率，使用384个候选序列替代9000个，采样减少23.44倍，比纯CEM提升16.00个百分点；共享四任务编码器达到89.39% E5 Direct宏成功率。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化技术；3. 仅在LeWM机器人控制benchmark上验证，未在遥感/卫星数据上测试；4. 未说明模型参数量、FLOPs等计算资源需求；5. 未讨论星上/边缘部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能值得注意的点是：其Direct推理仅需2.9-5.5 ms，说明端到端架构在推理速度上有潜力，但这是针对完全不同的问题设定（世界模型中的动作预测而非目标检测）。对于遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题，本论文无直接可借鉴内容。

Abstract: Forward latent world models predict how actions change a scene, but recover actions for a desired change only through expensive test-time search. We introduce INTACT (INtent-To-ACTion), an end-to-end JEPA that turns action-labeled, reward-free trajectories into a deployable intent-to-action interface. Each transition supplies physical intent $z_{t+1}-z_t$, while a future goal supplies deployment intent $\operatorname{sg}(z_g)-z_t$. The architecture is isomorphic between the local and goal motion-intent backbone-input graphs through an identical four-slot grammar and shared parameters, and between supported local and goal motion-intent families through action-law semantics induced by the same predictor rather than pointwise latent equality. INTACT also provides intact transfer from RGB evidence to action-effective latent intent coordinates and from intent families to their corresponding action-law families. Asymmetric endpoint gradients ground physical successors and fix future goals as anchors, joining representation learning and control without pointwise latent matching or globally linear dynamics. The resulting coordinates support a robust distributional action law: its conditional mean serves directly as a search-free policy, while sampling remains available for diversity or optional verification. On the four official LeWM tasks, one-epoch, zero-search models reach 85.78\%, 100.00\%, 97.67\%, and 97.89\% success. Optional local CEM centered on the Direct plan reaches 96.86\% macro success using 384 instead of 9,000 candidate sequences, reducing sampling by $23.44\times$ while improving pure CEM by 16.00 points. One shared four-task encoder reaches 89.39\% E5 Direct macro and improves every task over jointly trained LeWM, while predicted--expert action-family kNN tracks Direct success at $r=0.954$. Direct inference takes 2.9--5.5 ms.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

### [121] [Analysis of the Shortcut Learning and Clever Hans Effect in CNN based ECG Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25117)
*Abhay Kumar Pathak,Mrityunjay Chaubey,Manjari Gupta,Deepti Mishra*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文研究ECG心电图图像分类中的捷径学习（shortcut learning）和Clever Hans效应，分析CNN模型是否依赖非生理性视觉线索而非心电图波形形态进行决策。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医学ECG图像分类的模型可解释性和捷径学习问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：ECG图像分类（医学影像）；场景：临床心电图诊断；传感器类型：ECG图像（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：心肌梗死、异常心跳、正常类别。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 使用卷积神经网络（CNN），具体架构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：公开ECG图像数据集；评价指标：Shortcut retention score、prediction consistency、confidence divergence、Integrated Gradients、occlusion sensitivity。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 构建了6种图像特征集（FS1-FS6），通过控制变量实验测试模型是否依赖波形信息或人工伪影进行分类。进行了归因分析和遮挡敏感性测试。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测方法。属于医学影像可解释性研究。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究的是模型是否学到正确特征（捷径学习检测），而非模型压缩或加速。对于遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无参考价值。

Abstract: Deep learning models for ECG image classification may achieve high accuracy by exploiting non-physiological visual cues instead of ECG waveform morphology. Given the black-box nature of deep learning models, their promise of high predictive performance often remains insufficiently translated into clinical or real-world trust, interpretability, and actionable decision-making. In this study, we examine shortcut learning and Clever Hans effect in a publicly available ECG image dataset using convolutional neural networks. In process we have created six image-derived feature sets (FSs), FS1: raw full ECG images, FS2: cropped waveform-only images, FS3: waveform-masked metadata images, FS4: red-arrow artifact images for the myocardial infarction class, FS5: contrast-enhanced images for the abnormal heartbeat class and FS6: Gaussian-blurred images for the normal class. These controlled representations were used to test whether classification performance persists when waveform information is removed or when artificial class-specific artifacts are introduced. Shortcut retention score, prediction consistency and confidence divergence across Feature-Set Representations have been calculated to assess the transparency about the learning pattern. Along with factual results, average Integrated Gradients and occlusion sensitivity test results are presented to inspect whether model attribution focused on ECG-relevant waveform regions or on non-clinical artifacts. Performance changes across feature sets and attribution patterns were used to identify potential Clever Hans behavior. This study evaluates whether ECG image classifiers learn clinically meaningful morphology or shortcut cues introduced by report layout, metadata, contrast, blur, or artificial markers.

</details>


### [122] [SplatStream: Fine Granular Scalable Gaussian Splatting for Adaptive 3D Scene Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25971)
*Muhammad Talha,William Gordon,Sajid Umair,Anique Akhtar,Joel Jung*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出SplatStream，一种面向动态3D高斯泼溅场景的可分级流媒体传输框架，通过质量/分辨率分层、帧间预测编码和轻量级跨层Transformer预测器实现自适应低延迟传输。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于动态3D场景的实时渲染与流媒体传输（Gaussian Splatting），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：动态3D场景的可分级流媒体传输与实时渲染；场景：沉浸式媒体、自适应流媒体；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于3D Gaussian Splatting的编解码框架，引入质量层和分辨率层的分层结构，使用轻量级跨层Transformer预测器进行层间和帧间预测，采用基于体积-不透明度的Gaussian打包策略。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到"lightweight cross-layer transformer based predictor"，但这里的"轻量"是指预测器网络规模较小，并非面向边缘/星上部署的模型压缩方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文面向的是流媒体传输场景，而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果、对比方法和消融实验。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的技术参考。论文未涉及任何目标检测、遥感图像理解或模型压缩部署内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的概念是"可分级编码"（scalable coding）思想，但这是流媒体领域的经典方法，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting (GS) enables high quality real-time rendering for immersive media, but its large representation size and frame-wise redundancy create significant challenges for adaptive streaming. This paper presents SplatStream, a fine granular scalable Gaussian splatting framework for dynamic 3D scene delivery. The proposed method decompose the GS scenes into quality and resolution layers, and introduces inter-layer predictive coding to achieve scalability. For temporal direction, B-frames are introduced to have temporal quality scalability. A lightweight cross-layer transformer based predictor is utilized for both cross layer and temporal predictions. In addition, a volume-opacity based importance measure is used for fine-grained Gaussian packetization, allowing visually important primitives to be transmitted earlier for progressive refinement. Finally, the scalable GS bitstream is mapped to an MPEG-DASH compatible sub-representation structure, enabling fine granular adaptive, low-latency delivery of dynamic Gaussian splatting content under bandwidth-varying conditions.

</details>

### [123] [FinAbstain: Uncertainty-Calibrated Multimodal RAG for Selective Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24875)
*Dorothy Torres,Wei Cheng,Henan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FinAbstain框架，面向金融多模态RAG的不确定性校准与选择性预测，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦金融时间序列预测、多模态RAG、不确定性校准与选择性预测，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等任何检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或部署技术。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：金融时间序列方向预测（1天/5天异常收益方向、20天波动率区间）与选择性预测（abstention）。应用场景：金融投资、风险管理。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: FinAbstain框架，包含point-in-time检索器、多模态agent（fundamental, news, technical, risk, verification agents）、不确定性聚合模块、控制器（决定预测或abstain）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及latency和cost作为评估指标，但面向金融系统而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体金融数据集名称。评价指标：accuracy, calibration, risk-coverage, citation, trading, latency, cost metrics。实验设置：采用模拟结果（simulated results）而非实证数据。

Experiments: 评估了temperature scaling, isotonic regression, conformal prediction及提出的hybrid uncertainty score四种校准方法。在1天/5天异常收益方向、20天波动率区间及abstention决策上进行评估。结果为模拟数据展示，非真实金融数据实证。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术方法。2. 论文使用模拟数据而非真实金融数据，结论的可靠性有限。3. 摘要未说明模型参数量、推理速度等工程指标。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题方向基本无关，无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的概念是"选择性预测（selective prediction）"——在不确定性高时拒绝预测，这在星上资源受限场景下可类比为"在图像质量差或模型置信度低时跳过推理以节省能耗"，但论文未涉及任何遥感或轻量化技术，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can synthesize financial narratives but may express high confidence when evidence is sparse, stale, or contradictory. This failure is especially consequential in forecasting, where filings, news, prices, volume, and technical signals can disagree. We present FinAbstain, a research framework for uncertainty-calibrated multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with selective prediction. A point-in-time retriever admits only information public at the forecast timestamp and supplies modality-specific evidence to fundamental, news, technical, risk, and verification agents. Their probabilistic assessments are aggregated with retrieval relevance, evidence contradiction, repeated-sample consistency, and historical calibration statistics. Temperature scaling, isotonic regression, conformal prediction, and a proposed hybrid uncertainty score are evaluated under a common chronological protocol. A controller predicts bullish, bearish, or neutral outcomes only when uncertainty is below a validated threshold; otherwise it abstains, requests evidence, reduces exposure, or routes the case to human review. The evaluation covers one- and five-day abnormal-return direction, twenty-day volatility intervals, and abstention decisions, using accuracy, calibration, risk--coverage, citation, trading, latency, and cost metrics. To make the design auditable before a full data collection is complete, we report explicitly labeled simulated results rather than empirical claims. These results illustrate the intended hypothesis: calibrated abstention may trade coverage for lower selective error and drawdown. The contribution is a time-safe architecture, a composite uncertainty formulation, and a reproducible evaluation blueprint for evidence-grounded selective financial forecasting.

</details>

TL;DR: 该论文讨论的是面向产品生命周期管理（PLM）的表格数据嵌入方法及其相似性搜索评估，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。

Abstract: Task-agnostic tabular embeddings are increasingly used for similarity search in real-world business systems such as Product Lifecycle Management (PLM). However, leading embedding approaches are optimized primarily for prediction tasks - not for producing human preference aligned similarity rankings. We argue that standard downstream metrics are insufficient to fully assess embedding trustworthiness for similarity search and that human preference aligned evaluation is a necessary and currently missing component. We present a concrete evaluation procedure and illustrate the problem through a PLM use case.

</details>


### [125] [Behavior-Driven Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24881)
*Caroline Dominik,Rolf Drechsler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于行为驱动开发(BDD)的可解释性方法(BDX)，用于解释安全关键系统的行为，以RISC-V处理器异常解释为案例验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或任何计算机视觉/深度学习内容，属于系统设计与可解释性工程领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何深度学习模型或神经网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何遥感或目标检测数据集及评价指标。

Experiments: 仅提及在RISC-V处理器异常解释场景下进行了案例研究，未提供定量实验结果。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术内容。论文聚焦于系统可解释性工程方法，属于软件工程/形式化方法领域。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: As system complexity has vastly increased, it has become significantly more challenging for a single person or a team to fully understand all aspects of an entire system. Particularly, this holds when considering all the different stages of a system's development life cycle, such as, e.g., design or maintenance. But especially for safety-critical systems it is essential that the final design can be trusted. Because of this, explainability is becoming an important requirement for modern systems. In this paper, we aim to achieve this goal by utilizing Behavior-Driven Development (BDD), where the expected system behavior is given in the form of structured scenarios. These scenarios give a sequence of actions for each functionality, and by this can be directly translated into explanations. We introduce this method of deriving explanations based on the specification as Behavior-Driven Explainability (BDX). While applicable at any development stage or abstraction level, a case study for the explanation of exceptions in a RISC-V processor shows the support this concept adds during system design.

</details>


### [126] [Multiclass Classification without Labels via Posterior Simplex Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24943)
*Raphaël Bonnet-Guerrini,Johann Ioannou-Nikolaides,Troels Petersen,Vincenzo Piuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种从无标签混合数据中恢复多类别结构的弱监督学习方法（Multi-class CWoLa），通过混合身份训练分类器并利用单纯形几何提取潜在类别。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文核心是弱监督/无标签分类方法（Classification without Labels），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署等任何相关主题。虽然实验用到了Galaxy10天文数据集，但这是天文图像而非遥感图像，且任务是无标签分类而非目标检测。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：弱监督多类别分类（无标签混合数据分类）；应用场景：通用分类任务，实验在MNIST、CIFAR-10、Galaxy10 DECaLS上进行；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：手写数字、自然图像物体、星系形态分类。

Model architecture: 摘要未详细说明具体网络结构。提到使用标准分类器（standard classifier）区分混合身份，以及后验单纯形拟合（post-hoc simplex fitting）或瓶颈架构（bottleneck architecture）来提取潜在类别结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。论文不关注部署效率。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST、CIFAR-10、Galaxy10 DECaLS；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，但提到比较了弱监督与全监督性能的差距。

Experiments: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、Galaxy10 DECaLS上进行了实验，表明仅利用混合身份即可恢复潜在类别及其混合比例，缩小了弱监督与全监督性能之间的差距。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 方法针对分类任务而非目标检测；3. 未讨论任何部署效率问题；4. 实验仅在小型通用数据集上进行，未在遥感场景下验证。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的弱关联是：在遥感在轨场景中，如果遇到无标签或弱标签的遥感图像分类问题，该方法的理论框架（从混合数据中恢复类别结构）可能有潜在参考价值。但与本课题（遥感目标检测+轻量化）的核心方向基本不相关。

Abstract: In many classification problems, reliable instance-level labels are unavailable. However, it is often possible to construct weakly enriched unlabeled samples: datasets selected by different cuts, sources, populations, or experimental conditions that change latent class proportions without revealing them. Classification without Labels (CWoLa) shows that, in the binary case ($K=2$), a classifier trained to distinguish two impure mixtures with different class proportions can recover an optimal class discriminator without knowing the mixture proportions. We extend this principle to multiclass learning from several unlabeled mixtures ($K>2$), where the learner observes only mixture identity and neither latent class labels nor class-prior matrices. We prove that, for a multiclass mixture model, the Bayes-optimal mixture classifier $g^\star$ maps data points into a $(K-1)$-simplex embedded in mixture-posterior space. The $K$ vertices of this simplex are induced by the latent classes through the unknown mixing matrix. Leveraging this geometry, we propose prior-free procedures that train a standard classifier to distinguish mixture identities and then extract latent class structure using either post-hoc simplex fitting or a bottleneck architecture. Experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Galaxy10 DECaLS show that mixture identity alone can recover latent classes and their fractions in the mixture. By narrowing the gap between weakly supervised and fully supervised performance, we provide a mathematically grounded, scalable tool for multiclass discovery in label-scarce domains.

</details>


### [127] [Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24983)
*Ziwei Zhang,Jonathan Yu-Meng Li,Zhihao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GDRO框架，用Sinkhorn散度替代似然函数，实现任意可采样条件生成器下的分布鲁棒优化，在库存管理和社交导航任务上验证效果。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于分布鲁棒优化（DRO）的生成式建模方法，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：分布鲁棒优化（DRO）；应用场景：库存管理（inventory）、社交导航（SocialGAN navigation）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明与遥感或目标检测相关。

Model architecture: 核心框架为GDRO（Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization），采用sampler-Sinkhorn pairing：sampler表示条件分布，Sinkhorn散度比较分布差异，无需似然函数。支持显式和隐式生成器。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署等任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：库存管理场景使用regret（遗憾值），社交导航场景使用collision rate（碰撞率）。

Experiments: 在库存管理任务上，GDRO相比名义决策（nominal decisions）减少60%的罕见场景遗憾值（rare-context inventory regret）；在SocialGAN导航任务上，减少50%的碰撞次数。摘要未说明与其他DRO方法的对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 摘要未讨论计算效率、推理速度等部署相关指标；3. 仅在库存管理和社交导航两个非遥感场景验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但Sinkhorn散度作为一种无需似然函数的分布距离度量，理论上可用于遥感目标检测中的域适应或分布偏移问题（如不同卫星传感器间的数据分布差异），但论文本身未朝此方向探索。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly adopted in distributionally robust optimization (DRO), but existing approaches trade off model compatibility and adversarial structure: methods that accept arbitrary samplers do not restrict worst-case laws to a generator family, while generator-parameterized adversaries rely on model-specific access such as likelihoods, scores, or training data. We propose Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization (GDRO), a principled framework that accepts any sampleable conditional generator as the nominal model and restricts worst-case laws to a chosen conditional generator family. The key is the sampler-Sinkhorn pairing: samplers represent the conditional laws exactly, while Sinkhorn divergence compares their induced distributions without likelihood access and can be estimated from samples alone. The resulting population problem admits a direct finite-sample approximation and differentiable primal-dual implementation at the active decision context. For Lipschitz losses, the population Sinkhorn radius bounds downstream degradation. Across explicit and implicit generators, our method reduces rare-context inventory regret by 60% and SocialGAN navigation collisions by 50% relative to nominal decisions.

</details>


### [128] [Mechanisms of Width Scaling in Normalized Residual Networks: The Effective Alignment Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24887)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Zicheng Yan,Yunquan Zhang,Guangming Tan,Fangming Liu,Daning Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析了神经网络宽度扩展（residual expansion）中训练-测试梯度对齐问题，提出了有效对齐维度（effective alignment dimension）这一可测量量，并给出了测试风险改进的高概率条件。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文是纯理论性的神经网络宽度扩展分析，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署中的任何具体任务或场景。

Reading priority: 1分。纯理论论文，不涉及遥感、目标检测、轻量化部署中的任何核心要素，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何遥感/卫星/目标检测任务或场景。实验仅在通用NLP模型（LLaMA-style Transformers, Pythia）和通用视觉模型（ResNet-20）上进行。

Model architecture: 分析对象为残差扩展（residual expansion）下的神经网络，实验涉及LLaMA-style Transformers、Pythia、ResNet-20。不涉及遥感目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文研究的是网络宽度扩展（即模型变宽/变大）的理论性质，与轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）方向相反。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标为有效对齐维度（effective alignment dimension）、经验错位概率（empirical misalignment）、留出损失变化（held-out loss changes）。

Experiments: 在LLaMA-style Transformers、Pythia、ResNet-20上实验，发现更宽的模型具有更大的有效对齐维度和更低的经验错位。残差干预实验验证了对齐统计量能预测留出损失变化的符号和幅度。

Limitations: 1. 纯理论分析，缺乏与遥感/卫星/目标检测任务的结合；2. 研究的是模型变宽（expansion）而非变窄/压缩，与轻量化方向相反；3. 实验仅在通用模型上进行，未验证在遥感数据上的有效性；4. 未讨论实际部署中的计算约束。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下理论视角可间接参考：1. 有效对齐维度（effective alignment dimension）这一概念可用于分析轻量化后模型的特征对齐质量，评估剪枝/量化对梯度信号-噪声几何的影响；2. 论文提供的训练-测试风险改进条件，可启发轻量化模型在遥感场景下的泛化性理论分析；3. 但整体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接关联度很低。

Abstract: Existing theories of neural-network width characterize asymptotic limits, but provide limited guidance on whether an expansion direction identified from finite training data remains beneficial on unseen data. We study this problem for function-preserving residual expansion and introduce the effective alignment dimension, a measurable quantity describing the signal-noise geometry of activation gradients. By deriving the exact mean and variance of the inner product between independently estimated training and test gradients, we obtain a finite-sample upper bound on misalignment probability. The bound depends only on the effective alignment dimension and an effective sample size, requiring finite second moments and a nonzero population gradient, without covariance spectral assumptions or prescribed width-growth rates. We integrate this certificate into the train-test residual-expansion framework, yielding a high-probability condition for test-risk improvement. Experiments across width-controlled LLaMA-style Transformers, Pythia, and ResNet-20 show that wider models exhibit larger effective alignment dimensions and lower empirical misalignment. Direct residual interventions confirm that the alignment statistic predicts the sign and magnitude of held-out loss changes.

</details>


### [129] [GAUGE: Grading Agent-Built Financial Models Without a Golden Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24889)
*Jiacheng Lu,Sinuo Wang,Wentao Zhao,Rui Sun,Cheng Hua,Tao Song,Hui Cai,Beidi Luan,Zhengze Wu,Lingjing Teng,Yijia He,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出GAUGE基准，用于评估金融分析模型（估值模型）与真实分析师实践的匹配度，不涉及遥感、目标检测或轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全聚焦于金融领域的估值模型评估，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或任何模型轻量化/部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：金融估值模型评估；应用场景：金融分析；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文关注的是评估基准（GAUGE），而非具体模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：1,001份分析师工作簿、196任务评估集、48任务评估核心；评价指标：failure-aware score φ₀、机械性指标通过率、判断性指标通过率。

Experiments: 55名参与者（资深分析师、初级分析师、金融学生）的已知组研究；24个agent的1,011次生成评分；最佳agent得分53.4，低于所有资深分析师和大多数初级分析师。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于金融NLP/Agent评估领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化无任何技术交集。

Abstract: Financial models combine public disclosures with analyst assumptions to produce forecasts and valuations. While some components can be checked mechanically, forecasts, discount rates, and target prices often admit multiple reasonable answers. Existing benchmarks nevertheless tend to grade such outputs against a single expert reference. Using independently built analyst models for the same companies, we find that across 108 directed pairs covering 65 companies, the median single-reference score is 0.33, 92.6% score below 0.70, and no same-vintage pair agrees on implied price within 10%. Point-tolerance grading can therefore penalize disagreement already present among professionals. We introduce GAUGE, a benchmark for evaluating agent-built valuation models against observed analyst practice rather than a single point answer. GAUGE uses 1,001 vendor-classified analyst workbooks and a 196-task evaluation set, with a three-layer observed-practice envelope, 56 auditable facets, eight validity gates, and deterministic structural checks. We validate the benchmark with a 55-participant known-groups study, company-grouped cross-fitting, and judge-stability audits. On the failure-aware score $φ_0$, senior analysts average 88.3, juniors 66.0, and finance students 43.2. Across 24 agents and 1,011 scored generations, the best agent scores 53.4, above the student mean but below every senior and most juniors. It passes 93% of mechanical facets and 78% of judgment facets, with a fleet-median gap of 26 points. Current agents are substantially stronger at model construction than valuation judgment. We release the methodology, a gated de-identified data tier, a controlled training split, a versioned 48-task evaluation core, and a withheld refresh pool.

</details>


### [130] [Algorithmic Separation between Constant-Depth and Logarithmic-Depth Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25200)
*Yunwei Ren,Zihao Wang,Jason D. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究深度网络在布尔函数逼近上的理论深度分离，证明了对数深度网络与常数深度网络之间存在算法层面的分离性，不涉及遥感、目标检测或轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文是纯理论深度学习研究，讨论布尔函数的逼近与学习效率，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构。讨论的是常数深度网络与对数深度网络的理论性质，未给出实际网络架构设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文使用布尔函数（超立方体上的均匀分布）进行理论分析，无遥感数据集或标准检测指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文为纯理论证明，无实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 该论文为理论工作，不涉及实际应用场景，无法直接用于遥感目标检测或模型轻量化任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。论文讨论的是布尔函数逼近的理论深度分离，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化的工程和算法需求（如小目标检测、旋转框、模型压缩、边缘部署等）无直接关联。

Abstract: Despite the empirical advantages of deep networks over shallow ones, theoretical depth separations largely concern approximation power, while algorithmic results are mostly limited to comparisons between two- and three-layer networks. In this work, we prove the first algorithmic separation between constant-depth and logarithmic-depth networks.
  Specifically, we identify a class of Boolean functions with hierarchically structured Fourier spectra that logarithmic-depth networks can learn efficiently using layerwise coordinate descent by reconstructing the spectra hierarchically and adaptively. We also exhibit a subclass for which every constant-depth, polynomial-width network with sufficiently regular activations and controlled spectral norms must incur constant $L^2$ approximation error under the uniform distribution over the hypercube.

</details>


### [131] [LLM as Forecasting Planner: Training-Free Text Conditioning for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24892)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dai Do,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的文本条件时间序列预测框架，利用LLM作为规划器、TSFM作为模拟器，通过MCTS搜索实现文本引导的时序预测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是文本条件时间序列预测（text-conditioned time-series forecasting），属于时序预测+大语言模型交叉领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：文本条件时间序列预测；应用场景：通用时序预测（如经济、气象等）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 框架由三部分组成：冻结的TSFM（Chronos/TimesFM）作为模拟器生成数值轨迹；Ranker LLM作为策略网络指导候选选择；Judge LLM作为价值函数评估完整轨迹。使用MCTS在预测时域上进行搜索。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文强调training-free框架，但未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。且框架依赖LLM+TSFM双模型+MCTS搜索，计算开销大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Context-is-Key、Time-MMD；评价指标：摘要未具体说明指标名称；实验设置：使用两个TSFM骨干（Chronos、TimesFM）和四个LLM进行实验。

Experiments: 实验表明LAFP框架在不同模型组合下均取得一致改进，支持序列搜索作为文本条件预测的有效无训练方法。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。框架依赖LLM推理，计算开销大，实时性差。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于时序预测+LLM方向，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无交集。

Abstract: Text-conditioned time-series forecasting predicts a series from both its numerical history and natural-language context, allowing forecasts to account for events and constraints that the past alone cannot reveal. This requires both reliable numerical forecasting and the ability to interpret contextual information. Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide strong numerical forecasts, while large language models (LLMs) can reason over text, but combining their strengths remains challenging because asking an LLM to generate or revise forecast values directly can distort the temporal structure captured by the TSFM. We instead formulate forecasting as a planning problem over TSFM-generated trajectories. The frozen TSFM acts as a simulator that proposes numerical continuations, while the LLM acts as a policy and value function that guides candidate selection and evaluates completed trajectories against the context. We instantiate this as \rc{} (\textbf{L}LM \textbf{A}s \textbf{F}orecasting \textbf{P}lanner), a training-free framework that bridges the modality gap without retraining either model, using Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) over the forecast horizon with a \emph{Ranker} LLM as policy and a \emph{Judge} LLM as value function. Experiments on Context-is-Key and Time-MMD across two TSFM backbones (Chronos and TimesFM) and four LLMs show that \rc{} delivers consistent improvements across model choices, supporting sequential search as an effective training-free approach to text-conditioned forecasting.

</details>


### [132] [Contextual Deconvolution for Variance-Stable Demand Sensing: Kernel-Modulated Operators in Promotional Retail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25664)
*Mohammad Forouhesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Contextual Deconvolution (CD)方法，用于需求预测中的去卷积分解，以降低安全库存和订单波动，属于供应链/零售需求预测领域，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文聚焦于供应链需求预测（demand forecasting）中的库存成本优化问题，属于运营管理/机器学习交叉领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或边缘部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：需求预测（demand forecasting）/ 需求感知（demand sensing）；应用场景：零售供应链库存管理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。数据集为M5和Favorita零售数据集。

Model architecture: Contextual Deconvolution (CD) 两阶段估计器：第一阶段为核调制带状算子（kernel-modulated banded operator），将促销驱动的瞬态冲击与平滑结构基线分离；第二阶段为层次化部分池化（hierarchical partial pooling），实现无单SKU训练的目录级部署。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法。文中提到的"catalog-scale deployment without per-SKU training"是一种统计层面的可扩展性设计，并非深度学习模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该论文不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：M5（30,490个SKU）和Favorita（2,845个商品）；评价指标：安全库存（safety stock）、持有成本（holding cost）、订单方差（order variance）、每SKU误差截面离散度（cross-sectional dispersion of per-SKU error）、误预测率（mis-forecast rate）、方差比（Variance Ratio）、总成本（total cost）。

Experiments: 在M5和Favorita数据集上进行了严格的out-of-sample评估，与11个基线方法对比。CD在每SKU误差截面离散度和误预测率上排名第一，但仅在持有成本超过缺货成本约20%时降低总成本，否则仅提供运营稳定性而非成本最小化。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 方法针对时间序列需求预测设计，不涉及图像/视觉任务；3. 未提供任何模型效率指标（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向基本无关，无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：文中"data-derived, not imposed"的设计哲学——即让模型根据数据自动决定何时使用复杂操作（文中是去卷积）、何时退化为恒等映射，这种自适应机制在轻量化设计中可类比为"根据输入复杂度动态调整计算量"的思路，但需要自行转化到遥感目标检测场景。

Abstract: Machine learning demand forecasts optimize statistical accuracy yet leave excess operational volatility that inflates safety stock and amplifies the Bullwhip effect. We introduce \textbf{Contextual Deconvolution} (CD), a two-stage estimator that reframes demand sensing as a convex decomposition: a kernel-modulated banded operator separates transient promotion-driven shocks from a smooth structural baseline, and hierarchical partial pooling enables catalog-scale deployment without per-SKU training. The operator is data-derived, not imposed---it reduces to the identity wherever the promotional response is impulsive (most of M5, all of Favorita) and contributes only where genuine multi-day carryover exists, so the gains rest on the structural decomposition itself. Evaluating strictly out-of-sample on 30,490 M5 SKUs and 2,845 Favorita items, with calendar-aware baselines given CD's identical future calendar, we anchor the contribution on a full inventory-cost accounting: CD lowers safety stock, holding cost, and order variance but under-provisions event spikes, reducing total cost only when holding costs exceed $\sim$20\% of stockout costs (95\% CI $[17\%,25\%]$); otherwise it is an operational-stability and inventory-capital layer, not an expected-cost minimizer. Its accuracy contribution is reliability rather than central tendency: across eleven baselines, CD attains the lowest cross-sectional dispersion of per-SKU error and mis-forecasts by more than 200\% on 0.8\% of SKUs versus 9.9--20.6\% for every baseline, ranking first on both in all four M5 draws. Because the Variance Ratio and std-based safety stock are minimized by any sufficiently smooth forecast, we treat them as diagnostics, not objectives. A supporting analysis shows the learned demand operators are non-normal, yet CD's compact parametric kernel matches their operational performance interpretably.

</details>


### [133] [Inverse RL Helps Align AI by Imitating Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24900)
*Michał Wiliński,Liu Leqi,Chirag Nagpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PARED方法，从演示数据中恢复隐式奖励函数，用于语言模型对齐，无需偏好标注。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型(LLM)的对齐(alignment)问题，涉及逆强化学习和奖励函数恢复，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言模型对齐（helpfulness, safety, instruction following）；应用场景：NLP/LLM；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: PARED方法：基于轻量判别器（lightweight discriminator）在响应级特征空间中区分演示样本与策略自身样本，恢复隐式奖励函数。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到"lightweight discriminator"，但这是用于奖励函数恢复的判别器，与模型压缩/轻量化无关。未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向NLP场景，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。实验涉及inference-time reranking和adversarial on-policy RL。

Experiments: 实验表明：恢复的奖励函数可改进基础策略（无需监督损失），在标准SFT后进一步优化可带来额外收益；可用于上下文对齐（contextual alignment）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何视觉任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化或边缘部署；4. 未讨论计算效率问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接启发是：PARED中"从演示中恢复隐式奖励"的思路如果类比到遥感领域，或许可思考如何从标注数据中隐式学习检测质量度量，但这非常牵强。

Abstract: Language model alignment aims to make model behavior reliably reflect desirable properties such as helpfulness, safety, and instruction following. Current approaches typically use supervised fine-tuning on demonstrations or reinforcement learning with rewards derived from verifiers or human feedback. These paradigms leave an important question underexplored: can demonstrations alone yield an implicit reward that can be inspected, reused, and optimized on-policy to align AI? Motivated by inverse reinforcement learning, we introduce Projected Alignment Reward Estimated from Demonstrations (PARED). PARED recovers the implicit reward underlying expert demonstrations as an explicit function over a small set of response-level features, learned by a lightweight discriminator that separates demonstrations from the policy's own samples in this feature space. Unlike a standard reward model, PARED requires no task-specific preference annotations: demonstrations provide the task-specific supervision, which can be augmented with AI feedback as additional dimensions of supervision. Through experiments involving inference-time reranking and adversarial on-policy RL, we show that the recovered reward improves a base policy without a supervised loss and yields further gains when optimized after standard supervised fine-tuning. Additionally, we demonstrate that PARED can be used for contextual alignment, in which a single policy can be tailored to the preferences of different audiences.

</details>


### [134] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization and Muon: Robustness under the Spectral Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26001)
*Wenzhi Zhong,Edward Milsom,Michael Murray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种矩阵感知的几何结构改进SAM优化器，在隐藏层参数上使用谱内扰动，结合Muon外更新，在ImageNet-1K上提升了ViT-S和ResNet-50的验证精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于通用视觉模型的优化器改进（SAM的几何结构），不涉及遥感场景、卫星数据、目标检测任务或任何轻量化/部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图像分类（ImageNet-1K）；场景：通用视觉；传感器类型：无；目标类别：无。摘要未说明遥感相关任务或场景。

Model architecture: ViT-Small/16 和 ResNet-50，均为通用视觉分类模型。摘要未说明遥感专用网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是优化器（SAM变体）的几何设计，不是模型压缩或加速方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等内容。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。论文仅在ImageNet-1K上报告验证精度，无推理速度或资源开销分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ImageNet-1K；指标：验证精度（validation accuracy）。无遥感数据集，无mAP、FPS等检测指标。

Experiments: 在ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50上，谱内扰动+Muon外更新的组合在验证精度上优于其他SAM变体。摘要未说明消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 仅在ImageNet-1K分类任务上验证，未在遥感场景或目标检测任务上测试；2. 未讨论计算开销增加对部署的影响；3. 未涉及轻量化或边缘部署场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果未来要将SAM类优化器用于遥感目标检测模型的训练，可参考其矩阵感知的谱扰动思想，但需注意：(1) 该方法增加了优化器计算复杂度，与轻量化目标可能冲突；(2) 需在遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016）上验证对检测精度的实际提升；(3) 需评估额外计算开销是否值得。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) aims to improve generalization by encouraging insensitivity to small, worst-case parameter perturbations. However, the notion of a "small" perturbation is inherently geometry-dependent: while existing SAM variants have explored a wide range of choices, a clear perspective on which geometries are most effective in practice remains elusive. Recent work on matrix-aware optimization, particularly the Muon optimizer, suggests that respecting the matrix structure of hidden-layer weights can lead to strong empirical performance. Motivated by this, we study matrix-aware geometry in both stages of SAM: we introduce a layerwise spectral inner perturbation for matrix-valued hidden-layer parameters and combine it with either AdamW/SGDW or Muon in the outer update. Across ImageNet-1K experiments on ViT-Small/16 and ResNet-50, we find that the combination of a spectral inner step with a Muon outer step performs consistently strongly, achieving the best validation accuracy on both models among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [135] [Reinformed Dreamer: An Asymmetric World Model Efficiently Trained through Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26040)
*Gaspard Lambrechts,Adrien Bolland,Daniel Ebi,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于潜在引导的非对称表示学习目标，改进Informed Dreamer算法，形成Reinformed Dreamer，用于强化学习中的表示学习与行为学习。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。论文聚焦于强化学习中的非对称表示学习（asymmetric reinforcement learning），属于通用机器学习/强化学习领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：强化学习中的表示学习与行为学习（model-based reinforcement learning）。应用场景：通用强化学习基准（benchmarks）。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Dreamer架构的改进，提出Reinformed Dreamer，引入潜在引导（latent guidance）的非对称表示学习目标。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 多个强化学习基准（several benchmarks）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 在多个基准上，Reinformed Dreamer相比Dreamer和之前的非对称方法表现出更一致的改进。摘要未提供具体数值结果。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。摘要未说明方法的计算开销或部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。该论文属于强化学习领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化的研究目标（检测精度、模型压缩、推理速度、星上部署）无直接关联。

Abstract: Much like humans benefit from guidance while learning, reinforcement learning algorithms may benefit from additional supervision beyond rewards. Leveraging additional information during training to learn better representations and behaviors has been the focus of asymmetric reinforcement learning. This learning paradigm has proven effective under partial observability when additional state information is available, but also under full observability when more refined state information is available. Focusing on model-based reinforcement learning, we study the effect of asymmetric learning on observation representations and on privileged information representations. First, we identify a limitation in the privileged information representations learned by an asymmetric model-based algorithm known as the Informed Dreamer. Then, we propose a novel asymmetric representation learning objective using latent guidance, resulting in a new algorithm called the Reinformed Dreamer. Experiments across several benchmarks show a more consistent improvement over Dreamer than previous asymmetric approaches.

</details>


### [136] [Stable FP4 Training via Transposition-Invariant Block Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24953)
*Mehdi Rahimifar,Amin Darabi,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Xing Huang,Yao Wang,Zhijun Tu,Yufei Cui,Yunke Peng,Hongliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于2D块FP4量化的低精度训练框架，解决大语言模型训练中FP4量化不稳定的问题，通过前向-反向缩放一致性实现高效训练。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦大语言模型(LLM)的低精度训练量化方法，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、在轨部署均无直接关系。虽然量化技术本身对轻量化有参考价值，但论文场景、任务、评估均面向NLP领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，仅量化方法本身有极有限的间接参考价值，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型低精度训练；应用场景：LLM训练效率优化；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明遥感相关场景。

Model architecture: 基于Transformer架构的密集LLM（最高7B参数）和30B MoE（混合专家）模型。关键设计：2D块FP4量化（2D block FP4 quantization）、MXFP8量化用于query和key投影的混合精度设计。

Lightweight method: 量化（Quantization）：提出2D块FP4量化，解决传统1D块量化中张量转置导致的缩放因子不一致问题；结合无截断缩放（truncation-free scaling）和随机舍入（stochastic rounding）控制量化误差；混合精度设计（MXFP8用于attention的Q/K投影）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文面向训练阶段而非推理部署，与星上/边缘部署场景不直接相关。

Datasets and metrics: 训练数据：最高100B tokens；评估指标：困惑度（perplexity）和下游任务准确率（downstream accuracy）；对比基准：BF16精度训练。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 在7B密集LLM和30B MoE模型上验证，训练最高100B tokens。FP4训练与BF16性能差距小于1.3%（困惑度和下游准确率），实现稳定端到端FP4训练。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对LLM训练场景，未涉及遥感目标检测模型；2. 仅验证到30B参数规模，未说明在更小模型上的表现；3. 未讨论推理阶段的量化部署效果；4. 未涉及遥感图像特有的多光谱/高光谱/旋转框检测等任务。

Ideas for my research: 1. 2D块量化的思想（transposition-invariant scaling）可借鉴到遥感检测模型的量化训练中，解决特征图转置导致的量化不一致问题；2. 混合精度设计思路（敏感部分用更高精度）可用于遥感检测模型的关键层（如小目标检测头）的量化策略；3. 随机舍入和无截断缩放方法可迁移到遥感模型的低比特量化训练中。

Abstract: Reducing training precision is a key lever for improving the e ciency of large language model (LLM) training, but pushing beyond FP8 to 4-bit oating point (FP4) remains challenging due to instability during optimization. We identify a fundamental source of this instability in existing microscaling approaches: scale inconsistency induced by tensor transposition. In conventional 1D block quantization, forward and backward passes assign di erent scaling factors to the same values after transposition, leading to biased and unstable gradient updates. To address this issue, we propose a low-precision training framework based on 2D block FP4 quantization, which enforces transposition-invariant scaling and preserves consistency between forward and backward computations. We further combine this with truncation-free scaling and stochastic rounding to control quantization error and maintain unbiased gradients. To handle the sensitivity of attention mechanisms, we adopt MXFP8 quantization for query and key projections, yielding a practical mixed-precision design. We evaluate our method on dense LLMs up to 7B parameters and a 30B Mixture-of-Experts model, trained on up to 100B tokens. Across all settings, our approach achieves stable end-to-end FP4 training and closely matches BF16 performance, with less than 1.3% degradation in perplexity and downstream accuracy. These results demonstrate that enforcing forwardbackward scaling consistency is su cient to enable practical FP4 training at scale, providing a simple and e ective pathway toward more e cient LLM training.

</details>


### [137] [Calibrated Partial Resets: Preventing Policy Collapse in Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24996)
*Luc McCutcheon,Evangelos Chatzaroulas,Saber Fallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Calibrated Partial Resets (CPR)优化器，通过按神经元效用缩放的部分重置来维持网络可塑性，避免持续学习中的策略崩溃。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关系。它研究的是持续学习（continual learning）场景下神经网络可塑性退化问题，属于优化器/训练策略方向，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测任务或任何模型压缩/加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 持续学习（continual supervised learning & reinforcement learning），非遥感场景。传感器类型、目标类别均不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络架构。方法CPR是优化器层面的策略，不限定模型结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。CPR是一种训练优化策略（神经元部分重置），与剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该方法不面向边缘/星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: SlipperyAnt（强化学习）、Continual MetaWorld、Continual MinAtar（持续学习基准）。不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在SlipperyAnt上，CPR是唯一在4亿训练步中避免策略崩溃的方法；在Continual MetaWorld和Continual MinAtar上优于先前的衰减和重置方法。消融实验揭示了可塑性与峰值性能之间的可调权衡。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 方法仅在持续学习/强化学习场景验证，未在视觉检测任务上评估；3. 未讨论计算开销或推理效率。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。CPR中"按神经元效用缩放调整"的思想，理论上可启发在轨模型持续适应（如模型在轨微调时保持可塑性），但与本课题核心（轻量化目标检测）关联度很低，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Neural networks are hindered by accumulating dormant neurons and loss of expressivity throughout training, particularly in non-stationary data settings, such as continual supervised and reinforcement learning. Recently, neuron resets have been used to maintain gradient flow and restore plasticity. However, full unit reinitialization often sacrifices peak performance and can destabilize training, leading to policy collapse.
  To preserve plasticity without destabilizing training, we propose Calibrated Partial Resets (CPR), an optimizer that periodically pulls low-utility neurons toward their initialization, with pull strength scaled by each neuron's utility. Unlike binary reset methods, partial resets avoid brittleness; unlike uniform decay, calibrated utility-scaling concentrates adjustment on the units that need it most.
  Among compared methods, only CPR avoids policy collapse over 400M training steps in SlipperyAnt, and it outperforms prior decay and reset-based methods on Continual MetaWorld and Continual MinAtar benchmarks. Ablations reveal a tunable trade-off between plasticity and peak performance, highlighting utility-scaled reinitialization as a promising direction for continual learning.

</details>


### [138] [Conformal Cascade: Distribution-Free Accuracy Guarantees for Multi-Tier LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25018)
*Yifan Dou,Shikan Fang,Shibo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Conformal Cascade（CC），一种基于共形预测的多层推理框架，通过预测集大小作为延迟规则，为LLM级联提供无分布假设的有限样本精度保证，无需模型训练仅需黑盒API访问。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的推理级联加速，属于自然语言处理/通用机器学习领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的剪枝/量化/蒸馏等经典压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM推理级联（多层级推理框架）；应用场景：通用NLP多选问答（科学、医学、常识、标准化考试）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 多层级级联架构：小模型处理简单查询，大模型处理困难查询；延迟规则基于共形预测集大小（而非置信度阈值）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及传统轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。本文的"轻量化"体现在推理级联（cascade）策略：用小模型处理大部分简单样本以减少推理成本，但这是系统级推理加速，非模型级压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。论文讨论的是LLM推理成本降低，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 18个多选问答基准（科学、医学、常识、标准化考试）；评价指标：精度保证（覆盖率）、级联成本（期望成本函数）；使用4个开源模型族构建两级级联。

Experiments: 在18个基准上，CC在大多数模型族-基准组合上优于最强校准调优启发式级联；在推理密集型基准上增益最大；在简单基准上大部分查询被提交给小模型且无精度损失。

Limitations: 1. 仅适用于多选问答任务，开放生成需额外答案聚类步骤（留作未来工作）；2. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；3. 未讨论任何视觉任务或遥感场景下的适用性；4. 未提供模型参数量、FLOPs等硬件部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值极低。唯一可抽象借鉴的思路是"级联推理"（cascade inference）概念——在遥感在轨场景中，简单场景用小模型快速推理，复杂场景切换到大模型或更精细处理。但本文方法依赖共形预测集大小作为延迟规则，且针对NLP多选任务设计，直接迁移到遥感目标检测（连续空间、旋转框、多类别）存在根本性障碍。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) cascades reduce inference cost by routing easy queries to a small model and deferring hard queries to a larger one. Production cascades govern this deferral through a confidence threshold, but LLM confidence scores are miscalibrated, the threshold must be tuned per model pair and per domain, and no setting yields a formal bound on cascade accuracy. We introduce \textbf{Conformal Cascade} (CC), a multi-tier inference framework that uses conformal prediction set size as the deferral rule: accept when the calibrated set collapses to a single answer, defer otherwise. The procedure delivers a distribution-free, finite-sample accuracy guarantee. By a per-tier union bound, the prediction set at the accepting tier covers the correct answer with probability at least $1 - Kα$ for any user-specified $α$; under a selection-preservation condition (consistent with, but not strictly implied by, our marginal coverage results), the bound tightens to $1 - α$. We further characterise expected cascade cost as an explicit function of $α$ and the calibration-set acceptance rate. Across 18 multiple-choice benchmarks spanning science, medicine, commonsense, and standardized exams, evaluated on two-tier cascades drawn from four open-weight model families, CC strictly improves over the strongest calibration-tuned heuristic cascade on the majority of family--benchmark pairs, with the largest gains on reasoning-heavy benchmarks where majority vote is unreliable; on easier benchmarks the cascade commits the vast majority of queries to the small model at no accuracy cost. Extension to open-ended generation requires an answer-clustering step that we leave for future work. The method requires no model training and only black-box API access.

</details>


### [139] [Lantern: Conflict-Aware Gradient Blending for Physics-Guided Diffusion Models in Calorimeter Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25060)
*Farzana Yasmin Ahmad,Vanamala Venkataswamy,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理引导的扩散模型Lantern，用于高能物理量热器簇射的蒙特卡洛模拟加速，通过Correlation Frobenius Distance (CFD)指标和两种物理感知辅助损失改进生成保真度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文面向高能物理（LHC量热器簇射模拟），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等任何模型压缩或加速方法。唯一的弱关联是使用了扩散模型（生成模型），但扩散模型在遥感目标检测中并非主流方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对扩散模型在遥感生成任务中的应用有额外兴趣，否则可跳过。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：高能物理量热器簇射的蒙特卡洛模拟加速（生成式代理模型）。应用场景：高亮度LHC物理模拟。传感器类型：量热器（calorimeter），非遥感传感器。目标类别：簇射（shower）物理特征，非遥感目标。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: Lantern：物理引导的扩散模型（physics-guided diffusion surrogate）。核心组件：GradBlend梯度混合策略（将辅助损失的梯度方向与去噪梯度方向结合）。辅助损失：方差稳定化体素残差损失（variance-stabilized voxel residual loss）+ 图拉普拉斯损失（graph Laplacian loss over detector geometry）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。扩散模型本身计算开销大，与轻量化方向背道而驰。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文面向地面高能物理模拟，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CaloChallenge Dataset 2（高能物理量热器数据集）。评价指标：FPD（Fréchet Physics Distance，物理特征空间的Fréchet距离）、CFD（Correlation Frobenius Distance，本文提出的相关性保真度指标）。对比方法：PCGrad、GradNorm、IMTL-G、ConFIG等多任务梯度平衡方法。

Experiments: 在CaloChallenge Dataset 2上，使用PCGrad/GradNorm/IMTL-G/ConFIG注入物理损失会使FPD比纯去噪膨胀2-100倍；而GradBlend（Lantern的核心策略）能引入相同信号而不退化，结合图拉普拉斯损失后Lantern同时改进了FPD和CFD。消融实验表明：体素残差损失与去噪梯度冲突，需要在训练末期进入纯去噪阶段；而图拉普拉斯损失无冲突，对调度策略不敏感。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 扩散模型推理速度慢，不适合实时/在轨场景。3. 仅针对高能物理特定领域，方法设计高度依赖量热器几何结构和计数统计特性。4. 未讨论模型大小、推理速度等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可能有启发：1. GradBlend梯度混合策略——在轻量化目标检测中，如果存在多个冲突的优化目标（如检测精度 vs 模型压缩损失），可借鉴梯度混合方法平衡不同目标。2. CFD指标的设计思路——在遥感目标检测中，可设计类似的相关性指标衡量检测结果的空间结构保真度（如小目标的空间分布一致性）。3. 物理感知损失的设计哲学——在遥感场景中，可引入遥感物理先验（如地物光谱特性、几何不变性）作为辅助损失引导轻量模型训练。但这些均为间接启发，非直接可复现方法。

Abstract: Monte Carlo simulation of calorimeter showers is a principal bottleneck for the High-Luminosity LHC, and diffusion models have emerged as fast, high-fidelity surrogates. Their denoising objective is purely statistical, however: a model can minimize it while placing the physics wrong. Existing physics-informed generative methods cannot close this gap, because they assume a closed-form law, a governing PDE residual or a hard per-sample constraint, that a shower does not supply: no per-sample PDE governs a stochastic cascade, and energy conservation fixes only one scalar per shower. Standard metrics ignore the correlation structure across calorimeter layers and voxels, comparing showers only in a physics feature space. We address both gaps. We introduce the Correlation Frobenius Distance (CFD), a single normalized score for correlation fidelity at layer-wise and voxel-wise scales. We then encode the soft per-sample structure available in a shower as two physics-aware auxiliary losses: a variance-stabilized voxel residual loss grounded in counting statistics, and a graph Laplacian loss over the detector geometry. We combine both with denoising through GradBlend, which anchors the step magnitude to the denoising gradient while letting the auxiliary steer its direction, yielding Lantern, a physics-guided diffusion surrogate. On CaloChallenge Dataset 2, injecting the physics losses through task-symmetric rules such as PCGrad, GradNorm, IMTL-G, and ConFIG inflates FPD by 2-100x relative to denoising alone, whereas GradBlend admits the same signal without regression and, with the Laplacian loss, Lantern improves both FPD and CFD. Our ablation on the auxiliary loss scheduler shows that the voxel residual loss, whose gradient conflicts with denoising, requires a terminal denoising-only phase to preserve shower fidelity, whereas the non-conflicting Laplacian loss is insensitive to the schedule.

</details>


### [140] [DRIFT: Direct-Recursive Intervention-Conditioned Forecasting of ICU Physiological Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25864)
*Weixin Liu,Juming Xiong,Congning Ni,Yanfan Zhu,Xingtao Lin,Bradley A. Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DRIFT混合时序预测框架，将直接预测与递归动作条件校正结合，用于ICU生命体征预测，在MIMIC-IV和eICU-CRD数据集上验证。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是ICU时序预测（生命体征、治疗动作条件预测），不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：时间序列预测（生命体征预测）；场景：ICU重症监护；传感器类型：无遥感传感器；目标类别：无目标检测类别。

Model architecture: DRIFT混合框架：直接预测模型（direct model）产生主预测 + 递归动作条件模型（recursive, action-conditioned model）提供约束校正。对比基线包括TFT-action（动作条件Temporal Fusion Transformer）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MIMIC-IV（6,046条入院记录）、eICU-CRD（8,345条入院记录）；指标：MAE（平均绝对误差），针对MAP（平均动脉压）在8h、24h、48h预测端点评估。

Experiments: DRIFT在MIMIC-IV上相比TFT-action的MAP MAE降低0.673%；在eICU-CRD上取得最低误差。治疗序列改变场景下，DRIFT在8h和24h的MAP误差低于TFT-action。鲁棒性实验表明优势在三种checkpoint选择规则下保持。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。整体精度提升幅度较小（0.673%），且仅适用于ICU时序预测场景。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于医疗时序预测领域，与遥感目标检测、轻量化、星上部署等核心研究方向无交集。

Abstract: Many time-series forecasts depend not only on prior observations but also on actions specified during the forecast period. In intensive care units (ICUs), future vital signs and laboratory values are influenced by treatments such as vasopressors. However, models that predict the full future sequence all at once make little use of these treatments, whereas autoregressive models can accumulate errors. We introduce DRIFT, a hybrid framework in which a direct model produces the primary forecast and a recursive, action-conditioned model contributes constrained corrections. We evaluate DRIFT on 6,046 admissions from MIMIC-IV and 8,345 admissions from eICU-CRD. Averaged across the 8-, 24-, and 48-hour forecast endpoints, DRIFT reduces mean absolute error for mean arterial pressure (MAP) by 0.673% relative to an action-conditioned Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT-action) on MIMIC-IV and achieves the lowest corresponding error among the compared models on eICU-CRD. Although the overall accuracy improvement is modest, a MIMIC-IV audit restricted to windows in which the supplied treatment sequence was altered showed that DRIFT achieved lower observed-target MAP error than TFT-action at 8 and 24 hours. Treatment-sequence alteration increased DRIFT's MAP error by 0.21-0.26 mmHg more than it increased TFT-action's error, with prediction changes occurring primarily after the supplied paths diverged. In a separate robustness experiment, the MAP advantage persisted under three shared checkpoint-selection rules emphasizing overall endpoint error, MAP error, or both equally.

</details>


### [141] [Score-Based Stabilization for Time-Dependent Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25119)
*Eshed Gal,Eldad Haber,Uri Ascher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于分数模型的偏微分方程数值模拟稳定化框架，通过学习得到的分数模型定义稳定化算子，对数值更新进行修正以保持物理一致性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关，属于计算数学/科学计算领域，研究的是偏微分方程数值模拟的稳定化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：偏微分方程数值模拟的稳定化；应用场景：科学计算（Advection, KdV, NLS, Burgers方程）；传感器类型：无；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构。使用score model（分数模型）定义稳定化算子，该算子将数值迭代向可容许状态流形收缩。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数值实验在Advection、Korteweg-de Vries (KdV)、Nonlinear Schrodinger (NLS)、Burgers'方程上进行。评价指标：鲁棒性、非物理不稳定性抑制、定性动力学保持。

Experiments: 在四个经典PDE方程上验证了方法的鲁棒性提升、非物理不稳定性抑制和定性动力学保持能力。摘要未提供与基线方法的定量对比。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化方法。属于科学计算领域，研究范式和应用场景差异极大。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于偏微分方程数值模拟方向，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: We propose a score-based stabilization framework for numerical simulation of partial differential equations, in which a learned score model defines a stabilization operator applied to provisional numerical updates. This operator augments standard time-stepping schemes by enforcing structure and physical consistency through a correction that drives iterates toward the manifold of admissible states. We show that the stabilization operator acts as a contraction toward this manifold, yielding a correction mechanism with basin-conditional stability. Numerical experiments on Advection, Korteweg-de Vries (KdV), Nonlinear Schrodinger (NLS), and Burgers' equations demonstrate improved robustness, suppression of nonphysical instabilities, and preservation of qualitative dynamics.

</details>


### [142] [Semantic Space Search Trajectory Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25122)
*Julian Agudelo,Alberto Tonda,Gabriela Ochoa,Vincent Guigue,Cristina Manfredotti,Evelyne Lutton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在语义空间中构建搜索轨迹网络(STN)的方法，用于可视化和比较不同机器学习算法的学习动态，并研究神经网络泛化机制。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于优化算法可视化和学习动态分析的工具方法，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：优化算法行为可视化与学习动态分析；应用场景：通用机器学习算法分析；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Search Trajectory Networks (STNs) — 基于图的工具，用于可视化和表征优化算法行为。本文在语义空间（模型在固定样本集上的预测空间）中构建STN，通过凝聚聚类和归一化Hamming距离对语义向量进行离散化和聚合。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。实验涉及分类和回归任务，以及标签随机化实验（Zhang et al., 2017）。

Experiments: 在分类和回归任务上使用不同机器学习算法验证方法有效性；通过对比标准训练与标签随机化训练，发现真实标签训练产生的STN图更密集、更高效、更集中。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关；2. 方法本身是分析工具而非检测或压缩模型；3. 未讨论在轨部署或边缘计算场景的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。STN作为一种学习动态可视化工具，理论上可用于分析遥感目标检测模型在训练过程中的行为变化，但本文未涉及遥感场景或轻量化任务，直接借鉴意义不大。

Abstract: Search Trajectory Networks (STNs) are a graph-based tool for visualizing and characterizing the behavior of optimization algorithms. STNs' reliance on discretization of the search space has largely confined them to low-dimensional or combinatorial settings. We introduce a methodology for constructing STNs in semantic spaces, defined as the space of a model's predictions on a fixed sample set. Our approach discretizes semantic vectors and aggregates them into network nodes via agglomerative clustering with complete linkage under a normalized Hamming distance. Since any predictor can be summarized by its semantic vector, this method enables comparison of learning dynamics across otherwise incomparable algorithm families. We apply semantic space STNs to classification and regression tasks solved using different machine learning algorithms, recovering known qualitative differences between them. Additionally, we use semantic space STNs to study neural network generalization by contrasting standard training with the label randomization regime of Zhang et al. (2017). The resulting STNs exhibit consistent structural differences, training on real labels produces denser, more efficient and more centralized graphs than training on shuffled labels. Together, our results show that semantic space STNs capture functional training dynamics arising from the interaction between learning algorithms and data, providing a tool for analyzing and comparing learning dynamics across machine learning models and training regimes.

</details>


### [143] [Endpoint Replay: Compressing the Recency Buffer in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25123)
*Parham Mohammad Panahi,Armin Ashrafi,Haoyu Du,Andrew Patterson,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于n步序列端点压缩的经验回放方法，在深度强化学习中用更小的存储量匹配大缓冲区的性能。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于深度强化学习（DRL）领域的经验回放机制优化，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化模型部署完全无关。不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：深度强化学习中的经验回放压缩；应用场景：Pinball环境和Atari 2600游戏；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的神经网络架构。核心方法是对经验回放缓冲区进行压缩，通过存储n步序列的端点来减少存储量。

Lightweight method: 提出了一种经验回放压缩方法（非模型轻量化），通过链式n步序列的端点选择来减少缓冲区存储量，存储量降低一个数量级。但这与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型轻量化技术无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 实验环境：Pinball环境和Atari 2600基准测试。评价指标：与传统大缓冲区的性能匹配程度。不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 通过实验证明该方法避免了朴素压缩策略的系统偏差，在Pinball和Atari 2600上匹配了传统大缓冲区的性能。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化技术或遥感相关方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于深度强化学习中的经验回放机制优化，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型轻量化技术，与课题方向无交集。

Abstract: Experience replay remains one of the most practical and useful algorithmic tools in the deep reinforcement learning (DRL) toolbox. Aside from the limited success of prioritized replay and specialized approaches for large asynchronous systems, most DRL algorithms make use of a large, uniformly sampled recency buffer---even the size, one million, remains unchanged. Could we store less data, reduce redundancy, or more effectively chain experience together to speed up value propagation and still retain the performance of large buffers? In this paper, we investigate a simple compression approach that stores representative transitions derived from the end-points of a chain of connected $n$-step sequences. By curating these end-points in a smaller recency buffer, our method maintains an effective memory horizon comparable to a standard large buffer while requiring an order of magnitude less storage. Through empirical evaluation, we demonstrate that this approach prevents the systematic bias inherent in naive compression strategies and matches the performance of traditional large buffers in the Pinball environment and the Atari 2600 benchmark.

</details>


### [144] [Interpretable GOHR Agents via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25132)
*Shiwei Tan,Yusong Zhao,Weiyi Qin,Wentian Wang,Jacob Feldman,Lazaros K. Gallos,Paul B. Kantor,Vladimir Menkov,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）对Transformer智能体在Hidden Rules游戏中的内部表征进行可解释性分析，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是强化学习智能体的可解释性分析，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务，也不涉及任何模型压缩或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：Transformer智能体的可解释性分析；应用场景：Game of Hidden Rules（GOHR）游戏环境；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：游戏中的形状（shape）和桶（bucket）概念。

Model architecture: tokenized autoregressive Transformer agent；稀疏自编码器（Sparse Autoencoders, SAEs）用于分析决策token嵌入。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Game of Hidden Rules (GOHR) 游戏环境中的两规则任务；评价指标：SAE维度对概念的选择性覆盖度。

Experiments: 在GOHR两规则任务上训练策略，用SAE分析决策token嵌入，发现SAE维度能恢复规则结构，且单个维度对应可解释策略（如试探一个规则假设并在负反馈后切换）。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化完全无关。不涉及任何视觉检测任务、遥感数据、模型效率优化或边缘部署。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于AI可解释性方向，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: A central challenge in interpreting learned decision-making systems is to determine whether their internal representations contain concepts that help explain their behavior. We report interpretability experiments for a tokenized autoregressive Transformer agent in the Game of Hidden Rules (GOHR). We focus on a compact two-rule task in which both hidden rules map object shapes to target buckets, but with different permutations. The policy is trained on episodes sampled from these two hidden rules and then evaluated with fixed weights. It is never given a rule label and does not use an explicit rule classifier; any rule information must be inferred implicitly from interaction history. In this setting, the correct rule is not identifiable before the agent tries an informative move and observes accept/reject feedback. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) trained on the agent's decision-token embeddings recover this structure. When held-out decisions are labeled by simple concepts such as the chosen shape or bucket, SAE dimensions that are highly selective for a concept cover most decisions where that concept is present. Individual SAE dimensions also correspond to interpretable strategies such as probing one rule hypothesis and switching after negative feedback.

</details>


### [145] [Physics-Informed CNN-LSTM for Street-Scale Urban Flood Prediction: Reconciling Aggregate Accuracy and Street-Level Plausibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25148)
*Luc DCosta,Yidi Wang,Jonathan L. Goodall,Rohan Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息约束的CNN-LSTM训练框架，通过可微惩罚项（重力、连续性、地形感知虚警）约束城市洪水预测的物理合理性，在Norfolk洪水数据集上验证了物理约束与像素级精度之间的权衡。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是城市洪水预测（urban flood prediction），属于水文/城市计算领域，不涉及遥感卫星、卫星图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然使用了CNN-LSTM和空间网格数据，但任务本质是回归预测而非目标检测，场景是地面水文而非遥感/卫星在轨。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。仅当对物理信息约束损失的设计思想有泛化兴趣时可略读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：城市洪水深度时空预测（回归任务）；应用场景：城市水文、交通路由；传感器类型：未说明（可能为地面传感器或水文模型输出）；目标类别：无目标检测，预测的是洪水深度标量场。

Model architecture: CNN-LSTM混合模型，在128×128空间网格上以15分钟间隔预测洪水深度。具体CNN和LSTM的层数、通道数等细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。模型运行在128×128网格上，计算量可能不大，但未讨论边缘/星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Norfolk, Virginia洪水数据集，包含两次风暴事件（2017年8月和2022年9月），共300个样本。评价指标：重力违规（gravity violations，量级1e-6）、街道级召回率（street recall 0.77±0.09 vs 0.44±0.10）、平均绝对误差（MAE）、街道级F1分数。使用重复随机划分和留一风暴测试评估鲁棒性。

Experiments: 物理约束模型达到近零重力违规（1e-6量级），街道召回率0.77±0.09（无约束基线0.44±0.10）；均匀虚警变体MAE降低16%但街道召回率降至0.25；TWI调制虚警惩罚在约束变体中取得最低MAE和最高街道级F1，街道召回率恢复60%。留一风暴测试中优势翻倍。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 数据集仅300样本，规模小；3. 仅覆盖两次风暴事件，泛化性有限；4. 未讨论模型效率或部署问题；5. 物理约束方法虽新颖但针对水文物理规律，不适用于目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与遥感在轨目标检测无关，但以下思路可能有间接启发：1. 物理信息约束损失的设计思想——在遥感目标检测中可考虑引入地理先验（如地物空间分布规律、传感器成像物理模型）作为正则化项；2. 地形感知（TWI）调制虚警惩罚的思路可类比于遥感中基于场景先验（如水域/道路掩膜）调整检测阈值；3. 论文揭示了像素级精度与应用级物理合理性之间的权衡，类似地，在轨检测中可能存在精度与推理速度/能耗之间的权衡，需要针对性设计。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Deep learning surrogate models trained with mean-squared-error loss produce statistically accurate but physically unconstrained flood predictions: water may flow uphill, appear spontaneously, or smooth over street-level corridors. We develop a physics-informed training framework for CNN-LSTM models that predict urban flood depths at 15 min intervals over a 128x128 spatial grid. Three differentiable penalty terms are embedded into the loss: (i) a gravity loss penalizing depth increases against the water-surface-elevation gradient, (ii) a continuity loss enforcing local mass conservation with rainfall-adaptive thresholds, and (iii) a topography-aware false-alarm penalty modulated by the topographic wetness index (TWI). We evaluate on the Norfolk, Virginia flood dataset spanning two storm events (August 2017 and September 2022, 300 samples), with all variants trained on identical splits and robustness assessed over repeated random splits and leave-one-storm-out tests. A road-proximal evaluation restricted to a TWI-derived street mask quantifies street-level skill. The physics-constrained model achieves near-zero gravity violations (order 1e-6) and the highest street-channel recall (0.77 +/- 0.09 vs 0.44 +/- 0.10 for the unconstrained baseline), the capability most relevant to traffic routing, and its advantage more than doubles on a held-out storm; a uniform false-alarm variant attains 16% lower mean absolute error but suppresses street recall to 0.25. The TWI-modulated penalty reconciles this trade-off: it improves on the uniform variant on every metric, recovering 60% higher street recall at the lowest MAE among constrained variants and the best street-level F1. These results expose a fundamental tension between aggregate pixel-level error and application-specific physical plausibility, and show that terrain-aware loss modulation offers a principled resolution.

</details>


### [146] [Accurate structural modeling of chemically diverse molecular interfaces with Vilya-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25156)
*Vilya Research,:,Pascal Sturmfels,Naozumi Hiranuma,Milad Salem,Benjamin D. Sellers,Stephen Rettie,CJ San Felipe,Chase A. P. Wood,Jeffrey K. Holden,Adam P. Moyer,Patrick J. Salveson,Ivan Anishchanka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Vilya-2是一个基于扩散Transformer的蛋白质-多肽相互作用结构预测模型，用于药物发现，与遥感卫星目标检测完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全属于计算生物学/药物发现领域，研究蛋白质-多肽相互作用的结构预测，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等方向没有任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：蛋白质-多肽复合物结构预测、小分子对接、大环化合物构象建模。应用场景：药物发现、de novo多肽设计。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：蛋白质、多肽、小分子、大环化合物。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 扩散Transformer（diffusion transformer），全原子表示（all-atom representation），支持不同分子类型间的迁移学习。与遥感目标检测模型架构无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。该模型面向高精度结构预测而非部署效率优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。该模型不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未明确说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：backbone RMSD（亚2Å），肽界面恢复率（59.1%）。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）无关。

Experiments: 与共折叠模型（co-folding model）对比，在肽界面恢复率上显著领先；在小分子对接任务上达到SOTA；泛化到训练中未见的新型蛋白质-小分子复合物和更大尺寸的大环化合物。与遥感目标检测实验无关。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何可借鉴信息。该论文的局限性在于其应用范围限定在计算生物学领域。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Structure-prediction networks built on co-evolutionary statistics have transformed protein-based drug discovery, yet their accuracy does not extend to peptide therapeutics--an increasingly important modality defined by non-canonical residues, macrocyclization, and complex topologies. We introduce Vilya-2, a diffusion transformer that extends the all-atom representation of Vilya-1 from modeling individual molecules to modeling their interactions with protein targets. This all-atom representation enables transfer learning between different molecular types, and delivers highly accurate structural modeling of peptides across sizes, classes, and compositions bound to therapeutically relevant targets. By generating diverse structural ensembles and ranking them with calibrated confidence, Vilya-2 recovers 59.1% of peptide interfaces to sub-2 Å backbone RMSD, far exceeding the performance of a representative co-folding model even when that model is given the bound receptor as a template. In addition, Vilya-2 is state-of-the-art at small-molecule docking, and generalizes to novel protein-small molecule complexes unlike those seen in training. It also generalizes to modeling molecular conformations of diverse macrocycles and disulfide-stapled miniproteins several-fold larger than any molecule seen in training. Finally, Vilya-2 can be used as a foundation model, and fine-tuned to enrich for active compounds in hit-to-lead campaigns. By unifying predictive accuracy with broad generalizability across chemical space, Vilya-2 is the structure-prediction oracle that de novo peptide design pipelines require--establishing the all-atom approach as a general foundation for the design and evaluation of de novo peptide therapeutics.

</details>


### [147] [CondPSE: A Polynomial-Filtered Structural Encoder with Conditional Modulation for Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25169)
*Woohyun Lee,Hogun Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CondPSE，一种基于可学习多项式图滤波器组和FiLM调制的图位置/结构编码器，在合成图结构判别任务上显著超越GPSE，但在真实分子性质预测任务上提升有限。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究图神经网络的结构编码（Positional/Structural Encodings），属于图表示学习领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无任何直接或间接关联。不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图结构判别（graph isomorphism discrimination）和分子性质预测（molecular property prediction）。应用场景：合成图基准（CSL, EXP）和分子图（ZINC）。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: CondPSE：可学习多项式图滤波器组（learnable polynomial graph filter bank）+ FiLM-style条件调制（cross-filter, local message-passing, graph-level signals）。预训练后冻结作为下游图模型的输入编码。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。该工作不面向边缘或星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 合成图基准：CSL（circular skip link graphs）、EXP（expander graphs）。分子性质预测：ZINC数据集。指标：分类准确率（CSL, EXP）和分子性质预测性能（具体指标摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 合成任务：CondPSE在CSL上准确率从GPSE的42.9%提升至97.3%，在EXP上从68.3%提升至99.9%。消融实验表明多项式滤波器组贡献最大。分子任务：CondPSE与GPSE性能相当，未显著超越。ZINC骨干网络扫描显示两者无一致排序。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。2. 强合成图判别能力未转化为下游分子性质预测优势，作者讨论了预训练目标与下游标签可能不匹配的问题。3. 摘要未说明模型参数量、推理速度等实际部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题基本无借鉴价值。唯一可抽象思考的点：图结构编码中的滤波器组设计思路（多项式滤波器+条件调制）若未来遥感检测中涉及图结构建模（如场景图、关系推理）可能有启发，但与本课题核心方向（检测模型轻量化、星上部署）距离很远。

Abstract: Message-passing graph neural networks are bounded by the 1-WL test and can miss topological structure that distinguishes non-isomorphic graphs. Positional and structural encodings (PSE) inject such topology-derived signals, and learned PSE encoders such as GPSE pretrain a single encoder to produce these signals from random node probes, which can then be frozen and reused as inputs across downstream graph models. We present CondPSE, a learned PSE encoder that applies a learnable polynomial graph filter bank to standard Gaussian node probes and refines the resulting structural-response branches through FiLM-style modulation conditioned on cross-filter, local message-passing, and graph-level signals. CondPSE is pretrained to reconstruct node-level positional/structural targets and graph-level invariants, and is then frozen for use as a downstream input encoding. On synthetic structural-discrimination benchmarks, CondPSE separates graph structures that 1-WL-bounded message passing cannot: it raises CSL accuracy from 42.9% to 97.3% and EXP accuracy from 68.3% to 99.9% relative to GPSE, and ablations show that the polynomial filter bank accounts for most of this gain. On real molecular property prediction, the picture is more limited. With a hybrid local-message-passing/global-attention backbone, CondPSE performs comparably to GPSE without surpassing it, and a ZINC backbone sweep shows no consistent ordering between the two encoders. We report these results and discuss why strong synthetic structural discrimination does not, on its own, yield a downstream advantage for frozen learned PSE encoders, including the role of downstream integration and possible mismatch between structural pretraining targets and molecular property labels.

</details>


### [148] [Rethinking CD: A Reproducibility Study and Extension on the Ineffectiveness of Contrastive Decoding at Mitigating Object Hallucinations in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25196)
*Arnav Bendre,Guneesh Gupta,Kavish Grover,Chayan Aggarwal,Shreyansh Modi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展了关于对比解码(CD)在多模态大语言模型(MLLM)中无法真正缓解目标幻觉的结论，证明CD带来的性能提升是虚假的。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦多模态大语言模型(MLLM)中的目标幻觉问题，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测模型或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态大语言模型(MLLM)中的目标幻觉缓解与评估；应用场景：通用视觉-语言理解；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及遥感目标。

Model architecture: 使用LLaVA和Qwen作为基础MLLM模型，对比解码(CD)策略作为训练-free的幻觉缓解方法，包含专家模型(amateur model)和业余模型(expert model)的双模型对比解码架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：POPE、MME、CHAIR；评价指标：目标幻觉相关的评估指标（如CHAIR中的物体幻觉率）；模型：LLaVA、Qwen。

Experiments: 实验验证了对比解码(CD)在POPE、MME、CHAIR上的性能提升是虚假的(spurious)，CD导致判别式数据集上的单向输出分布偏移，自适应合理性约束(APC)将采样退化为贪心搜索，幻觉信号在专家和业余模型各层中的传播分析。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/地球观测领域；2. 不涉及任何目标检测模型；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；4. 研究的是MLLM幻觉问题，与遥感在轨目标检测的研究范式差异极大。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究的是MLLM中的对比解码策略评估，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务目标、技术路线和应用场景上均无交集。

Abstract: Contrastive decoding (CD) has been proposed as a training-free strategy for mitigating object hallucinations in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), with reported gains on benchmarks such as POPE. However, recent work has questioned whether these gains reflect genuine improvements in visual grounding. In this study, we reproduce and extend the findings of "The Mirage of Performance Gains: Why Contrastive Decoding Fails to Mitigate Object Hallucinations in MLLMs." Specifically, we test the claim that CD induces a unidirectional output distribution shift in discriminative datasets and examine its generalizability across datasets. We also verify that the adaptive plausibility constraint (APC) reduces sampling to greedy search on both discriminative and generative benchmarks. Beyond reproduction, we rigorously study the effects of CD across generative and discriminative datasets. We conduct several experiments that provide additional insights: we analyze the logit distributions induced by different CD strategies on generative datasets, propose a proxy method and compare its performance against CD techniques, and investigate how hallucination signals propagate through each layer of the expert and amateur models. Experimental results across MME, POPE, and CHAIR using LLaVA and Qwen validate the original claims and show that the apparent improvements from CD are often spurious and do not consistently translate into stronger visual grounding for reducing hallucinations. These findings challenge the effectiveness of current contrastive decoding strategies and motivate the development of more reliable approaches for mitigating hallucinations in MLLMs.

</details>


### [149] [A Unified Algorithmic Framework for Hybrid Reinforcement Learning in Tabular MDPs with Shifted Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25207)
*Zheshun Wu,Renjie Zheng,Jinhang Zuo,Zenglin Xu,Fang Kong*

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关，属于纯强化学习理论工作，研究的是表格型MDP中的混合学习（在线+离线）问题，不涉及任何视觉感知或遥感应用场景。

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为混合强化学习（hybrid RL），场景为表格型马尔可夫决策过程，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测等任何视觉任务。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未提及具体数据集名称和评价指标，仅提到"extensive experiments"验证理论发现。
