Limitations: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接或间接的借鉴。论文聚焦于强化学习的理论最优性证明，不涉及视觉感知、模型压缩或边缘部署等实际问题。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于纯强化学习理论方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题设定、技术路线和应用场景上均无交集。

Abstract: This paper investigates a hybrid reinforcement learning setting in tabular Markov Decision Processes (MDPs), where an agent aims to learn an optimal policy by combining online interactions with a target environment and offline data from a source environment. A central challenge is that offline data may be collected from outdated environments with shifted transition dynamics, making naive integration of historical data ineffective. To address this, we propose a unified algorithmic framework featuring two algorithms: MIN-UCB-VI for regret minimization and MAX-LCB-VI for best policy identification. Both algorithms leverage fine-grained bias information to more effectively exploit offline data under general transition shifts. We provide theoretical guarantees for our framework, including both instance-dependent and independent upper bounds on regret and sub-optimality gap. Furthermore, we establish matching lower bounds to demonstrate the optimality of our approach and validate our theoretical findings through extensive experiments.

</details>


### [150] [Neurai-VN Benchmark: Standardized Machine Learning Models for Multimodal Digital Phenotyping in Mental Health Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25232)
*Quoc-Cuong Pham,Hoang-Thuy-Duong Vu,Thi-Thanh-Huong Ha,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个基于智能手机和可穿戴设备的多模态数字表型数据集（Neurai-VN），用于心理健康监测的二元分类任务，并提供了可复现的基准结果。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于数字表型（Digital Phenotyping）和心理健康监测，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：心理健康监测的二元分类（Healthy Control vs. Depression, Healthy Control vs. Clinical, Healthy Control vs. Anxiety, Depression vs. Anxiety）；应用场景：数字表型（Digital Phenotyping）；传感器类型：智能手机和可穿戴设备；目标类别：心理健康状态分类，非遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构细节，仅提及线性模型（linear）、基于树的模型（tree-based）和神经网络基线模型（neural baseline models）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Neurai-VN dataset（自建多模态数据集，100名越南成年人，两周采集）；评价指标：subject-level F1 score（宏平均F1）；实验设置：subject-wise cross-validation（5折交叉验证）。

Experiments: 四组二元分类任务在5折交叉验证下的平均subject-level F1分数：Healthy Control vs. Depression 和 Healthy Control vs. Clinical 达到0.71，Healthy Control vs. Anxiety 达到0.69，Depression vs. Anxiety 达到0.56。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方面的局限性信息。摘要本身也未说明该工作的局限性。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在任务场景、数据模态、方法体系上均无交集，无法提供可借鉴的技术思路或可复现方向。

Abstract: Digital phenotyping (DP) using smartphones and wearable devices has shown considerable potential for mental health monitoring. However, progress remains difficult to evaluate due to heterogeneous datasets, inconsistent preprocessing pipelines. In this study, we present a reproducible benchmark built upon the Neurai-VN dataset, a high-resolution, multimodal dataset comprising passive sensing and active assessment from wearable and smartphone devices, collected from 100 Vietnamese adults over two weeks. The benchmark defines four clinically relevant binary classification tasks evaluated using standardized subject-wise cross-validation. Representative linear, tree-based, and neural baseline models are evaluated across predefined feature configurations. Mean subject-level F1 scores across five cross-validation folds reached 0.71 for Healthy Control vs. Depression and Healthy Control vs. Clinical, while Healthy Control vs. Anxiety and Depression vs. Anxiety achieved 0.69 and 0.56, respectively. These benchmark results provide reproducible baselines for future research on multimodal DP for mental health classification tasks.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond Single-Episode Optimization: Sliding-Window Aware Generative Auto-Bidding for Long-Term Advertising Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25233)
*Binglin Wu,Chuan Yue,Yingyi Zhang,Xianneng Li,Ruyue Deng,Weiru Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是在线广告自动竞价系统（auto-bidding），属于计算广告/推荐系统领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关。不涉及任何目标检测任务，也不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等方向。

Model architecture: SWAG-Bid分层框架，包含：Masked Trajectory Model（掩码轨迹模型）用于市场预测和候选计划生成；Multi-Window Model Predictive Control Sampling (MWMS) 用于多窗口评分；Per-Step Gated Adaptive Layer Normalization (PSG-AdaLN) 用于状态自适应门控；Return-to-Go和Cost-to-Go通道携带预算和约束信息。与遥感目标检测模型架构无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AuctionNet-Sparse（广告竞价数据集）及AliExpress在线A/B测试。评价指标：约束满足（constraint satisfaction）和价值获取（value acquisition）。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10、SSDD、SARDet等）完全无关。

Experiments: 在AuctionNet-Sparse和AliExpress在线A/B测试上进行了实验，表明SWAG-Bid在滑动窗口评估下实现了有竞争力的约束满足和价值获取。摘要未提供与其他方法的详细对比或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化信息。该论文面向广告竞价场景，其滑动窗口建模思路虽有一定方法论价值，但与目标检测、遥感图像处理、模型压缩加速等核心问题无交集。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。其滑动窗口规划思想（MWMS）和分层框架设计在方法论层面有一定参考价值，但无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测轻量化任务中。

Abstract: Auto-bidding systems optimize bids to maximize value under efficiency constraints such as Cost-Per-Action (CPA). Existing methods treat each day as an independent episode. However, many advertisers produce value so sparsely that per-day efficiency ratios become statistically unreliable, undermining advertiser retention. Platforms therefore evaluate window-level efficiency over sliding windows of $W{=}7$ days, ensuring fair evaluation and long-term advertising effectiveness. This creates cross-episode coupling: each day's bidding decisions affect up to $W$ overlapping windows, so setting daily targets requires anticipating future market conditions. We propose SWAG-Bid (Sliding-Window Aware Generative Auto-Bidding), a hierarchical framework decomposing this challenge into episode-level planning and step-level execution. The planner uses a Masked Trajectory Model to forecast markets and generate candidate plans, scored across all overlapping windows by Multi-Window Model Predictive Control Sampling (MWMS) with exponential confidence decay. The controller adjusts reliance on this guidance through a state-adaptive gate, Per-Step Gated Adaptive Layer Normalization (PSG-AdaLN), complemented by Return-to-Go and Cost-to-Go channels carrying budget and constraint information. Experiments on AuctionNet-Sparse and online A/B tests on AliExpress show that SWAG-Bid achieves competitive constraint satisfaction and value acquisition under sliding-window evaluation.

</details>


### [152] [Bridging Compute- and Data-Optimal Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25271)
*Tian Qin,Kimia Hamidieh,David Alvarez-Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Compute-Data (CD) scaling laws，统一了计算最优缩放和数据最优缩放，通过token有效性函数η量化多轮重复/改写生成数据的价值，发现η随模型大小、token-per-parameter比率和衍生数据量动态变化，且存在饱和效应。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，而是关于大语言模型预训练的缩放定律（scaling laws）理论研究，属于泛机器学习/深度学习基础理论方向，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对scaling law理论有广泛兴趣，否则对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题帮助极小。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：预训练数据缩放定律理论研究；应用场景：大语言模型（LLM）预训练；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。实验使用14M到600M参数规模的模型，基于Dolma-3语料库。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是数据缩放策略（multi-epoch repetition, paraphrasing）而非模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Dolma-3语料库；评价指标：token effectiveness function η（token有效性函数），衡量衍生token相对于新鲜token的价值。

Experiments: 实验覆盖14M到600M参数规模的模型，针对两种数据扩展策略（多轮重复、改写）拟合η函数。发现η并非恒定，而是依赖于模型大小、tokens-per-parameter比率和衍生数据量，且存在饱和效应。将训练划分为三个操作区域：compute-bound、data-bound和model-bound。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文本身局限：实验规模仅到600M参数，未验证更大模型；仅研究了两种数据扩展策略。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但可抽象借鉴的思路：论文提出的"token有效性函数η"概念——即衍生数据相对于原始数据的边际价值递减——在遥感数据增强场景中可能有类比意义。例如，遥感目标检测中通过旋转、翻转、马赛克等数据增强生成的训练样本，其边际收益是否也存在饱和效应？但这是非常间接的启发，且论文本身不涉及遥感或检测任务。

Abstract: Classical compute-optimal scaling laws assume an unbounded supply of fresh pretraining data, yet pretraining is increasingly entering a regime in which compute grows faster than the availability of high-quality data. We propose Compute-Data (CD) scaling laws, a unified framework that bridges compute-optimal scaling, where data scales freely with compute, and data-optimal scaling, where the corpus is fixed while compute can grow without bound. CD scaling extends classical scaling laws by introducing a token-effectiveness function, $η$, which quantifies the value of a derived token-produced, for example, through multi-epoch repetition or paraphrasing-relative to a fresh token, ranging from a perfect substitute to having no value. We fit $η$ for two data-expansion strategies, multi-epoch repetition and paraphrasing, across model sizes from 14M to 600M parameters using the Dolma-3 corpus. We find that token effectiveness is far from constant: it depends jointly on model size, the tokens-per-parameter ratio, and the amount of derived data, and it saturates as the corpus is expanded. The functional form of $η$ implies diminishing returns when substituting compute for data as either model size or data availability increases. It also partitions training into three operational regimes---compute-bound, data-bound, and model-bound---and shows that classical compute-optimal allocation is suboptimal across most practically relevant settings.

</details>


### [153] [HeAD-CP: Heterophily-Aware Diffused Conformal Prediction Sets for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25273)
*Phan Binh Nguyen Lam,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HeAD-CP，一种基于节点局部同质性估计的自适应图扩散共形预测方法，解决DAPS在异配图上预测集过大的问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究图上的共形预测（conformal prediction）与不确定性量化，属于图神经网络（GNN）和统计学习领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无任何直接或间接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图节点分类的不确定性量化（共形预测）。场景：图结构数据（引文网络等）。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于GNN softmax输出的无标签局部同质性估计，设计三种节点级扩散变体：signed-γ、edge-compatibility、DAPS-baseline-with-correction。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 10个图基准数据集（含Texas、CiteSeer、PubMed等）。指标：平均预测集大小（mean prediction-set size）、边际覆盖保证（marginal coverage guarantee）、配对Wilcoxon检验p值。

Experiments: HeAD-CP家族在所有数据集上预测集大小≤plain APS；DAPS在6/10数据集上超过APS。后验oracle在8/10数据集上优于DAPS（p<0.01），异配图Texas上提升10.3%。同配图CiteSeer、PubMed上DAPS仍胜出但优势极小（≤0.002）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未讨论计算开销和部署可行性；3. 校准的无标签选择器设计仍是开放问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。若强行关联，共形预测的不确定性量化思路可迁移至遥感目标检测的置信度校准，但论文本身不涉及检测任务和轻量化，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) provides distribution-free uncertainty quantification, and its extension to graphs is an active research direction. Diffused Adaptive Prediction Sets (DAPS) is a widely used graph-aware diffusion baseline, propagating Adaptive Prediction Sets (APS) non-conformity scores along edges with a uniform coefficient $λ$. We identify a fundamental shortcoming of this design: the uniform low-pass diffusion presupposes graph homophily and proves detrimental on heterophilic graphs, enlarging the mean prediction-set size by up to 10.6% relative to plain APS. To mitigate this, we propose HeAD-CP, a family of node-wise diffusion variants whose coefficients are determined by a label-free local-homophily estimate derived from the GNN softmax. Three variants, namely signed-$γ$, edge-compatibility, and a DAPS-baseline-with-correction, are most effective at extreme heterophily, intermediate heterophily, and moderate-to-high homophily, respectively, and all preserve the marginal coverage guarantee. On ten benchmarks, the HeAD-CP family stays at or below plain APS on every dataset, while DAPS exceeds APS on six. The post-hoc oracle over the family improves over DAPS on 8/10 datasets at $p<0.01$ (paired Wilcoxon), with the largest gains on heterophilic graphs (10.3% on Texas); on the two homophilic datasets where DAPS still wins (CiteSeer, PubMed), it retains a marginal advantage of at most 0.002, statistically insignificant on CiteSeer ($p=0.23$). Designing a calibrated label-free selector that approaches this oracle is the main outstanding empirical question.

</details>


### [154] [When Does Deep Representation Learning Help Single-Cell Clustering? A Sensitivity-Aware Diagnostic Benchmark for Biomedical AI Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25288)
*Nguyen Thanh Phong,Truong Viet Vu,Nguyen Ha Thu,Tran An Ky,Tran Hoang Thong,Le Pham Thuy Hien,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了scRNA-seq数据聚类中深度表示学习（自编码器、VAE）与经典PCA管线的性能，发现深度方法并非普遍优于PCA，并给出了数据集感知的计算决策框架。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文研究的是单细胞RNA测序（scRNA-seq）数据的无监督聚类问题，属于生物信息学/精准医学领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关。不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解等任何遥感视觉任务。也不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：单细胞RNA测序数据的无监督聚类。应用场景：精准医学/生物信息学。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：细胞类型（4-11种）。

Model architecture: 对比了9种聚类管线，包括：经典PCA管线、对比自编码器（contrastive autoencoder）、概率变分自编码器（probabilistic VAE）、深度自编码器（deep autoencoder）等。使用了Optuna超参数搜索、scVI V2等工具。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是深度表示学习是否值得额外的计算和调参成本，但并未提出或使用剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 10个真实scRNA-seq数据集（90-5,685个细胞，19,046-41,480个基因，4-11种细胞类型），另有7个数据集用于scVI V2专项比较。评价指标：Adjusted Rand Index (ARI)。统计方法：Friedman检验、Wilcoxon-Holm检验、TOST检验、Sobol总阶敏感性分析。

Experiments: 对比自编码器取得最高平均ARI（0.7872），但Holm校正检验未证明其显著优于最强基线。三种可复现模式：概率VAE在小数据集上有效，深度自编码器在中规模多批次/多类型数据上胜出，经典PCA在线性投影已能捕获主要变异时仍有竞争力。Sobol分析显示学习率（S_T=0.70）和潜在维度（S_T=0.56）是主要方差贡献因素。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文本身局限：未覆盖更大规模数据集，未涉及监督学习或检测任务，计算成本分析仅停留在调参层面而非模型压缩。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。但可间接注意：1）该论文的"数据集感知决策框架"思路可类比到遥感场景——不同分辨率、不同目标尺度、不同传感器类型下，轻量化策略的选择也应是数据感知的；2）Sobol敏感性分析的方法论可用于分析轻量化方法中哪些超参数（如剪枝率、量化位宽）对性能影响最大。但整体相关性极低。

Abstract: Single-cell ribonucleic acid sequencing (scRNA-seq) is a foundational technology for precision-medicine workflows that contribute to United Nations Sustainable Development Goal 3 on Good Health and Well-being, and unsupervised clustering is the analytical step that turns raw expression matrices into interpretable cell populations. Practitioners therefore face a recurring engineering decision: is an additional deep representation stage worth its compute and tuning cost, or do classical principal component analysis (PCA) pipelines already suffice? We address this question with a diagnostic benchmark of nine clustering pipelines on ten real datasets (90-5,685 cells, 19,046-41,480 genes, 4-11 cell types), augmented by a partial scVI V2 specialized comparison on seven datasets. The protocol integrates Optuna hyperparameter search, repeated-run robustness, Friedman/Wilcoxon-Holm/TOST testing, and Sobol total-order sensitivity analysis. The contrastive autoencoder achieved the highest mean Adjusted Rand Index (0.7872), but Holm-corrected tests did not establish dominance over the strongest baselines. Per-dataset analysis reveals three reproducible regimes: probabilistic variational autoencoder (VAE) variants help on the smallest datasets, deep autoencoders win on mid-scale data with multi-batch or many-type structure, and classical PCA pipelines remain competitive when linear projection already captures the dominant variation. Sobol indices identify learning rate ($S_T=0.70$) and latent dimensionality ($S_T=0.56$) as the dominant variance contributors, indicating where limited tuning budgets should be allocated. The contribution is therefore a dataset-aware and compute-conscious decision framework for biomedical AI pipelines supporting sustainable healthcare analytics, rather than a universal superiority claim.

</details>


### [155] [AMRD: Adaptive Multi-Teacher Relational Distillation for Lightweight Speech Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25289)
*Yuqi Li,Yi-Cheng Lin,Xianglong Wang,Kuo Yang,Xiaoqin Feng,Yixuan Wang,Huiran Duan,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多教师关系蒸馏（AMRD），通过单类SVM动态分配教师权重并结合关系蒸馏损失，提升语音情感识别的轻量化效果。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦语音情感识别（speech emotion recognition），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或任何视觉任务，仅在知识蒸馏方法层面有极弱的可迁移性。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，仅蒸馏方法层面有极弱参考价值，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：语音情感识别（SER）；场景：边缘设备实时应用；传感器类型：音频/语音；目标类别：情感类别（如IEMOCAP、CREMA-D数据集）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体学生网络架构，仅提及四种学生架构用于实验。教师模型为大规模自监督模型。

Lightweight method: 多教师知识蒸馏（multi-teacher knowledge distillation），包含两个创新：1）基于单类SVM的每批次自适应教师权重分配；2）关系蒸馏损失（relational distillation loss），对齐教师与学生之间的相似性矩阵。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及针对边缘设备（edge devices）的轻量化目标，但无具体量化数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：IEMOCAP、CREMA-D（均为语音情感识别数据集）；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 在两种数据集和四种学生架构上，AMRD在多数设置下优于单教师蒸馏基线；消融实验证实两个组件（自适应权重+关系蒸馏）具有互补增益。

Limitations: 1）与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关，属于语音领域；2）摘要未报告参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等轻量化核心指标；3）关系蒸馏方法在视觉目标检测任务上的迁移性未验证。

Ideas for my research: 1）关系蒸馏（relational distillation）的思想——对齐教师与学生特征/预测的相似性矩阵——可尝试迁移到遥感目标检测的轻量化中，用于保留检测头中类别间关系结构；2）自适应多教师权重分配策略（基于单类SVM评估教师一致性）可借鉴到遥感场景中多尺度/多模态教师蒸馏；3）但整体而言，该论文与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化课题的直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: On-device speech emotion recognition (SER) is critical for real-time applications, yet large self-supervised models that excel at SER are too costly for edge devices. Multi-teacher knowledge distillation can compress them into a lightweight student, but two challenges remain: teacher reliability varies across batches, and logit-level distillation ignores inter-sample relational structure. We propose Adaptive Multi-teacher Relational Distillation (AMRD) to address both. A one-class SVM on each teacher's logit similarity matrix assigns per-batch weights favoring more coherent teachers. A relational distillation loss aligns teacher and student similarity matrices, capturing structure that logit matching misses. On IEMOCAP and CREMA-D datasets across four student architectures, AMRD outperforms single-teacher distillation baselines in most settings, and ablations confirm both components yield complementary gains.

</details>


### [156] [Breaking the Periodicity Assumption: Robust Tensorial Multi-View Clustering via Graph-Spectral Low-Rank Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25295)
*Jintian Ji,Xingsu Li,Songhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了t-SVD张量多视图聚类方法对样本排列顺序的敏感性，并提出基于图傅里叶变换（GFT）的图谱低秩张量学习框架来解决该问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题为多视图聚类（multi-view clustering），属于无监督学习/聚类领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多视图聚类（multi-view clustering）；应用场景：通用聚类任务；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于t-SVD的张量分解框架；提出基于图傅里叶变换（Graph Fourier Transform, GFT）的图谱低秩张量学习框架，替代传统FFT基函数；提出anchor-based变体处理大规模数据。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 在多个通用benchmark数据集上进行实验，具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 实验验证了t-SVD方法对样本排列顺序的敏感性，并证明所提GFT方法相比现有TMC方法具有竞争性或更优性能。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未讨论方法的计算效率、实时性、模型大小等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：GFT替代FFT的思路在信号处理层面有一定理论价值，但与本课题（遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化）无直接关联。

Abstract: Tensorial multi-view clustering (TMC) has achieved strong performance due to its ability to capture high-order correlations across multiple views. Most existing t-SVD-based TMC frameworks apply the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) along the sample mode to impose frequency-domain low-rank constraints. However, we reveal that this widely adopted design critically relies on an implicit ``periodicity assumption'' induced by the sample arrangement. When samples are ordered by class, neighboring indices tend to be semantically similar, creating artificial local continuity along the sample mode and a favorable spectral structure for FFT-based low-rank regularization. Once this ordering is removed by random permutation, existing t-SVD-based TMC methods suffer severe performance degradation. This strong sensitivity to class ordering conflicts with the permutation-invariant nature of clustering and indicates that part of the reported performance may be attributed to a privileged sample arrangement rather than genuine high-order structure modeling. In this paper, we systematically investigate this phenomenon and its underlying algebraic and spectral mechanisms. To address this fundamental flaw, we further propose a graph-spectral low-rank tensor learning framework based on the Graph Fourier Transform (GFT), which replaces the fixed Fourier basis along the sample mode with a data-driven graph spectral basis, thereby capturing the intrinsic manifold structure without relying on a particular sample ordering. Moreover, we develop an anchor-based variant to address large-scale datasets efficiently. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks validate our findings and demonstrate the competitive or superior performance of the proposed methods compared with state-of-the-art TMC approaches.

</details>


### [157] [Zhinv: Real-time hub-height wind field reconstruction using only local sparse observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25298)
*Zongwei Zhang,Chin Chun Ooi,Lianlei Lin,Sheng Gao,Tiantian He,Yew Soon Ong,Junkai Wang,Hangyi Yu,Jiaqi Zhang,Hanqing Zhao,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Zhinv端到端重建框架，将稀疏不规则的风场观测数据直接重建为细粒度网格风场，用于风电并网和低空环境感知。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦风场重建（wind field reconstruction），不涉及遥感图像、卫星载荷、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然提到"低空环境感知"，但方法本质是空间插值/重建，非遥感图像目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：风场细粒度重建（wind field reconstruction）；场景：风电并网、风资源评估、低空环境感知；传感器类型：稀疏风观测站（非遥感卫星传感器）；目标类别：风速/风向场，非目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构，仅描述为"end-to-end reconstruction framework"。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"efficiently reconstruct"，但无量化指标。

Datasets and metrics: 实验区域：中国东北、欧洲、东南亚；对比方法：Kriging（克里金插值）；评价指标：误差降低约66%（与Kriging相比），具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在三个区域验证了Zhinv的准确性、鲁棒性和效率，相比Kriging误差降低约66%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 无轻量化/部署相关信息；3. 无模型结构细节；4. 无参数量、计算量等量化指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。唯一可能的交叉点是：如果未来研究涉及"从稀疏遥感观测重建稠密信息"（如从稀疏卫星观测重建连续场），其端到端重建思路可参考，但与目标检测和轻量化无直接关系。

Abstract: The high proportion of wind power connected to the grid places higher demands on fine-grained knowledge of regional wind fields. Since the wind information directly obtainable in actual operations is mostly sparse, discrete, and irregularly distributed local observations, it is difficult to directly meet the needs of tasks such as wind power regulation, wind resource assessment, and low-altitude environmental perception of continuous regional wind fields. Therefore, we propose Zhinv, an end-to-end reconstruction framework that directly weaves sparse and irregular observations into a fine-grid wind field at hub-height. Experiments in Northeast China, Europe, and Southeast Asia demonstrate that Zhinv can accurately, robustly, and efficiently reconstruct fine-grid wind fields from sparse observations, reducing the error by about 66% compared with Kriging. With local wind-power observations as input, Zhinv enables wind power centers to bypass NWP and complex assimilation processes, supporting direct and real-time wind resource assessment from locally available data.

</details>


### [158] [Retraction-Free Optimization over the Stiefel Manifold for the LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25299)
*Yuan Zhang,Jiang Hu,Zhijian Lai,Lin Lin,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无回缩、无惩罚参数的Stiefel流形优化算法，并将其应用于大语言模型的LoRA微调（Manifold-LoRA），以加速几何适应过程。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文核心是流形优化算法及其在大语言模型LoRA微调中的应用，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，阅读优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：流形优化算法设计；应用场景：大语言模型（LLM）的LoRA微调；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 提出Manifold-LoRA方法，将LoRA微调重新建模为Stiefel流形上的优化问题，采用landing技术（直接着陆到流形上）替代传统的retraction算子。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文涉及LoRA（低秩适应），这是一种参数高效微调方法，可视为某种形式的轻量化训练策略，但论文重点在优化算法本身，而非模型压缩或加速推理。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文面向大语言模型微调，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要仅提及"benchmark datasets"和"downstream performance"，未给出具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 在基准数据集上进行了数值实验，验证了所提方法的效率和下游任务性能。未提供具体对比方法和消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 完全未涉及遥感/卫星场景；2. 未讨论目标检测任务；3. 未提供模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）相关分析；4. 未讨论边缘/星上部署可行性；5. 实验仅在LLM微调场景验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1) Stiefel流形优化思想或可迁移到遥感模型中的正交约束（如BN层、卷积核正交正则化）优化问题；2) LoRA的低秩思想在遥感轻量化中已有应用，但本文的流形优化视角对遥感场景的适配性存疑。总体而言，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Optimization over the Stiefel manifold plays a significant role in various machine learning tasks. Existing methods either use the retraction operators, requiring costly orthonormalization for large-scale matrices, or employ landing methods that rely on careful step size selection and penalty parameter tuning. To address these challenges, we propose a retraction-free and penalty parameter-free algorithm that directly lands on the manifold. By leveraging the strongly-convex-like property of the quadratic penalty function and the proximal smoothness of the Stiefel manifold, we establish global convergence guarantees with the best-known iteration complexities under both constant and diminishing step sizes. Then, we reformulate the low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning problem for large language models as a manifold optimization problem, introducing Manifold-LoRA for geometry-accelerated adaptation. This approach employs the proposed landing technique and a carefully designed step size strategy to accelerate the training process. Numerical experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate the efficiency and strong downstream performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [159] [Guiding Posterior Exploration with Optimizer-Derived Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25312)
*Moritz Schlager,Emanuel Sommer,Thomas Möllenhoff,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出利用AdamW优化器预热阶段产生的曲率估计作为预条件，加速贝叶斯神经网络中MCMC采样的收敛，减少采样预热时间并提升数值稳定性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于贝叶斯神经网络的不确定性量化采样加速方法，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。

Reading priority: 1分。完全不涉及遥感、目标检测或轻量化部署，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：贝叶斯神经网络的不确定性量化；应用场景：通用机器学习；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络架构，仅提及"various network architectures"。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化。论文讨论的是贝叶斯神经网络中MCMC采样的加速（通过预条件策略），而非模型压缩、剪枝、量化、蒸馏或NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标，仅提及"various datasets"。

Experiments: 在多种数据集和网络架构上验证了所提预条件采样策略的有效性，声称能维持或提升预测性能与不确定性量化质量，且无额外计算开销。

Limitations: 摘要未讨论局限性。论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：如果未来在轨部署中需要贝叶斯不确定性估计（如检测置信度校准），该方法的采样加速思路可能有参考价值，但与本课题核心方向（轻量化目标检测模型）无直接关联。

Abstract: Sampling-based methods offer a principled approach to uncertainty quantification in Bayesian neural networks. Their practical use, however, is often challenged by the computational cost of exploring high-dimensional and multimodal posterior distributions. To overcome these difficulties, Bayesian Deep Ensembles, i.e., warmstarting the sampling from several optimized solutions, have proven to be an effective strategy. In this paper, we demonstrate that curvature estimates computed during the warmstart as a byproduct in adaptive optimizers such as AdamW can inform the sampling phase at negligible additional cost. Specifically, our proposed preconditioned sampling strategy based on optimizer-derived geometries can substantially reduce or even eliminate the need for a lengthy sampling burn-in phase and leads to greater numerical stability. This approach consistently maintains or improves predictive performance and uncertainty quantification without any additional computational costs. We confirm the consistency of our findings across various datasets and network architectures.

</details>


### [160] [Explainable AI for Chronic Kidney Disease Prediction Using Simulated Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25348)
*Md Zahid Hasan Ontor,Md Al Amin,Anik Dev Nath,Bikash Kumar Paul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用联邦学习与集成学习（随机森林、AdaBoost、XGBoost）对临床数据集进行慢性肾病早期预测，并引入可解释AI提升模型透明度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦临床医学（慢性肾病预测），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等压缩加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学分类（慢性肾病预测）；应用场景：临床医疗；传感器类型：临床数据集（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：CKD阳性/阴性。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 联邦学习框架 + VotingClassifier集成（Random Forest, AdaBoost, XGBoost）+ GridSearchCV调参。非视觉模型，无CNN/Transformer/检测头等结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。且临床医学模型与星上部署场景无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：临床CKD数据集（具体名称未说明）；评价指标：平均准确率（Average Accuracy = 99%）。无遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、LEVIR等）。

Experiments: 全局模型平均准确率99%，对比了Random Forest、AdaBoost、XGBoost三种基学习器在联邦学习框架下的表现。无消融实验、无遥感检测对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术细节。联邦学习框架虽可用于分布式场景，但本文未涉及遥感数据或视觉检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。联邦学习（FL）作为分布式训练范式，理论上可用于遥感卫星多节点协同训练，但本文未涉及遥感场景、视觉检测或轻量化，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Chronic Kidney Disease (CKD), characterized by the gradual loss of kidney function, remains a significant public health challenge. Early detection is crucial for preventing severe complications and enhancing patient outcomes. In this study, Federated Learning (FL) with a VotingClassifier was used to predict CKD using a clinical dataset, where Random Forest, AdaBoost, and XGBoost were utilized to compare and identify the best-fitting model for the global server. Additionally, GridSearchCV was applied to optimize the models' performance on the client's side. To enhance model transparency and trustworthiness, explainable AI (XAI) techniques were incorporated to interpret the prediction mechanisms. The global model's average accuracy was 99%, highlighting the potential of interpretable FL models in supporting early CKD diagnosis and advancing data-driven healthcare solutions.

</details>


### [161] [Raven: High-Recall Sequence Modeling with Sparse Memory Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25357)
*Arshia Afzal,Aviv Bick,Eric P. Xing,Volkan Cevher,Albert Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Raven，一种线性时间序列模型，通过输入依赖的稀疏内存更新机制在状态空间模型和滑动窗口注意力之间取得平衡，提升长上下文召回能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自然语言处理/序列建模中的长上下文召回问题，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星在轨部署或任何视觉任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非后续工作将Raven架构迁移到视觉/遥感领域，否则参考价值极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：长上下文序列建模（long-context recall）；应用场景：自然语言处理/序列模型；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Raven模型：维护固定数量的内存槽（memory slots），通过输入依赖的路由机制（input-dependent routing）在每个时间步仅选择性地衰减和更新部分内存子集。融合了状态空间模型（SSM）和滑动窗口注意力（SWA）的思想。

Lightweight method: 论文本身不涉及轻量化方法。Raven是线性时间复杂度的序列模型，但其轻量化属性（线性复杂度）是针对序列长度而言的，并非模型压缩/剪枝/量化/蒸馏等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然模型是线性时间复杂度，但未讨论在边缘设备或星上部署的可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确说明具体数据集名称。评价指标为长上下文召回率（long-context recall）相关的基准测试。

Experiments: 在召回密集型基准测试中，Raven与现有线性时间基线模型相比具有竞争力或更优；在SWA和SSM性能急剧下降的场景下，Raven仍能保持强长上下文召回能力；可外推到训练长度16倍的上下文长度。

Limitations: 1. 论文完全面向NLP/序列建模领域，未涉及任何视觉或遥感任务；2. 未讨论模型参数量、计算量等轻量化关键指标；3. 未在遥感数据集上验证；4. 未讨论在轨/边缘部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1. Raven的稀疏内存更新机制（仅更新部分内存槽）可类比于特征图上的稀疏卷积或稀疏注意力，可能对遥感大图的局部目标检测效率有启发；2. 线性复杂度模型设计思路（如SSM变体）若结合视觉任务，可能为遥感在轨模型提供新的骨干网络选择。但整体而言，该论文与课题方向差距较大。

Abstract: Long-context recall in linear-time sequence models highlights a tradeoff in how they write to memory. State-based linear models, such as state-space models (SSMs) and linear Transformers, write densely, updating the entire state for each newly arrived token, which leads to interference and makes specific past tokens hard to recover. Sliding-window attention (SWA) exhibits the opposite behavior: it writes sparsely by storing explicit token representations, but only within a fixed window, so recall drops once the relevant token is evicted. Interpolating between these models, we introduce Raven, a linear-time sequence model that maintains a fixed set of memory slots and, at each step, decays and updates only a selected subset via learned, input-dependent routing. This lets Raven mitigate SWA's position-based overwriting and hard eviction while reducing interference from dense state updates in SSMs, thereby preserving long-range content much more effectively. Across recall-intensive benchmarks, Raven is competitive with or outperforms prior linear-time baselines, achieving strong long-context recall where both SWA and SSMs sharply degrade. It remains effective when extrapolating to context lengths as large as 16x its training length, with similar gains in hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [162] [Rethinking Likelihood distributions: Student's t Likelihood Boosts Bayesian Neural Network Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25376)
*Pei-Hsuan Hsia,Lars H. Heyen,Arvid Weyrauch,Markus Goetz,Achim Streit,Sebastian Krumscheid,Charlotte Debus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究贝叶斯神经网络中似然分布假设（如Student's t分布）对回归任务预测性能的影响，发现Student's t分布优于高斯分布。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无直接或间接关系。论文聚焦于贝叶斯神经网络的似然分布选择（Student's t vs. Gaussian），任务为回归，模型为MLP，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：回归任务（regression）；应用场景：人工数据集和真实数据集上的标准回归；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 标准多层感知机（MLP），探讨不同深度和宽度配置。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 人工数据集和真实世界数据集；评价指标为预测性能（predictive performance），具体指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 对比了多种似然分布假设（如skewed, heavy-tailed），发现Student's t分布优于Gaussian分布，且独立于数据分布和MLP架构（深度和宽度）。部分情况下Student's t可缩短训练时间。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对回归任务，未涉及分类或目标检测；2. 仅使用MLP，未在CNN/Transformer等视觉模型上验证；3. 未讨论计算开销、推理效率等部署相关指标；4. 与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：若在遥感目标检测的贝叶斯不确定性建模中，可尝试用Student's t分布替代高斯分布作为似然假设，但论文未涉及检测任务和视觉模型，迁移可行性未知。

Abstract: In Bayesian neural networks (BNNs), variational inference is a widely adopted framework for modeling uncertainty in a distributional way, with the evidence lower bound (ELBO) serving as the standard objective function. Several distributions contribute to the ELBO loss, such as the prior, approximated posterior, and likelihood distribution. Typically, these distributions are all approximated by a Gaussian distribution, since it is easy to compute, allows for reparameterized gradients, and provides a closed-form loss for training. However, several works have highlighted that this assumption may not generally hold, posing the risk of model misspecification. Alternative distributions have been proposed for the prior specifically, while the effect of distribution choice on the likelihood distribution remains unexplored. In this work, our aim is to close this gap by investigating whether alternative assumptions for the likelihood distribution can outperform the commonly used Gaussian. We compare several likelihood distribution assumptions, such as skewed or heavy-tailed, across regression tasks on both artificial and real-world datasets using standard multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). Our findings demonstrate that Student's t yields better predictive performance than a Gaussian likelihood distribution, independent of the data distribution and MLP architecture (depth and width). In some cases, Student's t can also lead to shorter training times, while still being easy to implement.

</details>


### [163] [Learned, Relied Upon, or Necessary? Separating Checkpoint Dependence from Task-Level Value in Sheaf GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25387)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究图神经网络中限制映射（restriction maps）的可解释性问题，提出区分"检查点依赖"与"协议相对替换"两种估计量，证明学习到的传输映射并不必然代表不可替代的边几何结构。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于图神经网络（GNN）中限制映射的可解释性理论分析，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为图神经网络的可解释性分析，应用场景为图结构数据（如NSD、DNSD、DSNN基准），不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: Sheaf Graph Neural Networks（层图神经网络），涉及限制映射（restriction maps）、边几何（edge geometry）、传输映射（transport）、DSNN（Directed Sheaf Neural Network）等图神经网络架构概念。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：NSD、DNSD、Directed Sheaf Neural Network (DSNN) 基准，Roman-Empire数据集（10个官方划分）。评价指标：任务值（task value）、性能恢复程度（如.0675和.0391的优势值）。

Experiments: 在5个DNSD基准上观察到固定检查点依赖；经过重训练后，4个基准上通过打破分配或共享映射控制可恢复完整性能；Roman-Empire数据集上保留了对持续重采样分配0.0675的优势和对参数匹配共享映射0.0391的优势。

Limitations: 论文与遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等方向完全无关。其理论贡献局限于图神经网络的可解释性领域，无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测任务中。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文的研究对象（图神经网络的限制映射可解释性）与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题域、方法体系和应用场景上均无交集。不建议作为参考论文。

Abstract: Learned restriction maps in sheaf graph neural networks are often treated as proof that the model has discovered useful edge geometry. That conclusion does not follow from parameter movement or from a post-hoc ablation: both can show how one checkpoint is organized while leaving open whether learned transport still helps after the rest of the model adapts. We separate these claims with two estimands. Checkpoint reliance intervenes on the maps of a fixed predictor; protocol-relative replacement retrains matched families that remove map capacity, edge variation, or persistent edge assignment. A task-null theorem shows why the claims can diverge: labels identify only the transported classifier directions, leaving $d^2-d$ invisible degrees of freedom in every full $d\times d$ map. An exact frame model then gives the boundary at which reliance becomes unreplaced task value. Label-only training realizes the predicted separation, while audits of public NSD, DNSD, and Directed Sheaf Neural Network (DSNN) implementations recover both replaceable and unreplaced transport regimes on real graphs. All five DNSD benchmarks exhibit fixed-checkpoint reliance. After retraining, assignment-breaking or shared-map controls recover Full performance on four; Roman-Empire retains a $.0675$ advantage over continually resampled assignment and a $.0391$ advantage over a parameter-matched shared map across ten official splits. Thus, a learned map can govern a fitted computation without constituting indispensable edge geometry. Claims of learned transport should pair checkpoint interventions with matched retraining.

</details>


### [164] [TWICE: Two-Clock, Two-Window Learning for Long-Horizon Conversion Prediction in Online Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25404)
*Kaiyuan Li,Kun Wang,Zhongbo Wang,Teng Sha,Ming Yan,Yanhua Cheng,Xialong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TWICE框架，将长时程转化率预测分解为目标窗口转化概率和分组延迟累积分布函数，解决在线广告中双时钟延迟反馈问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是在线广告领域的转化率预测问题，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；不涉及任何目标检测任务；不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。属于泛机器学习/广告推荐方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：长时程转化率预测（CVR prediction）；场景：在线广告（online advertising）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: TWICE框架，将CVR分解为目标窗口转化概率 + 分组延迟累积分布函数（CDF）。包含click-clock和conversion-clock两个时钟的监督信号。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"Serving requires neither historical lookup nor convolution"表明推理时无需历史查找或卷积操作，但未给出具体效率指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：public benchmark + industrial advertising dataset + Kwai在线A/B测试；评价指标：expected revenue (+2.486%), revenue (+1.858%), conversions (+2.061%)。

Experiments: 在公开基准和工业数据集上验证有效性；在Kwai广告系统在线A/B测试中取得收益提升，已部署到全流量。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要本身未讨论模型效率、部署资源开销等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于广告点击率/转化率预测领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化方向无交叉。

Abstract: Long-horizon conversion prediction under delayed feedback creates a two-clock, two-window learning problem in online advertising. A short base observation window releases recent clicks on the click clock before their outcomes mature, whereas conversions continue to arrive on the conversion clock throughout a longer target conversion window. The click clock provides timely but partially observed status supervision. The conversion clock reveals long-tail delays, but the delay composition within an arrival-time slice is weighted by historical click cohorts with different traffic volumes and target-window conversion rates.
  We present TWICE, a framework that factorizes long-horizon post-click conversion rate (CVR) into a target-window conversion probability and a grouped elapsed-delay cumulative distribution function (CDF). The two clocks provide complementary supervision. Click-clock records train the target-window CVR head through a current-status likelihood over the base observation window. Newly arrived conversions train the delay model on the conversion clock. To account for the cohort mixture, TWICE uses fixed click-time predicted CVR (pCVR) mass as cohort exposure in an arrival-conditioned likelihood. This accounts for differences in cohort traffic and conversion propensity. The resulting aggregate records are self-contained. A single learned CDF produces monotone predictions for all requested horizons up to the target conversion window. Serving requires neither historical lookup nor convolution. Experiments on a public benchmark and an industrial advertising dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of TWICE. In an online A/B test in Kwai's advertising system, TWICE increased expected revenue, revenue, and conversions by 2.486%, 1.858%, and 2.061%, respectively. It was subsequently deployed to full traffic.

</details>


### [165] [SPARC Segmentation to Prediction via Affine Regression and Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25413)
*Shivani,Subhayan Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向B2B电商交易倾向预测的框架，用反事实解释生成（DiCE）替代SMOTE进行少数类样本增强，并结合PyPARC分段仿射分类器输出校准概率，实现高精度营销客户分层。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全聚焦于B2B电商交易倾向预测（商业数据挖掘/客户关系管理），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关领域，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：B2B交易倾向预测（propensity modeling）；场景：B2B电子商务平台；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: PyPARC（piecewise affine classification framework）分段仿射分类器，结合DiCE（Diverse Counterfactual Explanations）生成合成少数类样本。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及"production deployed"，但未涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：某大型B2B电商平台两年纵向数据，类别不平衡比1:9；评价指标：Precision（精度），在决策阈值0.8时93.1%，阈值0.7时未直接给出但对比SMOTE提升26.1个百分点。

Experiments: 与SMOTE基线对比：在阈值0.8时精度93.1% vs 83.9%（提升9.2个百分点）；在阈值0.7时相比SMOTE提升26.1个百分点。通过定量邻近度分析和UMAP聚类可视化验证了DiCE生成样本的分布保真度优于SMOTE。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术细节。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度、参数量等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。DiCE反事实解释生成方法在理论上可用于遥感数据增强（如生成罕见目标样本），但论文未涉及遥感场景，且轻量化方面无任何贡献。

Abstract: Transaction propensity prediction in B2B e commerce presents unique challenges distinct from B2C contexts, primarily due to the heterogeneous procurement behaviors of organizational entities, which violate SMOTE's implicit assumption of within class feature homogeneity. Specifically, B2B buyers exhibit multi modal procurement cycles that render linear interpolation between minority class samples structurally invalid, producing synthetic data that does not represent real purchasing behavior. This paper introduces a production deployed propensity modeling framework designed to address these complexities through two primary contributions. First, we replace conventional SMOTE based augmentation with a synthetic data generation approach leveraging Diverse Counterfactual Explanations (DiCE). This method produces minority class samples with superior distributional fidelity compared to SMOTE, as validated through quantitative proximity analysis and UMAP cluster visualization. Second, we adapt the PyPARC piecewise affine classification framework to generate calibrated propensity probabilities, facilitating the interpretable segmentation of customers into actionable risk tiers. Evaluated on two years of longitudinal data from a large scale B2B e commerce platform with a 1 to 9 class imbalance ratio, the proposed architecture achieves 93.1% precision at a decision threshold of 0.8, a 9.2 percentage point improvement over SMOTE based baselines at the same threshold (83.9%), and a 26.1 point improvement over SMOTE at threshold 0.7 (66.04%), demonstrating consistent superiority across operating points. These results demonstrate the framework's efficacy in enabling high precision marketing campaigns with significant improvements in customer activation and return on investment.

</details>


### [166] [PIcsC: Partitioning-Induced Covariate Shift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25441)
*Behraj Khan,Behroz Mirza,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari,Tahir Qasim Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Fisher信息的协变量偏移校正框架PIcsC，用于缓解跨训练数据分区的分布不匹配问题，适用于交叉验证、终身学习和联邦学习场景。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通用机器学习中的协变量偏移校正问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：协变量偏移校正（covariate shift correction）；应用场景：交叉验证、终身学习、联邦学习；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）的正则化框架PIcsC，包含条件自适应机制（结合FIM偏移与KL散度检测显著分布偏移）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性。

Datasets and metrics: 超过40个数据集；评价指标：性能提升百分比（fragmentation-induced performance degradation reduction >20%和>25%），联邦学习准确率提升（3-5个百分点）。

Experiments: 在碎片化批次和折叠设置下，性能退化减少超过20%和25%；在7个联邦学习基准上，一致优于FedAvg、FedProx和SCAFFOLD 3-5个百分点。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：联邦学习场景与卫星星座分布式学习有一定概念重叠，但本文未涉及遥感数据或目标检测任务，且不涉及任何轻量化技术。

Abstract: Covariate shift across training-data partitions biases model selection and parameter estimation in cross-validation, lifelong learning, and federated learning. We propose \textit{Partition-Induced Covariate-shift Correction} (\texttt{PIcsC}), a Fisher information-based regularization framework that mitigates distribution mismatch between data partitions and a reference distribution. \texttt{PIcsC} approximates partition divergence using the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM) and incorporates the resulting statistic as a regularizer during optimization. The same formulation applies to both centrally partitioned datasets (batches or cross-validation folds) and inherently distributed data (federated clients or decentralized nodes), requiring only partition-local gradient statistics rather than raw data. We further introduce a conditional adaptation mechanism that combines FIM shift with KL divergence to detect significant distribution shifts and activates regularization only when necessary. Experiments on more than 40 datasets demonstrate consistent improvements under both natural and synthetic covariate shift. On fragmented batch and fold settings, \texttt{PIcsC} reduces fragmentation-induced performance degradation by more than 20\% and 25\%, respectively. On seven federated learning benchmarks, it consistently outperforms FedAvg, FedProx, and SCAFFOLD by 3 -5 percentage points without requiring client-specific personalization. These results demonstrate that Fisher information provides an effective and unified mechanism for mitigating partition-induced covariate shift across both centralized and distributed learning.

</details>


### [167] [Bits and Memories: Measuring Verbatim Extraction Across LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25451)
*Akshay Sasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究语言模型量化对逐字记忆提取的影响，发现量化选择性遗忘记忆数据但不足以作为隐私防御手段。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是语言模型（Pythia models）的量化对隐私风险（逐字记忆提取）的影响，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或任何视觉任务。虽然涉及"量化"（quantization）这一轻量化技术，但应用场景、任务目标、评估方式与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言模型隐私风险评估（逐字提取 vs 成员推断）；应用场景：NLP模型部署；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Pythia models（语言模型），三种模型尺寸。

Lightweight method: 量化（quantization），五种精度级别（full precision 到 4-bit），两种量化算法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注的是量化对隐私的影响，而非部署效率。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Pythia models 已知记忆的公开序列集；评价指标：逐字提取率（verbatim extraction）、困惑度（perplexity）。

Experiments: 在五种精度（full precision 到 4-bit）、三种模型尺寸下，使用两种量化算法和两种评估语料库进行实验。发现量化选择性遗忘记忆数据（遗忘速度比能力下降更快），但4-bit量化仍能恢复大部分记忆序列，且记忆存活率随模型尺寸增大而上升。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测/视觉任务；2. 未讨论量化对模型推理速度、参数量、FLOPs等部署效率指标的影响；3. 仅针对语言模型，结论无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测模型。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的点：论文发现量化对不同类型信息（记忆数据 vs 通用能力）的选择性影响——这提示在遥感目标检测模型量化时，可能也需要关注量化对不同类别目标（如小目标 vs 大目标）或不同任务性能（检测精度 vs 推理速度）的非对称影响。

Abstract: Language models are almost always quantized before they are deployed, and a growing line of work asks whether quantization also lowers their privacy risk. That work measures privacy almost entirely with membership inference. We think this is the wrong thing to measure for the risk that most people actually worry about, namely a model reproducing its training data word for word, and we measure that directly. Using the Pythia models and the public set of sequences each of them is known to have memorized, we track verbatim extraction across five precision levels, from full precision down to four bits, and across three model sizes, while measuring general capability (perplexity) at every point. We find two things. Quantization is a selective forgetter: verbatim memorization falls off faster than capability at every precision and every model size we tried, and this holds under two unrelated quantization algorithms and two evaluation corpora. But the selectivity is not enough to make quantization a privacy defense, which cuts against the optimistic reading of earlier membership-inference results. At the largest model we study, four-bit quantization still reproduces most of the memorized sequences while giving up only a few percent of capability, and the fraction of memorized data that survives quantization grows with model size. We conclude that compression should not be treated as a way to remove memorized training data, and that extraction, not membership inference, is the number practitioners should be watching. All code, sampled evaluation data, and per-configuration results are released.

</details>


### [168] [Emergent Latent-State Computation under Stochastic Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25459)
*Xiaoyu Huang,Lulu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究序列模型在随机波动率设定下对隐式随机动力学的内部表征机制，属于机械可解释性方向，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化均无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感领域内容；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等模型压缩或加速方法。论文属于机械可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）在金融时间序列（随机波动率模型）中的应用，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机械可解释性分析（mechanistic interpretability）；应用场景：金融时间序列中的随机波动率建模；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 分析了多种架构（across architectures），包括Transformer；具体结构细节摘要未详细说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用合成数据（controlled multivariate stochastic volatility setting），非遥感数据集；评价指标为latent-state decodability（隐状态可解码性）等可解释性指标。

Experiments: 主要发现：模型存在两阶段计算（two-stage computation）；隐表示编码了关于下一隐波动率状态的信息；在Transformer中，隐状态可解码性出现在特定架构阶段，位置取决于波动率周期；长周期模式下计算简化为显式隐状态滤波器（线性投影+ℓ²归一化）；输出头替换实验表明噪声MSE训练下的性能下降部分源于读出对齐失败而非表征失败。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或检测方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是金融时间序列的机械可解释性，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关技术。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has largely focused on language models and deterministic toy tasks. Much less is known about how sequence models internally represent latent stochastic dynamics under noisy, partially observed observations. We study this question in a controlled multivariate stochastic volatility setting, where models observe only returns while the ground-truth latent volatility state is known to the researcher. This setting provides a useful benchmark for mechanistic interpretability under partial observability: the latent state is hidden from the model but directly available for evaluation. Across architectures, losses, and output heads, we find evidence for a two-stage computation. Hidden representations encode substantial information about the next latent volatility state, and the output head maps this representation to squared return forecasts. Furthermore, in Transformers, latent-state decodability emerges at identifiable architectural stages whose location depends on the volatility period. In long-cycle regimes, this computation simplifies into an explicit latent-state filter consisting of a learned linear projection followed by $\ell^2$ normalization. Output-head replacement further shows that part of the degradation under noisy MSE training arises from readout misalignment rather than representation failure. These results suggest that stochastic volatility models provide a useful benchmark for mechanistic interpretability under noisy latent dynamics and partial observability.

</details>


### [169] [Data-Dependent Regret and Polyak Corrections for Constrained Online Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25480)
*Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种带自适应步长的在线凸优化方法AdaOGD-PFS，通过数据相关的梯度累积和Polyak修正项改进遗憾界，在球约束和半空间约束问题上验证了38-43%的遗憾界提升。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于在线凸优化（online convex optimization）理论方向，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测任务、轻量化方法或星上部署场景。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为约束在线凸优化，应用场景为安全关键系统中的约束优化问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测等任何视觉任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文方法为在线梯度下降（OGD）结合Polyak可行性步（Polyak feasibility step），属于优化算法而非神经网络模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在球约束（ball-constrained）和半空间约束（halfspace-constrained）问题上进行，评价指标为遗憾界（regret bound），不涉及任何遥感或视觉数据集。

Experiments: 在球约束和半空间约束问题上，AdaOGD-PFS相比基线方法实现了38-43%的遗憾界提升，数据相关梯度和Polyak修正项均有贡献。

Limitations: 论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。其理论贡献局限于在线凸优化领域。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于纯优化理论，不涉及任何视觉任务、遥感场景或模型轻量化技术，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无参考价值。

Abstract: Constrained online convex optimization requires minimizing regret against adversarial convex costs while satisfying a convex constraint at every round, as needed in safety-critical applications. A computationally efficient method combines online gradient descent with a Polyak feasibility step, using one constraint evaluation and one subgradient per round. Although this method achieves O(sqrt(T)) regret with per-round feasibility, we derive a tighter, data-dependent analysis by retaining two quantities omitted by the standard worst-case argument. First, we replace the gradient envelope G_f^2 T with the observed accumulation G_T = sum_t ||grad f_t(x_t)||^2. Second, we identify a nonnegative Polyak correction P_T that measures the cumulative squared displacement caused by feasibility projections and enters the regret bound with a negative sign. The resulting improvement, Delta_T = (eta/2)(G_f^2 T - G_T) + P_T/(2 eta), is always nonnegative. We further propose AdaOGD-PFS, an adaptive-step-size method that achieves O(sqrt(G_T)) regret while preserving per-round feasibility. Experiments on ball- and halfspace-constrained problems improve the regret bound by 38 to 43 percent, with both data-dependent gradients and Polyak corrections contributing substantially.

</details>


### [170] [Quantum Speedups for Stochastic Optimization with Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25492)
*Bin Luo,Chengchang Liu,Jonathan Allcock,Shengyu Zhang,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究重尾梯度噪声下的随机优化，提出量子均值估计器及量子随机梯度下降方法（QNSGD、QPSGD），属于量子优化理论，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文为纯理论分析，无实验数据集或评价指标。

Abstract: We study stochastic optimization with heavy-tailed gradient noise. We first propose a novel quantum mean estimator for multivariate heavy-tailed random variables that achieves lower query complexity than optimal classical estimators in the low-dimensional regime. We further develop an unbiased quantum mean estimator by applying a generalized multi-level Monte Carlo technique. We prove quantum lower bounds showing that, when the dimension $d$ of the random vector is small and can be viewed as a constant, our quantum estimators are optimal up to logarithmic factors. We further derive stronger dimension-dependent lower bounds for tail index $p>4/3$, showing that a nontrivial dependence on the dimension is unavoidable in the low-dimensional regime. Based on these estimators, we propose a quantum normalized stochastic gradient descent method ($\texttt{QNSGD}$), which finds an $ε$-stationary point using $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(\sqrt d\,ε^{-\frac{5p-4}{2p-2}}\big)$ queries to the quantum stochastic gradient oracle. For a convex objective function, we propose a quantum projected stochastic gradient descent method ($\texttt{QPSGD}$), which computes a solution with $ε$-optimal solution using $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(\sqrt d\,ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{2p-2}}+ε^{-2}\big)$ queries in expectation. These sharper bounds improve upon the classical lower bounds $Ω\big(ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{p-1}}\big)$ for nonconvex problems and $Ω\big(ε^{-\frac{p}{p-1}}\big)$ for convex problems in the low-dimensional regimes $d\lesssimε^{-\frac{p}{p-1}}$ and $d\lesssimε^{-\frac{2-p}{p-1}}$, respectively.

</details>


### [171] [Anti-Backdoor Coreset Selection via Cumulative Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25502)
*Qi Zhao,Christian Wressnegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于累积熵的防后门攻击训练时防御方法，通过核心集选择隔离良性样本以训练无后门模型。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于神经网络后门攻击的防御策略（Anti-Backdoor Coreset Selection），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化任何核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题（神经网络后门攻击防御）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：后门攻击防御（backdoor defense）；应用场景：通用神经网络训练安全；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。方法基于训练样本的累积熵（Cumulative Entropy）进行核心集选择（coreset selection），并引入每轮遗忘（unlearning）机制以增强良性与有毒样本的可分离性。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。本文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提及使用自然准确率（natural accuracy）作为性能衡量标准。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法。仅声称该方法相比先前防御方法能一致缓解后门攻击且对自然性能影响可忽略。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但可推断：该方法仅针对训练时后门防御，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或模型轻量化，与本研究方向无直接关联。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。核心集选择（coreset selection）思想在理论上可用于轻量化训练数据筛选，但本文针对的是后门防御而非模型压缩或加速，且未涉及遥感场景。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Recent training-time defenses against neural backdoors isolate a benign subset from poisoned training data, to learn a backdoor-free model from it. In this paper, we formulate this defense strategy as a coreset selection problem, giving rise to so-called "Anti-Backdoor Coreset Selection." Since poisonous samples have (a) lower prediction uncertainty and are (b) less frequent than benign samples, coreset selection naturally focuses more on samples associated with benign functionality than the backdoor functionality. We use the Cumulative Entropy as selection criterion to further facilitate this effect. The metric tracks the learning dynamics of training samples and allowing us to select benign samples with high informativeness for the coreset. Additionally, we unlearn the chosen samples in each epoch to facilitate the separability between benign and poisonous samples. Together, this yields an exceptionally effective training-time defense that constructs a benign coreset to train a backdoor-free model. Unlike prior defenses that compromise natural accuracy and fail against certain attacks, our method mitigates backdooring attacks consistently with a negligible impact on natural performance.

</details>


### [172] [AMPBench-MT: A Homology-Controlled Benchmark for Antimicrobial Peptide Potency, Spectrum, and Safety Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25518)
*Ziheng Zhou,Huiyu Luo,Xiaohu Zhu,Nan Wang,Xuebiao Qin,Chaoyan Zhang,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个面向抗菌肽（AMP）的多任务基准测试AMPBench-MT，用于评估AMP识别、物种条件效力、溶血性、毒性和选择性等多维度性能，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文研究的是计算生物学/生物信息学领域的抗菌肽（AMP）发现与多任务基准构建，涉及AMP/non-AMP识别、物种条件效力回归、溶血性/毒性/选择性评估等。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化相关的内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：抗菌肽（AMP）二元识别、物种条件效力回归（pMIC）、端点特异性效力与安全性评估。应用场景：计算生物学/药物发现。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：AMP/非AMP肽序列。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及使用了frozen protein-language-model embeddings（冻结蛋白质语言模型嵌入）、graph and classical regressors（图模型和经典回归器）作为评估方法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AMPBench-MT（自建基准，基于规范肽记录）。评价指标：二元识别性能、pMIC回归误差、端点特异性效力与安全性指标（低毒性、HC50溶血性、选择性等）。共161个端点特异性模型评估。

Experiments: 主要发现：高二元识别性能不能可靠指示测定端点行为；冻结蛋白质语言模型嵌入在pMIC误差上表现领先；图模型和经典回归器表现接近；在负样本稀缺时，PR导向指标可能具有误导性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性信息。摘要未说明模型效率、部署约束等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无任何可借鉴点。研究方向完全不同（计算生物学 vs. 遥感计算机视觉），技术栈（肽序列分析 vs. 遥感图像目标检测）、评价体系（生物活性指标 vs. mAP/FLOPs/延迟）、应用场景（药物发现 vs. 星上智能处理）均无交集。

Abstract: Computational AMP discovery is often evaluated through AMP/non-AMP recognition, yet follow-up decisions depend on assay-derived evidence such as target-species potency, hemolysis, toxicity, and selectivity. Existing AMP and peptide benchmarks cover binary recognition, multilabel annotation, assay regression, or broader peptide-model comparison, but they do not jointly place AMP recognition, species-conditioned potency, spectrum, safety-facing proxy endpoints, and cross-endpoint behavior within one sequence-homology-controlled protocol. To address this problem, we introduce AMPBench-MT, a provenance-preserving benchmark that standardizes canonical peptide records and organizes them into binary recognition, species-conditioned pMIC regression, and endpoint-specific potency and safety-facing readouts. Across 161 endpoint-specific model evaluations, high binary performance does not reliably indicate assay-endpoint behavior. Frozen protein-language-model embeddings form the leading pMIC error cluster, while graph and classical regressors remain close. Spectrum labels further reveal that PR-oriented metrics can be misleading under scarce observed negatives, whereas low-toxicity, HC50 hemolysis, and selectivity expose smaller but more assay-facing signals. AMPBench-MT shows that AMP evaluation should move beyond recognition leaderboards toward endpoint-aware evidence auditing. Our proposed benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ZihengZhou06/AMPBench-MT.

</details>


### [173] [Multi-Scale Structural Features for Continual, Comprehensible Visual Recognition in a Developmental Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25531)
*Zeki Doruk Erden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于发育学习的无梯度持续学习框架，通过多尺度视觉特征表示改进形状识别，在类增量MNIST上实现无需回放缓冲区的持续学习。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于持续学习(continual learning)这一通用机器学习问题，使用MNIST手写数字数据集，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然持续学习对在轨模型自适应更新有潜在价值，但论文本身未涉及遥感场景或轻量化部署。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。该方法本身是无梯度的，但论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs或推理速度等轻量化指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。无梯度特性可能对低功耗部署有潜在优势，但论文未就此展开讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST（类增量设置）；评价指标：分类准确率(accuracy)、持续学习中的知识保留能力(retention)；实验设置：类增量学习场景，无任务边界预设。
