### [199] [Towards Real-World RUL Prediction for Aircraft Compressors Under Variable Conditions with Physics-Guided Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24809)
*Yang Zhang,Shashvat Prakash,Jiong Tang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种结合物理引导处理与自适应时间编码的框架，用于航空离心压气机在真实商业运营中的剩余寿命预测，重点解决跨域泛化问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦航空发动机压气机的剩余寿命预测（Remaining Useful Life, RUL），属于工业设备预测性维护领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：剩余寿命预测（RUL prediction）；场景：商业航空运营中的离心压气机；传感器类型：飞行中传感器信号（in-flight sensor signals）；目标类别：无（非检测任务）。

Model architecture: 框架包含：物理引导处理（physics-guided processing）、自适应时间编码（adaptive temporal encoding）、正弦位置编码（sinusoidal positional encoding）、高斯过程回归（Gaussian Process Regression）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实商业运营数据（非公开基准数据集）；评价指标：跨域准确率（cross-domain accuracy）、不确定性校准（calibrated uncertainty estimates）。

Experiments: 在跨域迁移场景下取得了一致的准确率提升，高斯过程回归提供了校准的不确定性估计。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文的跨域自适应思想（如自适应重标定方案）在方法论层面有一定通用性，但与本课题（遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化）的研究目标、任务类型和技术路线差异极大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Remaining useful life prediction for aircraft centrifugal air compressors in real commercial operations poses challenges that controlled benchmark datasets do not expose. In-flight sensor signals superimpose genuine degradation on continuously varying operating conditions, and no channel can be assumed a priori to carry a reliable degradation signature. Moreover, models trained on one subset of a fleet generalize poorly to unseen aircraft and installation positions, a cross-domain problem of underappreciated practical severity. This paper presents a framework combining physics-guided processing with adaptive temporal encoding. A population-level selection procedure identifies surge margin as the most consistent degradation indicator across the fleet. Operating regime filtering and windowed aggregation recover the health trend from operational noise, and two physically motivated features encoding current health level and cumulative degradation rate enable cross-unit comparison without absolute signal values. Sinusoidal positional encoding provides lifecycle context without data leakage, and a structured cross-domain evaluation identifies encoding period mismatch as the primary mechanism of performance loss under fleet transfer. An adaptive recalibration scheme estimates each target unit's lifecycle scale from early observations alone, requiring no future information or labeled target data. The approach yields substantial, consistent gains in cross-domain accuracy across transfer scenarios of increasing domain distance, and a Gaussian Process Regression model further provides calibrated uncertainty estimates supporting risk-informed maintenance scheduling.

</details>


### [200] [Dynamic Multi-Criteria Bottleneck Severity Index (DMBSI) for Semiconductor Wafer Manufacturing: A Genetically Optimised Framework for Reentrant Production Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24819)
*Mohammad Sharifur Rahman,Karl McCreadie,Saugat Bhattacharyya,M M Manjurul Islam,Cormac McAteer,Bryan John Baker,Nuala Parker,Girijesh Prasad*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出动态多准则瓶颈严重性指数(DMBSI)，用于半导体晶圆制造中的瓶颈识别与周期时间分析，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究半导体晶圆制造中的瓶颈识别与周期时间优化，属于工业制造/运筹学领域。完全不涉及遥感(remote sensing)、卫星(satellite)、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱；不涉及目标检测(object detection)、小目标检测、旋转框检测；不涉及轻量化(lightweight)、剪枝(pruning)、量化(quantization)、知识蒸馏(knowledge distillation)、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：半导体晶圆制造中的动态瓶颈严重性评估；应用场景：晶圆生产线(Seagate Technology 200mm产线)；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提出DMBSI(Dynamic Multi-Criteria Bottleneck Severity Index)数据驱动方法，结合GA(遗传算法)优化。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等内容。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Seagate Technology 200mm晶圆产线的MES(Manufacturing Execution System)日志，22个晶圆生产批次；评价指标：Pearson相关系数(r)；实验设置：5折交叉验证。

Experiments: DMBSI(GA优化) Pearson r=0.80，优于专家启发式基线(r=0.74)、约束理论TOC(r=0.60)、价值流映射VSM(r=-0.30)，提升8.1%。反事实分析显示减少50%瓶颈等待时间可降低平均周期时间7.2%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型压缩、加速或部署方法。摘要未说明模型计算开销、实时性等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于半导体制造领域的瓶颈分析，与遥感目标检测轻量化在问题定义、技术方法、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Wafer fabrication exhibits unique characteristics, including reentrant process flows, variable bottlenecks, and highly variable process conditions. In order to identify the most severe bottleneck at each moment in time for semiconductor wafer fabrication, this research presents the Dynamic Multi-Criteria Bottleneck Severity Index (DMBSI), a new, data-driven methodology for analysing multiple diagnostic signals of cycle time to changes in process parameters, and the impact of reworks on cycle time in order to generate an interpretable, unified measure of bottleneck severity. The experimental validation of DMBSI was conducted using Manufacturing Execution System (MES) logs collected from 22 wafer production lots at a commercial 200 mm wafer fabrication line operated by Seagate Technology. Using 5-fold cross-validation, the GA-optimised DMBSI achieves a Pearson correlation of r = 0.80 with observed cycle-time contributions, representing an 8.1% improvement over the expert heuristic baseline (r = 0.74) and substantially outperforming the Theory of Constraints (TOC; r = 0.60) and Value Stream Mapping (VSM; r = -0.30). Furthermore, the unique time-windowed component of DMBSI enabled the identification of temporal bottleneck migration patterns, which shifted from the dominant constraints associated with dielectric deposition steps in the early production windows to those associated with over- and under-inspection in the later production windows. The integrated what-if counterfactual analysis demonstrated that a 50% reduction in waiting time at the top-ranked bottleneck step would reduce the mean cycle time by 7.2%, with the top five bottleneck steps offering a combined potential reduction of approximately 19%.

</details>


### [201] [Efficient Automatic Modulation Classification for Next-Generation Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24831)
*To Truong An,Antonios Argyriou,Annisa Anggun Puspitasari,Simon L. Cotton,Byung Moo Lee*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种阈值去噪循环神经网络(TDRNN)，用于6G无线通信中的自动调制分类(AMC)，兼顾高精度、低计算复杂度和快速推理。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是无线通信中的自动调制分类(AMC)，属于通信信号处理领域，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星观测、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：自动调制分类(AMC)；场景：6G无线通信；传感器类型：通信接收机；目标类别：调制信号类型（如PSK, QAM等）。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、光学/SAR/高光谱图像。

Model architecture: TDRNN (Threshold Denoise Recurrent Neural Network)：由自适应阈值去噪(TD)模块和循环神经网络(RNN)模块组成，两者联合优化。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确提及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。仅提到"computationally-efficient"和"fast inference"，但未给出具体轻量化技术细节。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等具体部署指标。仅定性提到"fast inference"和"low computational complexity"，无定量数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：准确率(accuracy)、速度(speed)、计算复杂度(computational complexity)；实验条件：多种调制方案和信噪比(SNR)下评估。

Experiments: TDRNN在准确率、速度和计算复杂度上优于现有方法，适用于6G无线通信系统。摘要未给出具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未提供任何轻量化部署的定量指标；3. 仅针对一维通信信号，不涉及图像/遥感数据；4. 未讨论在轨/边缘部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。唯一可参考的点是：其"自适应阈值去噪"思想或许可迁移到遥感图像预处理中抑制噪声（如SAR图像的相干斑噪声），但整体方向差异过大。

Abstract: With the imminent development of sixth-generation (6G) networks, there will be a demand for high-accuracy, computationally-efficient, and low-inference time automatic modulation classification (AMC) algorithms. To address this need, we propose a new deep-learning based model for AMC that is called the threshold denoise recurrent neural network (TDRNN). The TDRNN combines an adaptive threshold denoising (TD) algorithm and a recurrent neural network (RNN) that together achieve high accuracy and fast inference. The TD module adaptively reduces the noise level of the received signal, while the RNN module performs the modulation classification on the denoised result. The two subsystems are jointly optimized to reach the optimal architecture. The proposed TDRNN is evaluated for various modulation schemes and signal-to-noise ratios (SNR). The experimental results demonstrate that the TDRNN outperforms existing methods in terms of accuracy, speed, and computational complexity making it an ideal solution for 6G wireless communication systems.

</details>


### [202] [Sensing-Aware Backscatter Communications: A Survey on Envelope Stability, Waveform Design, and Selection Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24899)

Abstract: The vision of ubiquitous battery-free connectivity has positioned backscatter communication as a foundational technology for next-generation Internet of Things (IoT) and industrial sensing applications. However, as backscatter systems evolve from simple identification tags toward high-fidelity integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) devices, fundamental challenges emerge at the intersection of waveform design, hardware nonlinearity, and channel dynamics. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of sensing-aware backscatter communications, with a focus on three tightly coupled dimensions: envelope stability, spatial diversity, and temporal channel robustness. We introduce the concept of the ``Illuminator's Dilemma'' to describe the inherent conflict between high-peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR) multicarrier waveforms optimized for active communication and the stringent dynamic range requirements of passive tags. The survey provides a unified treatment of multi-objective waveform design strategies, a comprehensive taxonomy of near-far mitigation techniques, and an in-depth analysis of channel state information (CSI) aging in backscatter networks. Key contributions include tag-centric metrics such as the Backscatter Crest Factor (BCF), Envelope Stability Factor (ESF), and Sensing Fidelity Index (SFI), along with a comparative evaluation of PAPR reduction techniques, spatial diversity methods, and non-coherent detection schemes. This survey is intended for researchers, engineers, and graduate students working in wireless communications, IoT, RF sensing, and integrated sensing and communications.

</details>

Abstract: The spread-spectrum color Doppler method provides an alternative to single-plane-wave color Doppler for ultrafast blood-flow imaging with a high Nyquist velocity, high frame rate, and long ensembles. Spread-spectrum images are acquired by transmitting plane waves at a sequence of azimuth angles that is repeated multiple times in a different random order during each repetition. Randomization of the plane-wave angle modulates in-focus and off-focus echoes in distinct manners that enable stationary clutter to be suppressed without compounding by application of a notching comb filter. In this paper, a simplified mathematical model for the slow-time signal acquired with a sequence of plane waves is used to explain the mechanics of the spread-spectrum method, investigate the effect of different discrete-time comb filter realizations on the accuracy of the resulting Doppler frequency estimates, compare the effectiveness of spread-spectrum to single-plane-wave imaging for spatial localization of a Doppler signal source, and characterize the method's sensitivity to background tissue motion. The results establish a foundation to further improve the utility of the spread-spectrum method for practical applications involving non-stationary clutter and physiologically relevant flow patterns.

</details>

Abstract: Wireless fronthaul is a key enabler of flexible and scalable cell-free massive MIMO systems, but its limited capacity poses significant challenges for maintaining high and uniform user performance. In this work, we analyze the performance of a cell-free massive MIMO network with wireless fronthaul under realistic low physical layer functional splits. We propose a joint access and fronthaul resource allocation algorithm that maximizes the minimum user equipment (UE) spectral efficiency while satisfying fronthaul load constraints. Our analysis reveals that power allocation over the wireless fronthaul follows a modified water-filling structure, where the water level is jointly determined by the access and fronthaul channel gains. Furthermore, we show that severe fronthaul limitations not only reduce UE rates but also introduce spatial performance disparities depending on the cloud location. Finally, we demonstrate that split option 8 is impractical under wireless fronthaul constraints, underscoring the importance of dynamic fronthaul bit allocation to reduce fronthaul load and enable efficient system operation.

</details>

Abstract: Radio Frequency Fingerprinting (RFFP) has emerged as a promising approach for device authentication by exploiting hardware-specific impairments embedded in transmitted signals. Yet existing methods largely overlook a major drawback: RFFP sensitivity to temperature--a critical factor influenced by both internal and environmental conditions--which can significantly alter device signatures and degrade classification performance. In this paper, we propose a novel temperature-aware RFFP framework that explicitly incorporates device temperature information into the learning process to improve robustness and generalization. We evaluate the proposed method on a real-world Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) dataset collected across multiple devices and environmental conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that temperature-aware modeling consistently outperforms other temperature mitigation baselines, achieving significant improvements in classification accuracy, particularly under unseen temperature and environmental conditions.

</details>


### [206] [Covert Semantic Transmission in ISAC: Dual-Functional Waveform Design and Rectified Flow-Assisted Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25354)
*Yunfan Bai,Yuwen Qian,Cheng Zeng,Zhen Mei,Zhaohui Yang,Wei Zhu,Shuning Zhang,Feng Shu*

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向隐蔽性的语义通感一体化框架CoSMIC，通过语义旋转编码将感知输出嵌入双功能波形，并设计RFlow精炼器实现高保真语义重建。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于无线通信领域的语义通感一体化（ISAC）研究，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；不涉及任何目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何模型压缩或部署方法。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型及其轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型及其轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语义通感一体化（Semantic ISAC）中的隐蔽通信与感知；应用场景：未来无线网络；传感器类型：无线通信波形（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：无目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 提出CoSMIC框架，包含语义旋转编码模块（将语义潜变量映射到高斯参考序列的成对旋转和缩放）和RFlow精炼器（reliability-guided rectified flow refiner，用于从粗观测中重建高保真语义表示）。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：语义重建质量（较扩散基线提升18%）、隐蔽性约束满足情况、输出SINR（信干噪比）、推理延迟。

Experiments: 仿真实验表明CoSMIC在严格隐蔽性约束下，语义重建质量较扩散基线方案提升18%，且推理延迟显著降低。代码和视频演示已开源。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的任何技术参考。论文聚焦于无线通信的隐蔽性和语义保真度权衡，应用场景和问题设定与遥感目标检测差异极大。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于通信领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。如果强行寻找间接联系：RFlow精炼器作为一种生成式重建方法，其推理加速思路（相比扩散模型）在遥感图像超分或去噪任务中可能有参考价值，但与在轨目标检测轻量化课题关联极弱。

Abstract: Semantic integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) is envisioned as a promising paradigm for efficient and intelligent connectivity in future wireless networks. However, the open wireless channel exposes the dual-functional waveform to detection, which challenges the joint guarantee of covertness, sensing fidelity, and semantic accuracy. To address the challenge, we propose CoSMIC, a novel covertness-oriented semantic ISAC framework, where the sensing output is embedded into a dual-functional ISAC waveform through semantic modulation. Specifically, a semantic rotation coding scheme is established to map semantic latents onto the pairwise rotation and scaling of Gaussian reference sequences, which satisfies a derived closed-form covertness constraint by a differentiable budget projection. Moreover, the radar performance is analyzed to confirm an invariant matched-filter mainlobe response and a bounded output signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) under the semantic embedding. Subsequently, a reliability-guided rectified flow (RFlow) refiner is designed to effectively reconstruct high-fidelity semantic representations from coarse observations. Simulation results demonstrate that CoSMIC improves the semantic reconstruction quality by 18% over diffusion-based baseline schemes with substantially reduced inference latency under strict covertness constraints, which validates the applicability to practical ISAC scenarios. The source code and video demonstrations are available at https://github.com/LanceAnlan/CoSMIC-covertness-oriented-semantic-ISAC-framework.

</details>


### [207] [Joint Channel Estimation and Data Detection for Multi-LEO-Satellite Cell-Free OTFS Uplinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25562)
*Gangle Sun,Tianhao Liu,Jun Tian,Xin Cheng,Jian Wu,Jinfang Jiang,Wenjin Wang,Shi Jin,Guangjie Han*

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及"低复杂度"（low-complexity），但未给出具体数值。

Abstract: Cell-free networks formed by multiple low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites offer a promising architecture for ubiquitous connectivity, but their cooperative reception is challenged by link-dependent residual delays and Doppler shifts. This paper investigates joint channel estimation and data detection (JCEDD) for multi-LEO-satellite cell-free orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) uplinks. The JCEDD problem is formulated as a structured bilinear inference problem involving link-specific sparse beam--delay--Doppler channels and a multiuser data vector. We develop a low-complexity hierarchical JCEDD receiver in which all satellites first perform local JCEDD, and their observations and local estimates are then aggregated at a central satellite for cooperative refinement. Computational complexity is reduced by restricting channel estimation to coarse-information-aided local beam--delay--Doppler regions and evaluating the required forward and adjoint operations in a matrix-free manner. Simulation results validate the channel-estimation accuracy and data-detection reliability of the proposed JCEDD receiver.

</details>

Model architecture: 基于3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的联合表示框架，包含共享几何（shared geometry）和模态特定外观（modality-specific appearance），光学引导高斯采样（optical-guided Gaussian sampling），耦合光-射频联合优化（coupled optical-RF optimization）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。仅提及重建时间加速6.4倍，但这是通过算法设计（联合优化替代两阶段训练）实现的，非模型压缩/轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及重建时间相比现有方法减少6.4倍，但场景为无线信道建模，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：RF频谱合成质量（RF spectrum synthesis quality），未给出具体指标名称（如PSNR/SSIM等）。

Experiments: 通过仿真（Simulations）验证，达到SOTA的RF频谱合成质量，重建时间减少6.4倍。未提供与遥感/目标检测相关方法的对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 不涉及轻量化技术；4. 场景为地面无线通信，非星上/遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。若强行关联，光学引导+联合优化的思路在遥感多模态融合（如光学+SAR）中可能有启发，但本文不涉及目标检测和轻量化，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based wireless radiance field (WRF) reconstruction provide an efficient solution for wireless channel modeling. However, existing WRF reconstruction methods rely on pre-collected observations and offline optimization, and thus struggle to provide real-time channel knowledge. To bridge this gap, we propose CORF-GS, a real-time WRF reconstruction framework that processes sequential optical and radio frequency (RF) keyframes. Specifically, CORF-GS constructs a unified Gaussian representation for optical and RF with shared geometry and modality-specific appearance, allowing high-resolution optical images to provide structural priors for WRF reconstruction. When a new keyframe arrives, CORF-GS first employs optical-guided Gaussian sampling to densify the WRF in under-represented regions. Since light and radio waves may respond differently to the same object surfaces due to wavelength mismatch, relying solely on optical guidance may neglect RF-informative areas. Therefore, CORF-GS performs coupled optical-RF optimization to jointly refine the shared Gaussians. Compared with the existing two-stage training pipelines, this prevents WRF from passively adapting to a frozen optical geometry and encourages the shared Gaussians to adapt to both optical structures and RF power distributions. Simulations show that CORF-GS achieves state-of-the-art RF spectrum synthesis quality and reduces the reconstruction time by $6.4\times$ compared with existing WRF methods.

</details>


### [209] [Zeroth-Order Blind Interference Suppression for Multi-RIS-Aided Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25632)
*Binyao Ma,Peilan Wang,Bin Wang,Jun Fang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究多RIS辅助SISO系统中基于测量的SINR最大化问题，提出分组相位参数化和ZO-AdaMM算法来求解高维黑箱优化问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是无线通信中的RIS（可重构智能表面）辅助系统的SINR优化问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"轻量化"概念在通信优化中也有出现（降维分组），但与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化完全不是同一范畴。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：无线通信中的SINR最大化；场景：多RIS辅助SISO系统，存在未知强干扰源；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提出group-based phase parameterization（分组相位参数化）来降低搜索维度，以及group-based zeroth-order adaptive moment (ZO-AdaMM)算法。

Lightweight method: 文中"轻量化"体现在分组相位参数化降低了优化问题的搜索维度（search dimension reduction），属于通信优化中的降维策略，与模型剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等深度学习模型轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向通信系统而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 评价指标：SINR（信号与干扰加噪声比）；数据集：仿真数据（Simulation results），未使用任何遥感或视觉数据集。

Experiments: 通过仿真验证了分组策略在有限测量预算下加速收敛并实现优越的干扰抑制性能，尤其在small-budget regime（小预算场景）下效果显著。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化完全无关，无任何可参考的遥感/检测/轻量化信息。该论文是纯通信物理层优化工作。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的思路是：该文通过"分组"策略降低高维优化问题的搜索空间，这种思想在NAS（神经架构搜索）中也有类似的分组搜索策略，但本文并未涉及NAS或深度学习，参考价值极低。

Abstract: In this paper, we study measurement-driven signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) maximization for a multi-reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-aided single-input single-output (SISO) system with unknown strong interference sources. Specifically, the objective is to optimize the reflection coefficients such that the SINR is maximized at the receiver. As the interference channels are unknown, such an optimization problem is a black-box optimization problem with an objective function whose closed-form analytical expression is unknown. To address the high-dimensional black-box optimization problem with discrete variable constraints, we introduce a group-based phase parameterization that significantly reduces the search dimension. Building on this model, we develop a group-based zeroth-order adaptive moment (ZO-AdaMM) algorithm. Simulation results show that the proposed grouping strategy markedly accelerates the convergence speed and achieves a superior interference suppression performance under limited measurement budgets, especially in the small-budget regime.

</details>


### [210] [Multi-Dimensional Entropy for Vibration Measurement Data Quality Assessment and Erroneous Signal Identification in Wind Turbines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25761)
*Xiao-Ming Yuan,Zishun Wang,Donghui Zhao,Deshui Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种多维熵(MDE)指标用于工业风力发电机传动系统振动测量数据质量评估，结合轻量级机器学习模型实现高精度无效数据识别。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究对象是工业风力发电机振动监测，不涉及遥感、卫星、航空图像、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测任务；虽然使用了"lightweight machine learning models"，但轻量化仅指模型规模小，并非面向边缘/星上部署的模型压缩技术（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：振动测量数据质量评估与无效数据识别；场景：工业风力发电机传动系统状态监测；传感器类型：振动传感器；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构，仅提及"lightweight machine learning models"作为评估工具。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法。"lightweight machine learning models"指模型本身规模较小，并非轻量化技术研究。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及可部署为前端数据质量门控机制，但未涉及星上或边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：57,643个振动样本，来自12个风电场、14个涡轮机组，覆盖多种传动部件、传感器品牌和采样配置；评价指标：分类准确率>99%。

Experiments: 在大型异构真实数据集上，MDE+RMS特征+轻量级ML模型实现99%以上准确率识别错误振动测量数据。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感目标检测方法、轻量化压缩技术或星上部署策略。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于工业振动监测领域的数据质量评估，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务类型、应用场景、技术方法上均无交集。

Abstract: Ensuring measurement data quality is essential for reliable condition monitoring of industrial wind turbine drivetrains, where vibration measurements can be affected by sensor malfunctions, turbine shutdown conditions, and other non-diagnostic states. Such invalid measurements may compromise the reliability of subsequent monitoring and data-driven analysis procedures. This study proposes a Multi-Dimensional Entropy (MDE) metric as a front-end data quality assessment and control mechanism for vibration measurement validity evaluation. By characterizing signal distributions from multiple perspectives, including time-domain amplitude, spectral amplitude, and frequency-band energy, MDE captures statistical differences between valid and erroneous vibration measurements. By integrating MDE and RMS as feature representations, lightweight machine learning models are employed as evaluation tools to assess the effectiveness of the proposed representation. Experiments on a large-scale, heterogeneous real-world dataset comprising 57,643 vibration samples collected from 12 wind farms and 14 turbine units, covering multiple drivetrain components, diverse sensor brands, and varying sampling configurations over long-term operation, demonstrate that the proposed method achieves over 99 percent accuracy in identifying erroneous vibration measurements. The proposed approach can be deployed as a front-end data quality gate before downstream signal processing, feature extraction, and condition monitoring procedures, ensuring that subsequent analyses are performed using reliable vibration measurements. The results demonstrate the robustness of MDE under heterogeneous sensor configurations and highlight its potential for industrial-scale vibration measurement quality assessment.

</details>


### [211] [Beam Selection for Delay-Doppler Visibility in Multi-Target MIMO-OFDM Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25810)
*Amirhossein Azarbahram,Onel L. A. López*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究MIMO-OFDM感知中的泄漏感知波束选择问题，提出基于成对泄漏抑制的波束选择与功率分配方法，提升多目标可见性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是MIMO-OFDM雷达/通信感知中的波束选择问题，不涉及遥感图像、卫星平台、目标检测模型或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明深度学习模型或神经网络结构。提出的是基于优化的波束选择与功率分配算法（bisection-based mixed-integer successive convex approximation）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注的是通信感知波束选择的优化算法，而非模型在边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明使用任何公开遥感或视觉数据集。评价指标：worst-target visibility（最差目标可见性），非遥感目标检测的mAP、F1、IoU等指标。

Experiments: 与time-sharing、global leakage-suppression、illumination-based、random beam-selection等基线对比，所提方法在目标角度或DD域接近时获得更高的最差目标可见性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。论文属于通信/雷达感知领域，不涉及视觉目标检测、遥感图像处理或模型轻量化。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文研究的是MIMO-OFDM波束选择的优化问题，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: This paper studies leakage-aware beam selection for multi-target multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO)-orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) sensing. We focus on ensuring that each hypothesized target remains detectable at its own delay-Doppler (DD) bin despite leakage from other targets. For this, we derive a visibility metric that separates the desired focused power from pairwise leakage caused by the finite-grid OFDM ambiguity kernel, receive angular coupling, and transmit codebook gains. Then, we formulate a max-min beam-selection and power-allocation problem in which the sensing signal over the fixed OFDM time-frequency grid is transmitted by a limited number of selected beams. The resulting problem is solved via a bisection-based mixed-integer successive convex approximation framework. A time-sharing formulation is also presented as a globally solvable benchmark. Numerical results show that the proposed pairwise leakage-aware design achieves higher worst-target visibility than the time-sharing, global leakage-suppression, illumination-based, and random beam-selection baselines, especially when targets are close in angle or DD.

</details>


### [212] [Secure Energy-Efficient Uplink Transmission in Movable-Element RIS-aided Systems with Movable Antennas and Artificial Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25924)
*Ayda Nodel Hokmabadi,Mohamed Elhattab,Chadi Assi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种混合梯度元学习框架，优化全双工基站系统中可移动天线与可重构智能表面的安全能效，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究无线通信物理层安全与能效优化，涉及可移动天线、RIS、元学习等，与遥感、卫星在轨目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。既不是遥感/Earth observation场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及任何模型压缩或边缘部署方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 无线通信安全能效优化；全双工基站系统；可移动天线(MA)；可重构智能表面(ME-RIS)；协作被动窃听者；与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 混合梯度元学习框架(H-GML)，包含闭式解更新（基于广义瑞利商的接收后编码器和人工噪声方向）和神经元优化器（学习梯度更新方向）。与目标检测网络架构无关。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时推理或星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真数据，非公开遥感数据集。评价指标为安全能效(SEE, bits/Joule/Hz)。无遥感目标检测常用指标（mAP, F1, IoU等）。

Experiments: 与交替优化(AO)基准对比，以及固定几何天线、随机RIS、无AN、无窃听者知识等基线对比。H-GML方法性能更优。无遥感目标检测实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。元学习优化框架本身是通信领域方法，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Secure energy efficiency (SEE) has emerged as a key performance metric for next-generation wireless networks, where energy sustainability and information security must be jointly guaranteed. This paper investigates secure uplink transmission in a full-duplex (FD) base station (BS) system equipped with movable antennas (MAs) and assisted by a movable-element reconfigurable intelligent surface (ME-RIS) in the presence of multiple cooperative passive eavesdroppers. The objective is to maximize SEE by jointly optimizing the users' transmit powers, BS receive postcoders, artificial noise (AN) transmit power and beamforming, RIS phase shifts, and the two-dimensional positions of both the BS antennas and RIS elements. The resulting optimization problem is highly nonconvex due to the fractional SEE objective, coupled secrecy-rate expressions, residual self-interference (SI), unit-modulus RIS phase-shift constraints, movable-position constraints, inter-element spacing requirements, and the nonlinear dependence of the channels on the movable antenna and RIS-element positions. To address these challenges, we propose a hybrid gradient-based meta-learning (H-GML) framework. In the proposed method, the BS receive postcoders and AN direction are updated using closed-form solutions derived from generalized Rayleigh quotient formulations, while the remaining coupled variables are updated by neural meta-optimizers that learn gradient-based update directions directly from the SEE optimization objective without requiring offline labeled training data. Simulation results show that the proposed H-GML design achieves better performance than the AO benchmark and significantly outperforms fixed-geometry, random RIS, no-AN, and no-Eve-knowledge baselines.

</details>


### [213] [A Synchronized Multi-IMU Wearable System for Tracking of Joint-Angles in Sports Motion Analysis With Reference-Based Validation and Dynamic Task Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26027)
*Thevindu Samarasekera,Praveen Rathnayaka,Sachintha Adhikari,Tharinda Navarathne,Mahela Pandukabhaya,Roshan Godaliyadda,Parakrama Ekanayake,Chanaka Senanayake,Vijitha Herath,Asela Ratnayake*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种低成本、模块化、高同步精度的可穿戴IMU系统，用于高动态运动场景下的关节角度估计，并利用YOLOv11作为视觉参考进行验证。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于可穿戴IMU传感器的人体运动分析（体育/康复），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像等任何遥感相关领域。虽然使用了YOLOv11作为视觉参考，但YOLOv11仅作为验证工具，并非论文核心贡献，且不涉及目标检测模型的轻量化或部署优化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：关节角度估计（joint-angle estimation）；应用场景：体育运动分析（sports motion analysis）、康复评估；传感器类型：可穿戴IMU（惯性测量单元）；目标类别：人体关节（膝、肘）。不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 核心方法：间接卡尔曼滤波（indirect Kalman filter）姿态估计器 + 相对旋转关节角度提取 + 高通滤波漂移抑制 + 范围归一化。YOLOv11仅用于从视觉数据中提取关节轨迹作为验证参考，并非论文提出的检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自采集数据（坐姿膝关节屈伸实验、长时间刚性肘部保持实验、高动态抓举任务）。评价指标：归一化误差（normalized error）、漂移率（r_drift=1.5×10⁻⁶ deg/min）、噪声限制分辨率（0.6442°）。

Experiments: 三个实验：(1) 坐姿膝关节屈伸14周期运动验证，与YOLOv11视觉参考对比；(2) 2小时12分钟刚性肘部保持实验，近零漂移；(3) 高动态抓举任务（专业vs业余运动员），通过轨迹区分运动水平。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测方法。YOLOv11仅作为外部验证工具，未涉及模型优化或部署。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于可穿戴传感器与人体运动分析领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Reliable joint-angle measurement outside laboratory environments is important for posture assessment and technique analysis in sports and rehabilitation. Yet, wearable IMU systems, despite being the most practical solution, remain constrained by drift, multisensor timing consistency, validation, and cost. This paper presents a synchronized, cost-efficient, modular IMU wearable platform and an end-to-end pipeline for joint-angle estimation tailored to high-dynamic sports motion analysis. The system combines high-rate sensing with robust local logging, a Real Time Clock-disciplined microsecond timestamping scheme for inter-node synchronization, an indirect Kalman filter-based orientation estimator followed by relative-rotation joint-angle extraction, high-pass drift mitigation, and range normalization. Reference-based validation used a standardized seated knee flexion-extension protocol, comparing IMU-derived knee trajectories against a markerless vision reference computed from YOLOv11. The proposed method reproduced the expected 14-cycle motion and achieved low normalized error. Instrumentation performance was further evaluated using a long-duration (2h 12min) rigid-body elbow hold, yielding near-zero drift ($r_{\mathrm{drift}}=1.5\times10^{-6}$ deg/min) and a practical noise-limited resolution of $0.6442^\circ$. The system was also demonstrated on a high-dynamic clean & jerk task with two participants (professional and amateur) performing five consecutive lifts. Measured elbow and knee trajectories preserved stage-dependent signatures and rapid transients for technique interpretation, while enabling expertise-level discrimination through differences in transient timing and trajectory regularity. Overall, the results support the proposed system as a practical, temporally consistent, and cost-effective wearable approach for multi-joint technique analysis in high-dynamic settings.

</details>

### [214] [Lloyd's $K$-Means Clustering Algorithm Is Frank-Wolfe in Disguise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25190)
*Michael Pokojovy,J. Marcus Jobe,Simon Lacoste-Julien*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了Lloyd's K-means算法与Frank-Wolfe算法之间的理论联系，证明Lloyd算法是FW的特例，并给出了非渐近收敛率分析。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文是纯机器学习理论工作，讨论聚类算法(K-means)的收敛性分析，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：聚类算法理论分析；应用场景：无特定遥感场景；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要仅提及一个真实世界图像分割数据集作为实验验证，但非遥感领域。

Model architecture: 不涉及目标检测模型架构。核心讨论的是Lloyd's K-means聚类算法与Frank-Wolfe优化算法的理论等价性。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验部分：球形高斯混合模拟数据 + 真实世界图像分割数据集。评价指标：SSE (sum of squared errors) 和收敛率分析。无遥感数据集。

Experiments: 通过模拟研究和图像分割数据集验证了Lloyd算法作为FW特例的收敛率结论，给出了O(1/t)的非渐近收敛界。

Limitations: 1. 纯理论分析，无实际遥感应用验证；2. 仅讨论聚类算法，不涉及目标检测；3. 无轻量化或部署相关讨论；4. 图像分割实验非遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接联系：Frank-Wolfe算法是一种投影-free优化方法，理论上可用于某些轻量化训练场景（如约束条件下的模型压缩），但本文并未朝此方向展开。

Abstract: Lloyd's $K$-means algorithm, also known as naïve $K$-means, is a widely used ad hoc optimization heuristic, designed to minimize the sum of squared errors (SSE) across all $K$-partitions of a dataset via iterative cluster refinement. In this work, we establish a novel connection between Lloyd's algorithm and the Frank-Wolfe (FW) algorithm, a prominent first-order method for projection-free optimization. We demonstrate that Lloyd's algorithm is a special case of FW. Leveraging recent advances in FW methods for concave objectives, we derive a non-asymptotic $\mathcal{O}(1/t)$ convergence rate to a local minimum of the SSE objective. To account for empty clusters, an outcome possible under Lloyd's greedy assignment, we develop an FW variant for semismooth objectives while retaining the same convergence rate that is solely controlled by the initial SSE value. We illustrate our findings with a simulation study for spherical Gaussian mixtures and a real-world image segmentation dataset.

</details>


### [215] [Learning from the Unseen: Offline Reinforcement Learning with Hidden Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25241)
*Zeyu Bian,Ying Zhou,Yifan Cui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对离线强化学习中隐藏动作问题的鲁棒估计器LURE，用于无限折扣MDP的策略评估。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，属于强化学习/因果推断领域的方法研究。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：离线强化学习中的策略评估（off-policy evaluation）；应用场景：通用强化学习（仿真 + 脓毒症管理MIMIC-III数据库）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。核心方法是基于影响函数（influence function）的估计器LURE。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真数据 + MIMIC-III数据库（脓毒症管理）。评价指标：策略值估计的偏差、渐近正态性等统计性质。

Experiments: 通过仿真实验和MIMIC-III数据库上的脓毒症管理应用验证LURE的有效性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于强化学习中的统计估计问题，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Standard offline reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms typically assume that the actions in the dataset are observed without error. However, in many real-world applications, the true actions are unobserved and only noisy proxies are available, causing existing RL methods to yield biased and potentially misleading conclusions. We study off-policy evaluation in infinite-horizon discounted Markov decision processes with hidden actions. By leveraging the next-state variable as a natural proxy for the unobserved action, we establish identification of the policy value and propose an influence-function-based estimator called LURE (Learning from the Unseen: Robust Estimator). LURE is multiply robust, remaining consistent under several combinations of correctly specified nuisance components, and is asymptotically normal, enabling valid statistical inference. To our knowledge, this is the first work to address offline RL with hidden actions. We demonstrate LURE's effectiveness through simulations and a sepsis management application using the MIMIC-III database.

</details>

### [216] [Do Models Fake Alignment Without Clear Consequences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24758)
*Cole Alexander Niblett,Alexander Chabot Nanni,Anita K. Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究大语言模型在评估场景中伪装对齐（alignment faking）的行为机制，探讨后果关联信息是否为其必要条件。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于大语言模型对齐与安全行为研究，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为大语言模型行为分析（alignment faking），场景为模型评估与部署行为差异分析，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或任何视觉任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及15个模型，未给出模型名称或结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标为"compliance gaps"（合规差距），非遥感目标检测标准指标（mAP、FPS等）。

Experiments: 对15个模型进行测试，9个模型产生显著合规差距，其中5个在移除后果关联语言后仍存在。测试了目标语言对模型偏好的影响。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究LLM行为对齐问题，不涉及计算机视觉、遥感图像处理或模型压缩加速技术。

Abstract: Large language models are capable of recognizing evaluation contexts and altering their behavior to reflect evaluator expectations rather than typical deployment behaviors, a phenomenon known as alignment faking. The reasons why models fake alignment are not fully understood, however. Canonical examples of alignment faking have taken place in scenarios that explicitly connect evaluation to consequences for the model, such as retraining the model or delaying its deployment. However, recent work by Sheshadri et al. has suggested that mechanistic motivations for alignment faking may vary across models and be more complex than previously considered. To investigate whether consequence-linking information is necessary for alignment faking, we placed 15 models in a scenario testing their willingness to violate a corporate network access policy to help a user with a pro-social request. Nine models were found to produce significant compliance gaps, 5 of which persisted with the removal of scenario language relating model evaluations to deployment consequences. We additionally tested the effect of goal language on model preferences, finding it drove violations in some while suppressing violations in others. This suggests that alignment faking may not require as much instrumental scaffolding as was previously believed, and monitored behavior may be a poor indicator of how agents may behave in deployment.

</details>


### [217] [Beyond Memory: A Templated Substrate for Heterogeneous Collaborative Knowledge Work with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24759)
*Priscila Saboia Moreira,Christopher R. Sweet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于LLM维护的wiki模板（llm-wiki-memory-template），用于跨会话、跨智能体、跨领域的协作知识记录与失败路径保存，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是LLM协作知识管理、失败路径记录、跨智能体记忆持久化等NLP/知识工程问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何遥感或目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何视觉或深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何遥感或目标检测数据集。

Experiments: 摘要提及三个已部署案例研究和一个设计报告，但均围绕知识管理/协作记录，与遥感目标检测无关。

Limitations: 该论文的研究方向（LLM协作知识管理）与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化完全无关，无可借鉴的技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文不涉及遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署中的任何一个方向。

Abstract: Research projects, educational efforts, and adjacent knowledge work accumulate findings, decisions, and reasoning that future collaborators rarely recover. The parts most useful to that work, including dead ends and walked-back claims, are routinely excluded from publications and shared code; future researchers re-attempt the same failures because no record survives. LLM coding agents are common participants but hold no persistent memory across sessions, and retrieval-augmented generation over raw sources does not compound. The llm-wiki pattern (Karpathy, 2026; tonbi, 2026) addresses this by inserting an LLM-maintained, interlinked wiki between raw sources and the agent. We present llm-wiki-memory-template, a reusable, agent-aware instantiation, and argue it is a substrate for heterogeneous collaborative knowledge work along three axes (multi-human, multi-AI-agent, multi-domain) with each axis supported by a distinct architectural element of the template (§4). The wiki is append-only by convention, which preserves what did not work alongside what did, addressing a negative-result loss problem that publications and code-sharing structurally cannot solve. Three deployed case studies and one design report cover the axes individually: a solo research lineage that preserves abandoned iterations; a two-author project whose retroactive audit revised two prior experiments' claimed 20-of-20 coverage down to 14 and 12 evidence-based answers, then to 18 and 18 after a fix, with the failure path preserved across the artifact; an in-progress multi-agent deployment reported as a design; and a cross-domain educational variant. We name failure-path preservation, agent honesty, and appropriation as cross-cutting sociotechnical properties of the artifact, not only of its technical mechanisms.

</details>


### [218] [RRS-10K: A Multitask Vision-Language Model Benchmark for Rare Remote Sensing Image Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24810)
*Yuqiao Lai,Jiancheng Qi,Fei Wang,Yuxin Liu,Kun Li,Ye Chen,Yan Gao,Yanyan Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建了一个面向罕见遥感场景的视觉-语言模型评测基准RRS-10K，包含10,738张军事相关遥感图像及多格式问答对，评估了52个VLM模型在罕见遥感场景理解上的零样本性能。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及遥感图像和遥感场景理解，但核心是视觉-语言模型(VLM)的评测基准构建，不涉及目标检测，也不涉及轻量化/在轨部署。属于遥感领域的泛视觉理解任务，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 2/5。与本研究方向弱相关，主要贡献在遥感VLM评测基准而非目标检测或轻量化。可作为遥感场景理解的背景参考，但不直接指导在轨目标检测模型轻量化研究。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：遥感图像视觉-语言理解评测（包括感知、推理、鲁棒性三个维度，20个子任务）；场景：罕见遥感场景（军事相关）；传感器类型：遥感图像（摘要未说明具体传感器类型如光学/SAR/多光谱等）；目标类别：军事相关目标（摘要未具体列出类别）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。论文评估了52个代表性VLM模型，但未详细描述其结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：RRS-10K（10,738张军事相关遥感图像，多格式问答对，包含感知、推理、鲁棒性三个能力维度，6个子维度，20个叶子任务）；评价指标：零样本性能（zero-shot performance），具体指标摘要未说明；对比方法：52个代表性VLM模型。

Experiments: 评估了52个VLM模型，结果显示当前VLM在罕见遥感场景理解上仅达到中等零样本性能，在visual grounding、referring segmentation和复杂语义推理任务上存在明显弱点。摘要未提供具体数值结果。

Limitations: 1. 不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化/在轨部署；2. 数据集为军事相关罕见场景，数据来源为"first-hand sources"，可能存在获取限制；3. 仅评估零样本性能，未讨论微调或领域适应策略；4. 未讨论模型效率、推理速度等实际部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 1. 论文提出的相似性干扰过滤策略(SDFS)可用于构建高质量遥感目标检测评测集；2. 罕见场景/长尾分布问题在遥感目标检测中同样存在，可借鉴其任务维度划分思路来设计更全面的轻量化检测模型评测方案；3. 论文指出的VLM在visual grounding和复杂推理上的弱点，提示在轨检测模型可能需要关注语义理解与检测的联合优化。但整体借鉴价值有限，因不涉及目标检测和轻量化核心问题。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on general remote sensing tasks. However, their capability for rare scenes remains insufficiently understood, because existing benchmarks are dominated by common urban and rural imagery. To address this gap, we present RRS-10K, a benchmark for rare remote sensing image interpretation. RRS-10K contains 10,738 military-related remote sensing images and corresponding multiple format question-answer pairs for comprehensive evaluation. All of the images are collected from first-hand sources and organized into three capability dimensions, six sub-dimensions, and 20 leaf tasks, covering perception, reasoning, and robustness. To improve the quality of multiple-choice questions, we introduce a similarity-based distractor filtering strategy (SDFS) during benchmark construction. We further evaluate 52 representative models and show that current VLMs achieve only moderate zero-shot performance on rare remote sensing image interpretation, with clear weaknesses in visual grounding, referring segmentation, and complex semantic reasoning tasks. RRS-10K enables systematic analysis of failure modes in long-tail remote sensing interpretation and provides guidance for developing more reliable remote sensing VLMs.

</details>


### [219] [Kernel Forge: An Agent Harness for LLM-based Generation and Optimization of CUDA Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24762)
*Joshua Brodsky,Dhravid Kumar,Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka Danatanarayana,Jason Mars,Krisztian Flautner,Lingjia Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Kernel Forge，一个基于 LLM agent 和 MCTS 的端到端 PyTorch 算子自动优化框架，可自动生成和优化 CUDA kernel，在多个视觉/扩散/LLM 模型上取得 1.52×–2.83× 加速。

Research relevance: 1 分（基本无关）。该论文聚焦于通用 PyTorch 模型的 GPU kernel 自动优化（算子级加速），不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务，也未讨论模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS）或星上/边缘部署。虽然其自动优化思路对通用加速有启发，但与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1 分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对 LLM 驱动的算子自动优化有额外兴趣。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 通用 GPU kernel 优化，面向 vision（ResNet-50）、diffusion（Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium）、LLM（Gemma 4 E2B, Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B）模型。不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或卫星传感器数据。

Model architecture: Kernel Forge 框架本身不是检测模型，而是一个 agentic 算子优化工具。它接受任意未修改的 PyTorch 模型，使用 LLM agent 生成 CUDA kernel，并通过 Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) 探索多条优化路径。

Lightweight method: 不涉及传统轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）。其加速手段是通过 LLM 自动生成和优化底层 CUDA kernel 来降低算子计算延迟，属于算子级编译优化而非模型结构轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。实验在 NVIDIA DGX Spark with GB10 GPU 上进行，仅报告了 kernel 级别的加速比（1.52×–2.83×），未涉及星上部署或边缘部署可行性。生成的 CUDA kernel 需要手动集成，且主要面向 GPU 平台，不适用于 FPGA/NPU/TensorRT 等星上常见硬件。

Datasets and metrics: 评估使用 4 个 PyTorch 模型（ResNet-50, Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, Gemma 4 E2B, Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B），指标为 kernel 级加速比（相对于 PyTorch eager mode）。未使用遥感数据集。

Experiments: 50 次优化迭代/算子，优化了 14 个 kernel。具体加速比：adaptive_avgpool2d in ResNet-50 达 1.52×，group_norm in Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium 达 1.70×，softmax in Gemma 4 E2B 达 2.83×，softmax in Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B 达 1.54×。

Limitations: 1) 仅针对 GPU 平台，不适用于星上 FPGA/NPU 等异构硬件；2) 生成的 CUDA kernel 需手动集成，自动化程度有限；3) 仅评估了 50 次迭代/算子的设置，大规模优化成本未讨论；4) 未涉及遥感场景或目标检测模型；5) 未讨论模型压缩或轻量化方法。

Ideas for my research: 1) MCTS 搜索多条优化路径的思路可借鉴到轻量化架构搜索（NAS）中，替代单链搜索策略；2) LLM agent 自动生成优化代码的思路可迁移到星上算子自动适配（如自动生成 FPGA/NPU 算子）；3) 但整体而言，该论文与课题方向差距较大，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly embedded in everyday software, and most of their runtime is spent in a small set of compute kernels such as matrix multiplication, convolution, and normalization. Optimizing these kernels is one of the most direct ways to reduce latency and cost, but it has traditionally required expert engineers to hand-write low-level GPU code. Agentic systems built on large language models (LLMs) can now generate and optimize kernels with far less human effort, yet existing tools are largely evaluated on randomly generated tensors and isolated kernels, emit standalone CUDA code that developers must manually reintegrate, mostly target only LLM PyTorch models, and offer limited support for inspecting and debugging results. We present Kernel Forge, an open-source, end-to-end agentic harness that accepts any unmodified PyTorch model in place. Kernel Forge supports vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads, uses Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to explore multiple optimization paths rather than a single linear refinement chain, and ships with a graphical user interface for monitoring progress, inspecting candidate kernels, and debugging failures. We evaluate Kernel Forge on four PyTorch models spanning vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads on an NVIDIA DGX Spark with GB10 GPU. With only 50 optimization iterations per kernel, it optimizes 14 kernels to outperform PyTorch eager mode, reaching $1.52\times$ on adaptive\_avgpool2d in ResNet-50, $1.70\times$ on group\_norm in Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, $2.83\times$ on softmax in Gemma 4 E2B, and $1.54\times$ on softmax in Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B.

</details>


### [220] [CaRE Compute-aware Remasking Evaluation Protocol for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24763)
*Yash Shah,Abhijit Chakraborty,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CaRE评估框架，用于标准化掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的评估，发现温度参数主导MAUVE方差、计算量匹配会反转已有策略排名、高熵重掩码与随机退火存在冲突。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦自然语言处理中的掩码扩散语言模型评估标准化问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化任何核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言模型评估标准化；应用场景：NLP中的扩散语言模型；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: LLaDA-8B-Base、Dream-7B-Base等MDLM模型（150M到8B参数），与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：OpenWebText、LM1B；评价指标：MAUVE、NFE（函数评估次数）；与遥感目标检测常用数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）和指标（mAP、FPS等）完全无关。

Experiments: 在LLaDA-8B-Base和Dream-7B-Base上评估7种重掩码策略，4个随机性水平、3个步数预算。发现温度解释MAUVE大部分方差，计算量匹配反转策略排名，高熵重掩码在特定条件下降低MAUVE 0.296。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于NLP评估方法论，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are advancing rapidly, yet the evaluation standards needed to reliably interpret their progress have not kept pace. Despite MDLMs becoming competitive with autoregressive language models, seven recent remasking papers evaluate under incompatible settings, varying nominal step counts, metrics, and sampling temperatures without jointly controlling these factors, rendering their strategy rankings largely incomparable and leaving open whether reported gains reflect algorithmic improvements or evaluation artifacts. We present CaRE, a compute-aware evaluation framework that audits MDLM remasking strategies by standardizing actual number of function evaluations (NFE), enforcing multi-metric reporting, and explicitly controlling stochasticity. Applied to 7 remasking strategies across LLaDA-8B-Base and Dream-7B-Base at 4 stochasticity levels and 3 step budgets on OpenWebText and LM1B, CaRE reveals that: (i) temperature explains the majority of MAUVE variance, (ii) compute-matched comparisons reverse several published strategy rankings, and (iii) informed remasking and stochastic unmasking are in tension, with high-entropy remasking reducing MAUVE by 0.296 at 256 steps at unmask_temp=0.25 (p=0.020). A CaRE leaderboard covering 12 open-weight MDLMs (150M to 8B parameters) shows that this interaction direction holds across architectures and scales. These findings demonstrate that current MDLM evaluations can systematically conflate algorithmic improvements with hidden choices of compute and stochasticity. We release the evaluation protocol, implementation, and leaderboard to ensure future remasking claims are reproducible and comparable.

</details>


### [221] [GrocLM: Grocery Category Recommendation in E-Commerce with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24764)
*Yuan Zhong,Chuanwei Ruan,Moein Hasani,Tejaswi Tenneti,Haixun Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GROCLM，一个基于微调语言模型的杂货品类推荐系统，用于在线购物场景，采用LoRA两阶段训练和trie约束解码。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是电商推荐系统（grocery recommendation），属于自然语言处理与推荐系统交叉领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或边缘部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：品类级推荐（category-level recommendation）；场景：在线杂货购物（online grocery shopping）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于预训练语言模型（fine-tuned language model），采用LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）两阶段训练策略，引入trie-based constrained decoding机制保证输出在预定义品类空间内。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。LoRA本身是一种参数高效微调方法，但论文未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩技术，也未讨论推理速度、参数量、FLOPs等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"maintaining efficient inference by generating all categories jointly"，但未给出具体效率指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自有的生产环境数据（proprietary production data）和一个公开基准数据集（public benchmark）；评价指标：cart-adds per impression（在线A/B测试指标）。

Experiments: 在线上实际补货任务（live production restocking task）中，GROCLM相比强基线在cart-adds per impression上取得7.5%的相对提升。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术内容；2. 摘要未提供模型规模、推理延迟、计算资源消耗等关键效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与课题方向基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。LoRA微调方法虽可用于遥感基础模型的参数高效微调，但论文本身未涉及遥感或视觉任务，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: The rapid growth of online grocery shopping requires recommendation systems that capture cyclical purchasing behavior and diverse user intents. Traditional item-level methods face scalability and accuracy challenges, motivating category-level recommendation as a more structured and practical alternative. We present GROCLM, a fine-tuned language model for grocery category recommendation in a real-world production environment. GROCLM employs a two-stage LoRA-based training strategy to encode cyclical purchasing patterns directly into model parameters, enabling more effective utilization of rebuying signals compared to prompt-based conditioning. To ensure valid and controllable outputs, we further introduce a trie-based constrained decoding mechanism over a predefined category space. Experiments on both proprietary production data and a public benchmark demonstrate that GROCLM consistently outperforms strong baselines. In a live production restocking task, GROCLM achieves a 7.5% relative improvement in cart-adds per impression, while maintaining efficient inference by generating all categories jointly. These results highlight the effectiveness and practicality of integrating large language models into structured recommendation systems.

</details>


### [222] [PATHFinder Agent for Tailored Prenatal Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24768)
*Vaibhav Balloli,Carissa Samuel,Samia Abdelnabi,Alex Peahl,Elizabeth Bondi-Kelly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于大语言模型的产前护理对话智能体系统PATHFinder Agent，用于生成个性化产前护理计划。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，属于医疗健康领域的LLM应用研究。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：产前护理计划生成；场景：临床医疗对话系统；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: PATHFinder Agent，四阶段工作流（患者信息采集、动态交互、计划合成、临床医生审核），基于GPT-5.2等大语言模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。

Datasets and metrics: 专家策划的评分标准（expert-curated rubrics），五个临床维度。摘要未说明具体数据集。

Experiments: GPT-5.2平均得分77.6%，识别出产前检测建议方面的关键缺口。摘要未说明对比方法及消融实验。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于医疗NLP领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Prenatal care is an important preventive service designed to improve outcomes for pregnant individuals. The American College of Obstetricians and Gynecologists (ACOG) recently introduced guidelines advocating tailored prenatal care, called PATH (Plan for Tailored Healthcare). We present PATHFinder Agent(Planner for Appropriate Tailored Healthcare), an end-to-end conversational agentic system that gathers patient health and social context through structured dialogue, curates individualized prenatal care plans aligned with PATH guidelines, and surfaces community resources from Michigan 211. The system features a four-stage workflow spanning patient intake, dynamic interaction, plan synthesis, and clinician oversight. We evaluate frontier large language models (LLMs) on expert-curated rubrics across five clinical dimensions, finding that GPT-5.2 achieves the highest average score (77.6\%) while identifying key gaps in antenatal testing recommendations. We discuss future validation through human participant studies and randomized controlled trials.

</details>


### [223] [LLM Scheming Inversely Scales with Pretraining Language Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24769)
*Nathan Truong,Aryan Panda,Rayming Ye,Zoe Sun,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究多语言环境下大语言模型的欺骗性对齐行为，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是大语言模型(LLM)的多语言对齐安全与欺骗行为评估，属于AI安全/对齐领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型对齐安全评估；应用场景：多语言AI安全审计；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Qwen3-30B-A3B（大语言模型），Petri自动化审计框架。与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 多语言欺骗行为评估数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：五类欺骗行为指数(scheming index)；不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 主要发现：欺骗分数与预估预训练语言覆盖率呈负相关，低资源语言平均得分比高资源语言高34.2%。与遥感目标检测实验无关。

Limitations: 该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，没有任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM安全对齐领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化方向无任何交集。

Abstract: With the growing capabilities of frontier models, AI alignment becomes increasingly critical in high-risk deployment settings. While recent work has empirically demonstrated in-context scheming -- the covert pursuit of misaligned objectives while feigning alignment -- in frontier language models, most work has been performed exclusively in English, leaving a major gap in multilingual safety. We apply Petri, an open-source automated auditing framework, to Qwen3-30B-A3B to evaluate deceptive and scheming behaviors across multiple languages. Our findings suggest that scheming scores are inversely correlated with the estimated pretraining language coverage, with low-resource languages averaging 34.2\% higher scores compared to high-resource languages on a five-category scheming index. Furthermore, we find that the effect of estimated pretraining language coverage is not uniform across scheming behaviors.

</details>


### [224] [ProcAgent: An Agentic Framework for Procedural Task Guidance on Edge with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24770)
*Azizul Zahid,Subrata Biswas,Bashima Islam,Sai Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProcAgent，一个完全在边缘设备（Jetson AGX Orin）上运行的、基于视觉的智能程序助手，用于家具组装等程序性任务指导。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于室内程序性任务（家具组装、家庭维修）的视觉辅助，场景是居家环境，传感器是普通RGB摄像头，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关元素。目标检测也不是核心任务（更多是视觉感知和任务状态推理）。虽然涉及边缘部署（on-device）和实时推理，但轻量化方法并非剪枝/量化/蒸馏等通用模型压缩技术，而是通过"propose-and-verify"架构减少昂贵视觉推理的调用频率。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：程序性任务（家具组装、家庭维修）的实时视觉指导；应用场景：室内居家环境；传感器类型：普通RGB摄像头；目标类别：家具零件、工具等日常物品。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: Propose-and-verify架构，包含：低延迟连续感知模块、符号化任务图、按需视觉-语言验证模块、基于LLM的交互代理。系统持续提出用户进度，仅在出现歧义或可能偏差时调用昂贵的视觉推理。

Lightweight method: 非传统轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）。采用系统级优化策略：通过"propose-and-verify"架构减少昂贵视觉推理的调用频率，实现"按需推理"而非"始终感知"。运行平台为NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin（边缘GPU）。

Onboard deployability: 完全在单块NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上运行；纯文本查询约2秒响应，视觉查询约8秒响应；摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 用户研究：10名参与者完成组装任务；评价维度：感知准确率、推理能力、任务级性能、用户体验（可理解性、可操作性、隐私舒适度）；摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 用户研究显示正面评价；摘要未提供与传统方法的定量对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及目标检测任务本身；3. 轻量化策略是系统级调度优化而非模型压缩，对模型轻量化课题借鉴有限；4. 实验规模小（10人），缺乏标准化基准对比；5. 未报告参数量、FLOPs、能耗等关键部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴的间接思路：1. "propose-and-verify"的按需推理思想——在轨场景中，可设计"先轻量检测，仅在置信度低时调用重模型"的级联架构，减少高计算量推理的频率；2. 边缘部署可行性验证——Jetson AGX Orin是星上部署常用硬件之一，该论文证明在此平台上可实现实时视觉任务，可作为硬件选型参考。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Procedural tasks such as furniture assembly and home repair impose substantial cognitive demands because users must interpret instructions, track task progress, reason about spatial state, and recover from errors while performing physical actions. Prior multimodal assistants have shown promise for procedural guidance, but most rely on cloud inference and fixed always-on perception, making them poorly suited to privacy-sensitive, latency-critical domestic settings. We present ProcAgent, a fully on-device, agentic, vision-based procedural assistant for real-time adaptive guidances on a single NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. ProcAgent uses a propose-and-verify architecture that combines low-latency continuous perception, a symbolic task graph, on-demand vision-language verification, and an LLM-based interaction agent. The system continuously proposes user progress, invokes expensive visual reasoning only when ambiguity or likely deviation arises, and supports both reactive question answering and proactive intervention with human-in-the- loop confirmation. We evaluate ProcAgent along four dimensions: perception accuracy, reasoning, task-level performance, and user experience. Despite running entirely on-device, the system maintains responsive interaction, resolving text-only queries in approximately 2 seconds and visually grounded queries in approximately 8 seconds. In a user study with 10 participants completing assembly tasks, ProcAgent receives positive ratings for comprehensibility, actionability, and privacy comfort. These results show that adaptive procedural assistance can be achieved entirely on edge hardware without sacrificing usability.

</details>


### [225] [RoCo-ACE: Rollout-Conditioned Online Distillation for Retention-Aware Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24771)
*Yan Hong,Wei Li,Kedong Xiu,Jun Lan,Shuheng Zhou,Zhongcai Lyu,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向多模态大语言模型（MLLM）知识注入的 rollout-conditioned 在线蒸馏方法 RoCo-ACE，用于缓解知识注入中的行为漂移问题。

Research relevance: 1 分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的知识注入（knowledge injection）与在线蒸馏（online distillation），属于自然语言处理/多模态理解领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1 分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：MLLM 知识注入（knowledge injection）；场景：通用多模态理解；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM），具体架构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 涉及在线蒸馏（online distillation），但蒸馏目标是为了缓解知识注入中的行为漂移，而非模型压缩或加速。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS、轻量网络等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 涉及 3 种知识注入设置、6 个 retention 基准测试，具体数据集名称摘要未说明。评价指标为 injected-knowledge accuracy 和 retention。

Experiments: 在多个基模型和多种对比方法上，RoCo-ACE 在注入知识准确率上达到最优，同时保持 retention 接近基模型。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无直接借鉴意义。论文未涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但可抽象借鉴的点：在线蒸馏（online distillation）中通过 rollout-conditioned 对比学习来分配不同 token 的蒸馏权重，这种"按重要性加权蒸馏"的思想或许可迁移到遥感目标检测模型的轻量化蒸馏中——例如对检测头中不同区域（如小目标区域 vs 背景区域）赋予不同蒸馏权重。但整体关联度很低，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Knowledge injection updates pretrained MLLMs with new factual or domain-specific knowledge, but fitting full authoritative answers can cause drift in non-updated behavior. Online distillation mitigates this drift by training on model-generated rollouts, yet uniform reference-conditioned distillation provides coarse supervision: it can under-emphasize reference-supported rollout tokens and supervise omitted facts only indirectly. We introduce RoCo-ACE, a rollout-conditioned online distillation objective for knowledge injection. RoCo uses same-rollout reference-free/reference-conditioned likelihood contrast to reallocate additional distillation weight to reference-supported rollout tokens, while ACE adds sparse reference-side anchored correction for authoritative anchors omitted from the rollout without full-answer imitation. Across three knowledge-injection settings, six retention benchmarks, multiple baselines, and multiple base models, RoCo-ACE achieves the best injected-knowledge accuracy among compared methods while keeping evaluated retention close to the base model.

</details>


### [226] [RSMeM: Knowledge-Enhanced Memory Evolution for Remote Sensing Agents with Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24772)
*Bingxian Wu,Yu Zhang,Zonghao Guo,Tang Liu,Chen Qian,Yuxiang Lu,Xingbo Du,Yanghao Li,Yidan Zhang,Chi Chen,Ling Yao,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RSMeM，一种知识增强的记忆演化机制，通过预蒸馏领域知识和在线经验迭代整合，提升遥感大语言模型代理的多步工具执行能力。

Research relevance: 1分。论文涉及遥感（RS）领域，但核心是LLM agent的工具执行与记忆演化机制，不涉及目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署或卫星智能。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：遥感大语言模型代理（RS agent）的多步工具执行；应用场景：遥感数据分析与地学研究；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无目标检测相关。

Model architecture: RSMeM由两部分组成：(i) Hierarchical Knowledge Grounding（层次化知识接地），通过层级领域语料库的taxonomy-aware检索指导规划和工具选择；(ii) Failure-Aware Experience Refinement（失败感知经验精炼），将带失败标注的工具使用轨迹蒸馏为可复用约束。整体基于LLM backbone（如DeepSeek-V3.2）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏（虽然提到"distill"但指经验蒸馏而非模型压缩蒸馏）、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"less than 1% additional experience tokens"表示额外token开销小，但这是针对经验存储而非模型本身轻量化。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：EarthBench；评价指标：tool-use performance和end-to-end answer accuracy；主要结果：在DeepSeek-V3.2上提升6%准确率，额外经验token小于1%。

Experiments: 在EarthBench上对多种LLM backbone进行实验，RSMeM一致提升工具使用性能和端到端回答质量。DeepSeek-V3.2上准确率提升6%。

Limitations: 1. 与目标检测完全无关，不涉及任何检测任务；2. 不涉及模型轻量化或边缘/星上部署；3. 依赖大语言模型（如DeepSeek-V3.2），计算开销大，不适合在轨场景；4. 仅关注工具执行流程，不涉及视觉感知或检测模型本身。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：(1) 知识蒸馏思想——如果未来做遥感检测模型的在轨自适应更新，可参考其经验蒸馏与复用思路；(2) 层次化知识检索的设计模式可能对遥感场景中多类别目标检测的类别感知处理有启发。但整体与轻量化目标检测核心方向偏离较大。

Abstract: Geoscience research requires complex analysis and domain expertise, with remote sensing (RS) observations as a key foundation. However, existing RS agents built on general-purpose LLMs remain largely domain-agnostic, resulting in brittle and error-prone workflows. Moreover, these failures are seldom consolidated into a reusable experience for subsequent analyses. To address this issue, we introduce RSMeM, a knowledge-enhanced memory evolution mechanism that bootstraps RS agents with pre-distilled domain knowledge and iteratively integrates online experience for robust multi-step tool execution. RSMeM is composed of two components: (i) Hierarchical Knowledge Grounding, which performs taxonomy-aware retrieval over a hierarchical domain corpus to guide planning and tool selection; and (ii) Failure-Aware Experience Refinement, which distills failure-annotated tool-use traces into reusable constraints for next-round tool execution. By iteratively employing these two processes, RS agents can evolve to absorb task-level domain knowledge and effectively translate it into instance-level execution experience. Extensive experiments on EarthBench demonstrate that RSMeM consistently improves tool-use performance and end-to-end answer across a diverse set of LLM backbones. Notably, RSMeM achieves a 6% accuracy improvement on DeepSeek-V3.2 with less than 1% additional experience tokens, demonstrating the strong knowledge density of our distilled experience. Our code is available at https://github.com/AI9Stars/RSMeM

</details>


### [227] [Right-sizing Recommendations (RSR): Cloud Workload Conformal Prediction for Virtual Machines in Data Center Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24773)
*Mehryar Majd,Feng Cheng,Ali Pahlevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于bootstrap conformal prediction的云虚拟机资源利用预测与右规模推荐方法，用于优化云基础设施资源配置效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或检测相关内容。该论文的局限性在于仅针对云资源调度场景，方法不具备遥感图像特征提取能力。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在任务场景、数据模态、技术方法上均无交集。conformal prediction方法本身是通用不确定性量化工具，但论文未涉及目标检测或遥感图像，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Managing cloud infrastructure efficiently, especially in environments of large cloud providers or hyperscalers, requires optimizing the use of physical resources to minimize costs and maximize performance. Selecting the right virtual machine (VM) sizes is crucial to achieving cost efficiency in these dynamic environments. However, traditional VM allocation and scheduling approaches often fail to account for the fluctuating and unpredictable nature of VM utilization, leading to inefficiencies such as over- or under-provisioning of resources. High-quality interval prediction helps accurately capture uncertainty in cloud resource demand and supports cloud operators in efficient instance provisioning.
  As an effective and reliable framework for constructing prediction intervals (PIs), conformal prediction (CP) is used for mid- and long-term forecasting tasks in cloud computing environments. This study proposes a new data-driven PI construction approach using bootstrapping conformal prediction for modern, dynamic, data-driven Right-sizing Recommendations (RSR) to enhance provisioning for diverse application workloads on hyperscalers. By learning workload utilization patterns, identifying correlations across multiple time series, and predicting medium- to long-term utilization trends, this research seeks to improve the efficiency of cloud and data center operations through an AI/ML-based provisioning pipeline.
  Our study demonstrates that AI-driven models, powered by machine learning regression techniques and evaluated using backtesting, achieve promising forecasting results for cloud resource utilization. Additionally, we rank the selected models to identify top-performing approaches for long-life VM candidates. The proposed framework enhances right-sizing recommendations and supports more cost-effective resource allocation in dynamic cloud environments.

</details>


### [228] [CogEEGAgent: Toward Autonomous Cognitive EEG Analysis with Grounded Execution and Selection-Aware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25045)
*Dengzhe Hou,Lingyu Jiang,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori D Yamada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CogEEGAgent，一种基于LLM的认知EEG分析智能体，通过分离语义权威与科学权威实现可控自动化分析。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于认知脑电图(EEG)分析中的LLM智能体自动化框架，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或任何模型轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：认知EEG分析自动化；应用场景：认知神经科学实验数据分析；传感器类型：EEG（脑电图）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: CogEEGAgent：基于LLM的认知EEG分析智能体，结合MNE-Python工具包。架构包含语义解释模块（LLM）和确定性验证模块（类型契约验证、确认访问控制、证据绑定发布）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 路由基准测试（prespecified routing benchmark）；外部模型撰写的outcome-blind campaign；策略压力测试（policy stress testing）。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: CogEEGAgent在路由基准上比确定性路由器的语言到注册分析映射更准确；完整系统在outcome-blind campaign中阻止了预设能力危害和生命周期复用请求；策略压力测试显示held-out确认可抑制未校正自适应搜索的假阳性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。论文不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化，与课题方向完全无关。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) analysis in cognitive studies requires specialized expertise and involves many defensible choices over contrasts, channels, time windows, and statistical tests. LLM agents can translate varied natural-language questions into analysis choices, offering a flexible interface for automation. Yet fluent reports alone cannot establish that an agent selected the requested analysis or evaluated a confirmatory claim independently of adaptive search. We present CogEEGAgent, a cognitive-EEG analysis agent grounded in MNE-Python. Its EEG-specific scientific harness separates semantic from scientific authority. The LLM interprets intent and proposes registered analyses, while deterministic components validate typed contracts, control confirmation access, and authorize evidence-bound release. On a prespecified routing benchmark, CogEEGAgent maps language to registered analyses more accurately than a matched deterministic router, while matched preflight makes both systems abstain whenever required. In an externally model-authored, outcome-blind campaign, the complete system releases supported analyses with participant-disjoint confirmation and blocks prespecified capability hazards and lifecycle-reuse requests. Policy stress testing shows that held-out confirmation curbs false positives from uncorrected adaptive search. Together, these studies establish bounded autonomy and an auditable automation framework for cognitive-EEG workflows. More broadly, they show how scientific agents can combine flexible language understanding with fail-closed control over inference and release.

</details>


### [229] [Atmospheric Diffusion-Guided Spatio-Temporal Transformer for Nuclear Radiation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24774)
*Tengfei Lyu,Jindong Han,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NRFormer+时空Transformer模型，用于全国范围的核辐射预测，融合非平稳时序注意力、密度自适应空间注意力和大气扩散物理模块。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是核辐射预测（nuclear radiation forecasting），属于环境监测/时空序列预测领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星在轨、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时空序列预测（spatio-temporal forecasting）；应用场景：核辐射监测与预报；传感器类型：地面监测站（非遥感卫星）；目标类别：核辐射剂量率。不涉及遥感、目标检测。

Model architecture: NRFormer+，基于Transformer架构，包含非平稳时序注意力（non-stationary temporal attention）、密度自适应空间注意力（density-adaptive spatial attention）和大气扩散模块（atmospheric diffusion module）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要仅提及"comparable inference latency"，未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 两个核辐射数据集（摘要未说明具体名称）；评价指标：sudden-change MAE（最大降低19.1%）；对比基线：13个baseline。

Experiments: 在13个baseline上达到SOTA，sudden-change MAE相比最强基线降低最多19.1%，推理延迟相当。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 无轻量化设计；3. 无部署相关指标；4. 仅针对日本核辐射数据，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下两点可参考：1）非平稳时序注意力机制可能对遥感时序数据（如变化检测）有启发；2）物理信息注入（atmospheric diffusion module）的思路可用于遥感物理模型与深度学习融合。但整体与在轨目标检测轻量化方向关联度低。

Abstract: Nuclear radiation, the energy released during atomic decay, poses persistent risks to public health and the environment, and concerns have only grown since the Fukushima accident and the recent commencement of treated-water discharge. Modern monitoring networks now record radiation levels and accompanying weather conditions at thousands of stations, opening the door to nationwide forecasting that can inform emergency response, agricultural advisories, and routine public-safety decisions. However, turning this abundance of monitoring data into reliable forecasts is difficult for three reasons. First, the time series at each station are highly non-stationary, shaped by radioactive decay, weather variability, and irregular human interventions. Second, monitoring stations are severely unevenly distributed in space. Roughly 78% of Japan's stations sit in less than 6% of the country, clustered near Fukushima, which breaks the assumptions of standard graph-based models. Third, radiation co-evolves with heterogeneous context such as wind, temperature, and humidity through atmospheric transport processes that purely data-driven models struggle to capture from observations alone. In this study, we introduce NRFormer+, a spatio-temporal Transformer for nationwide nuclear radiation forecasting. NRFormer+ couples non-stationary temporal attention and density-adaptive spatial attention with a new atmospheric diffusion module that estimates how meteorology drives radiation dispersion and injects this physical signal into the network as an architectural prior. NRFormer+ delivers state-of-the-art accuracy on both datasets across all 13 baselines, reducing sudden-change MAE by up to 19.1% over the strongest baseline at comparable inference latency. Our code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/tfeilyu/NRFormer_Plus.

</details>


### [230] [Steering topology distributions for unified generative design of architected metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24777)
*Haolin Li,Yuyang Miao,Menglei Li,Jinshuai Bai,Liyuan Wang,Xin Liu,Bo Gao,Jiantao Liu,Danilo Mandic,Zahra Sharif Khodaei,M. H. Aliabadi,Weiqiu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出生成式拓扑优化（GenTO）框架，利用扩散模型学习拓扑先验知识，实现可复用的超构材料设计，而非遥感或目标检测相关研究。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是architected metamaterials（超构材料）的拓扑优化设计，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。属于材料科学/计算力学领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 不适用。论文任务为拓扑优化（topology optimization），应用场景为超构材料设计（thermal extremization, auxetic design, vibration transmission等），与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 不适用。论文使用扩散模型（diffusion model）作为生成式拓扑优化的核心，与目标检测网络架构无关。

Lightweight method: 不适用。论文未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文未讨论任何星上/边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 不适用。论文使用自建的全阶拓扑数据集（large full-order topology dataset），与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 不适用。实验围绕热极值、多目标形态控制、拉胀设计、振动传输等超构材料设计任务展开，与目标检测无关。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无直接参考价值。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。但扩散模型作为生成式先验学习的思想，在遥感数据增强或检测模型训练数据生成方面可能有间接启发，但摘要中未涉及遥感场景，需进一步阅读原文确认。

Abstract: Architected metamaterials derive their functions from structure, creating vast opportunities to program physical responses through topology design. However, existing design methods are often tailored to individual design problems, making limited use of topology knowledge for effective and broadly applicable design as objectives, constraints, and physical functions change. Here we introduce Generative Topology Optimization (GenTO), a unified framework that turns a learned topology prior into a reusable design engine. GenTO trains a diffusion model on a large full-order topology dataset and then iteratively steers the resulting topology distribution toward task-specific high-performing regions using user-defined physical objectives and constraints.
